# Steps M09-M12: Wenna wakes, collections and hearts, travel and Duskwick, Act II opens

Issue-ready specs for S-064 to S-094 (plus the splits listed in [Index changes](#index-changes)). Each step below is
written so the plan job can paste it into one `agent:improvement` issue: the title is the step heading after the
id, the six sections map to the issue's `## Why`, `## What`, `## Acceptance criteria`, `## Verification plan`,
`## Out of scope` and `## Risk & rollback` (the `#### Assets` block goes inside `## What`).

Order of authority: `docs/roadmap/CANON-NOW.md` LOCKED > `docs/roadmap/CANON.md` > the reconciliation
rows in `docs/roadmap/steps/*.md` (for the topics they name, until the named close-out edits the losing file) > the
bible (`docs/roadmap/bible/*.md`) > the INDEX row > the issue. Where two bible files disagree, this file picks one and says so in
[Bible reconciliations used by these steps](#reconciliations); an implementer follows the step, and the close-out step
of the milestone (S-070, S-079, S-087, S-094) copies the reconciliation into the bible file that lost.

Links are relative to `docs/roadmap/steps/`: bible files are `../bible/<file>.md#<anchor>`.

<a id="conventions"></a>

**Conventions every step here uses** (do not repeat them in the issue; link this section):

- **Find earlier-step code by grep, not by guess.** Steps before M09 were specified in parallel. Where a step names a
  module from an earlier step (`js/realms/index.js`, `js/realms/manager.js`, `js/realms/recipes/sandbox.js`,
  `js/realms/dungeon-compile.js`, `js/realms/recipes/dungeon.js`, `js/dungeon-mod.js`, `js/cond.js`,
  `js/content/flags.js`, `js/dialogue.js`, `js/scenes.js`, `js/quests.js`, `js/items.js`, `js/abilities.js`,
  `js/progress-graph.js`, `js/hud/journal.js`, `js/world-post.js`, `js/props-interior-home.js`, `js/ai/index.js`,
  `js/boss/defs.js`, `tests/chapter.mjs`, `js/debug/tests/realm.js`), the implementer first greps for it (for example
  `grep -rn "export function generateRealm" js/`) and uses the real path and export names. If a helper the step needs
  does not exist, the step builds the smallest version the spec needs and says so in the PR body.
- **Shipped code this file names was checked on `main` at `b79d73d`:** `js/sky.js` (`SPRITE_KEYS` line 201, `KIND`
  line 202, `buildSkyAtlas` 236, `class SkySprites` 334, the random horizon loop at 348-354, the sky isle at 347; the
  fragment shader already has an additive path through `vAdd`), `TA.isles = 6` in `js/tune.js` 536 (asserted by
  `js/debug/tests/wp8.js` 128, "no stand-in isles" at 568), `js/data.js` (`CFG.maxHp` 20 line 16, `CFG.potionMax` 3
  line 17, `SHOP` 73, `PLUSHIES` 82, `SECRETS` 146, `NPCS` 261, `DOOR_LINES` 393, `SIGNS` 403), `js/save.js`
  (`SAVE_VERSION`, `MIGRATIONS`, `migrate`, `normalize` with the `maxHp` clamp at line 66, `loadSave`), `js/ui.js`
  (`topFit` 55 with `cols = 10 | 5`, `fitTop` 232, the hearts build at 442-460, the `ending` overlay at 136,
  `openEnding` 851 with the `.ex` text at 855), `styles.css` (`#hearts` 92, `lay-land` hearts 667, `lay-port` hearts
  751), `js/completion.js` (`COMPLETION_ROWS`, `completion(W, S)`), `js/hud/tracker.js` (the `%` chip at 9-17),
  `js/hud/nav.js` (`objective` 18, `MapCard` 318), `js/terrain.js` (`buildMapCanvas` 527), `js/world.js` (post-pass
  guards `o.<pass> !== false` at 130-132, `addInteract` 579), `js/interact.js` (the `well` case 138, the shop heart
  314, the heart chest 337), `js/models.js` (`npcDef` 240 with cases `ribbit`, `clover`, `shellby`, `miso`, `hoot`,
  `digby`), `assets/manifest.js` (sections `sprites` 15, `textures` 70, `billboards` 98, `models` 121, `ui` 131; the
  horizon cards are 1024 × 307 webp), `tests/inpage.mjs`, `tests/smoke.mjs` (the "★ 100%" tracker check at 182),
  `tests/playthrough.mjs` (the ending card checks at 598-628), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (jobs `unit`, `smoke`,
  `pwa`, `opening`, `playthrough`, `inpage` matrix).
- **Text.** Every new string is a text id in `js/text/en/<realm>.js` (`home.*`, `cc.*` for Duskwick, `lore.*`,
  `side.ssNN.*`), at most 220 characters, `{tokens}` from `js/hud/keys.js` only, `~` only in Miso's lines, `!!` only
  in Kinako's lines, Pip never speaks. Dialogue entries live in `js/content/dialogue/<realm>.js`, quests in
  `js/content/quests/<realm>.js`, scenes in `js/content/scenes/<realm>.js`, NPC looks in `js/content/looks.js`.
  Every new speech marker is added to [../bible/charter.md#reserved-markers](../bible/charter.md#reserved-markers) and
  the content lint in the PR that first ships the speaker.
- **Scenes** are written in the seven-step format of [../bible/story.md#scene-format](../bible/story.md#scene-format)
  (`shot`, `say`, `wait`, `flag`, `emit`, `fade`, `pose`; everything else is an `emit`). Every scene is skippable
  (hold {interact} 0.8 s; the remaining `flag` steps and `after` apply) and replayable from the Lore tab unless the step
  says otherwise.
- **Save.** M09 changes no schema (save stays **v8** from S-024). **S-071 bumps to v9** (the `collect` maps). Nothing
  else in M09-M12 bumps. New state goes in `S.flags`, `S.items`, `S.quests`, `S.collect`, `S.chests`, `S.secrets`,
  `S.bosses`, `S.plushies`, `S.travel`, `S.bestiary`, `S.dungeons` with the id rules of
  [../bible/systems.md#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids). Every new flag key or family is registered in
  `js/content/flags.js` in the same PR (the content lint fails otherwise).
- **Invariants.** Every step's acceptance criteria include "Invariants: paste the block from
  [../bible/charter.md#invariants-block](../bible/charter.md#invariants-block) and keep the lines named". The
  lines are named by their first words: *Frozen home island*, *Frozen realms*, *Hidden until whole*, *One save slot*,
  *Budgets per view*, *Precache*, *Assets*, *Tone*, *Original IP only*, *Machine*.
- **Home post-passes** follow [../bible/atlas-home.md#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes) rules 1-10: own stream
  `new RNG(7151 * k + c)` from the table there, guarded by `o.<passName> !== false` after `placeSecrets` in
  `World.generate()`, append-only lists, solids and decks through `attachPost(W)` (S-004), portal records collected
  into `W.portals` (S-026), and four tests in `tests/world.test.js` (SLOW group, seeds 1-40): (a) the world with and
  without the pass has equal `placementPrint`, `fingerprint`, `walk`, `reach` and `unreachable`; (b) the pass's list
  has the documented ids in order; (c) every item obeys its `ok()`; (d) every item meant to be walked to is reachable
  in the post-solid walk grid.
- **Isle post-passes** use the streams of [../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes)
  (`new RNG(seed * 71 + c)` for the finds step) and the anchor rule of
  [../bible/atlas-isles.md#conventions](../bible/atlas-isles.md#conventions): nearest valid 1 m cell to `(x, z)`
  within `R` (default 12), chosen among the five nearest valid cells by the pass's RNG, and a unit test that every
  find lies within `R` of its anchor.
- **Machine.** Before any browser run: `free -h` (want ≥ 6 GB available); one browser at a time; stop every server
  and browser started. In-page runs are `node tests/inpage.mjs <suite>`, chapter runs `node tests/chapter.mjs <id>`.
  A new in-page suite is a file `js/debug/tests/<suite>.js` registered in `js/debug/tests.js` beside `wp1`-`wp9`, plus
  one entry in the `inpage` matrix of `.github/workflows/ci.yml`. This file adds the suites `wenna` (S-064),
  `collect` (S-072), `travel` (S-080) and `duskwick` (S-083), and the chapter jobs `ch7` (S-067), `ch1`-`ch6`
  (S-069) and `ch8` (S-091) to the chapter matrix S-030 created.
- **Art.** Painted images are generated inside the step by a GPT Sol subagent (T3 orchestrator `delegate_task`,
  Codex `image_gen.imagegen`) or a Gemini model, with the fixed preamble of
  [../bible/art-audio.md#preamble](../bible/art-audio.md#preamble) + the kind suffix of
  [../bible/art-audio.md#kinds](../bible/art-audio.md#kinds) + the subject given in the step (subjects end with
  "Palette: <hexes>."). Processing follows [../bible/art-audio.md#pipeline](../bible/art-audio.md#pipeline)
  (white-key one-liner, `python3 -I tools/slice_sheet.py`, `tools/export_sprites.py`, `tools/make_seamless.py`,
  `tools/process_strip.py`, Pillow webp). Character portraits use the transparent recipe of
  [../bible/cast.md](../bible/cast.md) "Portraits" (magenta key if the tool returns an opaque image, 512² webp,
  ≤ 60 KB). Raw generations are never committed; the prompt and the processing commands are appended to
  `docs/art/prompts.md`; turnaround reference sheets go to `docs/art/ref/<key>.webp` (never under `assets/`). Every
  key gets a `realm` tag when it belongs to one realm and a fallback in `js/realms/fallbacks.js`. 3D is code-built
  (`js/models.js`, `js/props.js`, `js/toon.js`, rig families from [../bible/cast.md](../bible/cast.md)) with the
  ART BRIEF pasted in the PR body; GLB slots are added to `assets/manifest.js` **commented out** with their height.
  Pip's GLB is never touched. If the image tool is unavailable in a session, the step ships with the fallback and
  says so in the PR; the art is then regenerated by the milestone's close-out step.

<a id="m09"></a>

## M09 · The Interlude II: Wenna wakes (Act I's turn ships)

**Goal.** Turn the bloom into the close of Act One. At the first dusk after the bloom the Great Tree speaks as Wenna,
the far isles' night windows go out one by one, Nell sails home on the lighthouse beam, and the root door in the Tree
opens onto Pip's Nook, Under the Roots (built in M08), Sir Rollo and the Glowbud Lantern. Ribbit "jetties" the jetty.
The ending card becomes "End of Act One · The Sneeze" in the same release.

**Entry criteria.**
- M08 closed: Pip's Nook and `placeRootDoor` (S-056), the Lantern (S-057), Dimlings (S-058), Under the Roots rooms
  1-9 hidden behind `?realm=under_roots` (S-059, S-060), Sir Rollo with handover data (S-061), its art (S-062).
- The scene runner (S-034), dialogue tables (S-032), quests (S-036), Field Notes with Lore and Ask the Tree (S-037,
  S-038), the chapter harness (S-030) and conditions (S-025) are merged or stacked.
- Owner decision 1 (load-bearing names, including the Duskwick rename) and owner decision 7 (the Act I card rewrite)
  are read from the vision issue's Owner decisions (or, if the owner left it open, the council record that settled it; INDEX §7). If decision 1 is still open when S-064 is reached, the plan job convenes the council
  (plan.md "The council") instead of asking the owner; if the council is unavailable, the CANON §15 default applies.

**Exit criteria.**
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch7` and `ch1`-`ch6` pass in CI; `test:playthrough` (ch0), `test:opening` and `test:smoke`
  pass with the `act1` card.
- `REALMS.under_roots.hidden === false`; the home world prints are byte-identical; the in-page `wenna` suite passes.
- Save is still v8; a v6 bloomed fixture Continues into the I.6 scene at dusk.

**What the player can do at the end.** After the bloom, at dusk in the village, I.6 plays (about 75 s, skippable,
replayable from the Lore tab). Wenna talks at the pedestal in a soft-gold frame (12 lines keyed to the story stage)
and voices Ask the Tree. Pip walks round the Tree to the root door, enters his Nook, goes down into Under the Roots,
finds the Glowbud Lantern, calms Sir Rollo, hears Wenna at the bottom, rides the root lift home, shows Nell the
Lantern and watches Ribbit open Nell's Jetty. `[E] Sail · the Dandelion` opens a chart on which every isle is a fog
bank ("Nell won't risk the fog yet."). Chapters 1-7 each have a chapter test; Act I is tested in three seed orders.

**Playtime added.** +3.0 h: the Interlude main path 2.0 h (ch7), I.6 0.1 h, Wenna and villager lines 0.2 h, the
Nook, the root door, Rollo's later lines and the first looks at the jetty and the chart 0.7 h. Cumulative 7.0 h
(INDEX).

**Save-version plan.** None. Everything is flags (`bloomDusk`, `wennaAwake`, `nellHome`, `lanternGot`, `rolloCalm`,
`jettyOpen`, `ch7`, `seen:*`, `scene:*`, `met:nell`, `wennaLine`), quests (`mq-act1`, `mq-ch7`) and items
(`lantern`). Save stays v8.

<a id="m10"></a>

## M10 · Collections, hearts and the Completion page

**Goal.** Give every collectible kind the whole game uses its save map, pickup, cue, toast and home before the first
new land ships: heart petals and up to 14 hearts in two rows, Hollows (one-room puzzle caves) with the two home
fairy-ring Hollows, Lamp Logs and the Bottle Chain with the lighthouse shelf, Vistas and Hoot's Board, Puddle's
Grotto (ss-07), Completion v2 grouped by land, and the economy ledger as a test.

**Entry criteria.** M09 closed (S-070 merged or stacked); save v8; `ch7` chapter test green.

**Exit criteria.**
- Save v9 with migration tests from v2-v8; `bun test` green including `tests/economy.test.js` and
  `tests/stats.test.js`.
- `hollow_home_1`, `hollow_home_2`, `puddles_grotto` and `lighthouse` compile, solve and pass the realm suite.
- The in-page `collect` suite passes; `G.debug.hud.audit()` is clean with 14 hearts at 1080p and 390 × 844.

**What the player can do at the end.** On home, with the Lantern, Pip opens two spiral panels in fairy-ring stumps and
solves two Hollows for two heart petals (half a heart's progress shows in a petal ring); sketches three vistas
(the fourth post waits for the Ivy Gloves) and sees them pinned on Hoot's Board; climbs into Pinch's Lighthouse and
sees the shipped bottle as Bottle 12 on the lamp-room shelf; reads every bottle and log found in the Lore tab; visits
Puddle in his grotto under the well and finishes ss-07; reads a Completion page grouped by land with game-wide rows.

**Playtime added.** +1.0 h (two Hollows 0.3 h, ss-07 0.35 h, vistas 0.15 h, the lighthouse and Lore reading 0.2 h).
Cumulative 8.0 h.

**Save-version plan.** **v8 → v9 in S-071** (`collect` maps; the shipped bottle becomes `collect.bottle['home:12']`;
and the `maxHp` clamp widens 20 → 32 inside the same bump, so an older build refuses a 32-HP save instead of clamping
it: the clamp-widening rule of INDEX §3). S-072 needs no bump. No other schema change.

<a id="m11"></a>

## M11 · Travel and the Duskwick land

**Goal.** ENGINE F24 travel (benches, the travel menu, the map card's This land / World tabs, fast travel), chart
sailing on the *Dandelion* with a painted sailing card, the shared Lamplighter kit with home's five benches, and the
whole Duskwick overworld (the `lagoon` recipe, Wickhaven on stilts, the Lamplighters' Tower outside, its art,
music and every find), hidden until M12. Perf checkpoint 2 closes it.

**Entry criteria.** M10 closed; owner decision 1 answered or at its default.

**Exit criteria.**
- `generateRealm('duskwick')` ≤ 1.2 s, prints frozen in `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/duskwick.json`, solver and
  the 40 × 40 m coverage test pass (fill ≤ 12 %), the realm suite passes at its 4 views in both cameras.
- The Duskwick bundle is ≤ 2.5 MB and core ≤ 30 MB (`node tools/build-pages.mjs`).
- `REALMS.duskwick.hidden === true`; the chart still shows every isle as a fog bank.
- Perf checkpoint 2's numbers are in `docs/roadmap/progress.md`.

**What the player can do at the end.** Sit on home's five Lamplighter benches (full hearts, autosave), fast-travel
between found benches from the map card, and open the World tab of the map. With `?realm=duskwick` (debug)
Duskwick generates fully dressed and painted; it stays `hidden: true`.

**Playtime added.** 0 h visible (benches are quality of life). Duskwick's 2 h ships in M12.

**Save-version plan.** None (v9). Benches use `S.travel` (v8); finds use `S.chests`, `S.secrets`, `S.collect`.

<a id="m12"></a>

## M12 · Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens

**Goal.** Put people, enemies and the first sight of the Lamp Thief into Duskwick; chapter 8 unhides the isle, gives
the Lamplighter's Chart and Lamp Log 1; Kinako's Floating Bazaar docks; Duskwick's two Hollows open.

**Entry criteria.** M11 closed (perf checkpoint 2 passed).

**Exit criteria.**
- `REALMS.duskwick.hidden === false`; `node tests/chapter.mjs ch8` passes in CI and the chain check `ch7-end` =
  `ch8-start` passes.
- `hollow_duskwick_1/2` compile and solve; the realm suite passes with people and enemies present.
- `tests/economy.test.js` passes with Kinako's shop in the ledger.

**What the player can do at the end.** Pip sails to Duskwick (the compass reads "Sail to Duskwick"), meets Flick and
the drowsy Wicklings, hears Granny Wick explain the beacons, gets the Chart and Lamp Log 1, knocks on the locked
Tower door, climbs the outer stair and sees a cloaked light flee straight up, reports back, and meets Kinako's
Floating Bazaar (a 300-gem Heart Container, the 400-gem Bright Wick). Sizzle Beetles and Coco Clonks roam outside
Wickhaven; Dimlings haunt the two dark caves; two Hollows (a petal, a gem bag) wait. Pinewhistle, Archwater and
Threemills show as fog banks on the chart until their lands ship.

**Playtime added.** +2.0 h (ch8 main path 1.0 h, Duskwick finds reachable now with the Lantern 0.6 h, Hollows
0.2 h, the Bazaar and people 0.2 h). Cumulative 10.0 h.

**Save-version plan.** None (v9). Kinako's Heart Container is `S.items.kinako_heart`, the Bright Wick
`S.items.bright_wick`; the Chart is `S.items.chart`; Lamp Log 1 is `S.items['log:1']` plus
`collect.log['duskwick:1']`.

<a id="s-064"></a>

### S-064 · feat: horizon isle cards point at fixed bearings, with night windows and fog banks

Milestone M09 · Depends on: S-025 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-home.md#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky), [../bible/art-audio.md#horizon](../bible/art-audio.md#horizon),
[../bible/story.md#I.6](../bible/story.md#I.6)

#### Why

The four painted isles on the home horizon are today placed at random bearings by a seeded loop (`js/sky.js` 348-354)
and two of them are drawn twice (six sprites from four cards). From M09 on they are real neighbours: the chart, the
I.6 scene, every beacon beat and the crown lanterns all point at them. This step pins all six cards to the bearings
of [../bible/atlas-home.md#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky), adds Kettle Isle (e) and Saltglass Flats (f) as fogged
cards, and adds the night windows the I.6 scene puts out one by one. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-064
(M09 The Interlude II: Wenna wakes); vision item **The Interlude II: Wenna wakes**.

#### What

1. **Pure data module `js/sky-home.js`** (no Three.js; `bun test` imports it). Exports `SKY_HOME` and three functions.

   | Card key | Land | Bearing θ | `az` = π − θ·π/180 | Width (m) | Night windows `[u, v, size]` (u from the left, v from the top of the painting, size × card width) | Fogged until | Window lit when |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `horizon_isle_a` (shipped) | Pinewhistle | 330° | −2.618 (≡ 3.665) | 190 | `[0.52, 0.30, 0.03]`, `[0.55, 0.42, 0.03]`, `[0.50, 0.55, 0.03]` | - | `!flag:wennaAwake` or `flag:beacon:pinewhistle` |
   | `horizon_isle_b` (shipped) | Archwater | 112.5° | 1.178 | 165 | `[0.53, 0.36, 0.05]` (R33) | - | `!flag:wennaAwake` or `flag:beacon:archwater` |
   | `horizon_isle_c` (shipped) | Duskwick | 157.5° | 0.393 | 140 | `[0.69, 0.32, 0.04]` (the tower), `[0.30, 0.70, 0.02]`, `[0.42, 0.72, 0.02]` (Wickhaven) (R33) | - | `!flag:wennaAwake` or `flag:beacon:duskwick` |
   | `horizon_isle_d` (shipped) | Threemills | 247.5° | −1.178 | 190 | `[0.37, 0.37, 0.03]`, `[0.50, 0.28, 0.03]`, `[0.63, 0.39, 0.03]` (the three mills) (R33) | - | `!flag:wennaAwake` or `flag:beacon:threemills` |
   | `horizon_isle_e` (new) | Kettle Isle | 67.5° | 1.963 | 165 | `[0.38, 0.78, 0.06]` (forge), `[0.50, 0.08, 0.04]` (spout) | `flag:act2done` | `flag:stone:kettle` |
   | `horizon_isle_f` (new) | Saltglass Flats | 202.5° | −0.393 | 210 | `[0.62, 0.40, 0.03]` (the Academy bell tower) | `flag:act2done` | `flag:stone:saltglass` |

   All six: `R: 760, y: 0, flip: 1, alpha: 1`. Haze 0.5 (shipped value) when clear, **0.95** while fogged. The sky
   isle is unchanged (`az 2.3, R 900, y 135, w 92`).
   - `cardState(S, card)` → `{ haze, fogged }` (conditions through `test()` from `js/cond.js`).
   - `windowLit(S, card)` → boolean (the "Window lit when" column).
   - `windowAlpha(S, card, night, winT)` → `night * (winT[card.key] ?? (windowLit(S, card) ? 1 : 0))`, where `night`
     is the sky's night factor (the existing `sky.night` value, 0 by day, 1 at night) and `winT` is a runtime-only
     override map a scene writes (never saved). Windows show from dusk to dawn: use `smoothstep(0.35, 0.65, night)`.
2. **`js/sky.js`.**
   - `SPRITE_KEYS` gains `'horizon_isle_e'`, `'horizon_isle_f'`, `'horizon_window'`. `buildSkyAtlas` sizes the two new
     cards like the shipped ones (`maxW` 448) and `horizon_window` at 64 px. The "no stand-in for painted isles" rule
     (line 244) stays for every `horizon_isle_*` key; `horizon_window` gets a canvas stand-in when its file is missing:
     a 64 × 64 radial gradient `#ffd98a` alpha 1 at the centre to 0 at r 32.
   - `KIND` gains `window: 6`. Replace the random isle loop (348-354) by one `add()` per `SKY_HOME.cards` row (in table
     order, so a-f are list entries 3-8 as today's six were), then one `add(…, KIND.window, …)` per window point: the
     window sits on its card's plane at `az = card.az − (u − 0.5) · card.w / R` (radians; `flip` 1), `y = (1 − v) ·
     card.w · aspect`, `R = 758` (2 m in front of the card), width `size · card.w`, alpha 0.
   - Vertex shader: `vAdd = kind > 5.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0;` and the tint for kind 6 is a new uniform `uWinCol` (vec4,
     `#ffd98a`, a = 1); windows skip the haze mix (`vHazeK = 0.0` for kind 6) and the below-horizon clip. The fragment
     shader already writes `a * (1.0 - vAdd)`, so windows blend additively in the same draw.
   - New method `SkySprites.setHome(S, night)`: computes every card's haze and every window's alpha from
     `js/sky-home.js` and writes `a1.w` (haze) and `a3.x` (alpha) **only when a value changed by more than 0.004**
     (one `needsUpdate` on the two attributes, never per frame otherwise). Called from the sky's per-frame update with
     `G.game.S` when the realm is `home` (other realms keep today's look until their own sky table ships).
   - Runtime override API for scenes: `sky.winT` (an object, empty by default) and `sky.setWin(cardKey, t)`; S-066's
     `sky.windowsOut` / `sky.windowsOn` events animate it.
   - **Still one draw call** for every sky sprite (sun, moon, clouds, isles, windows).
3. **`js/debug/tests/wp8.js` line 128**: the horizon count check becomes
   `t.eq(S.count(4), SKY_HOME.cards.filter((c) => A.cells[c.key]).length, 'horizon isles')` (cards whose art
   loaded). `TA.isles` stays 6 and is no longer read by `js/sky.js`; leave it in `js/tune.js` with a comment
   "superseded by js/sky-home.js (S-064)".
4. **Debug:** `G.debug.sky.cards()` → `[{ key, az, w, haze, windows: [alpha, ...] }]`; `G.debug.sky.win(key, t)`
   sets `sky.winT[key]`.
5. **Flags read** (register in `js/content/flags.js` if missing, kind `story`, `setBy` the step named):
   `beacon:pinewhistle` (S-106), `beacon:archwater` (S-127), `beacon:threemills` (S-148), `beacon:duskwick`
   (S-165), `stone:kettle` (S-232), `stone:saltglass` (S-254), `act2done` (S-165), `wennaAwake` (S-066).
6. **New in-page suite `wenna`** (`js/debug/tests/wenna.js`, registered in `js/debug/tests.js`, added to the CI
   `inpage` matrix). This step's tests: card bearings; windows at night before and after `wennaAwake`; fog haze on
   e and f; one sky draw call.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · ship size | Prompt subject (after preamble + `horizon` / `card` suffix) | Processing | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `horizon_isle_e` (`billboards`, core) | `horizon` · generate 2048 × 614, ship 1024 × 307 webp q82, alpha, ≤ 60 KB | "A far-away volcanic island shaped like a round teakettle, a single soft plume of white steam from its spout-like peak, a few dark trees on its terraced flank, a tiny warm-lit forge village at its foot, black sand at its foot. Palette: #3b3540 #e66f4f #f4d1a6 #8fc7a0." | white-key one-liner → `slice_sheet.py --alpha 40 --down 4 --grow 3 --min 2000` → Pillow `resize((1024, 307), LANCZOS)` → webp | `F-none` (not drawn) |
| `horizon_isle_f` (`billboards`, core) | as above | "A far-away long flat pale desert island with low wind-shaped dunes, a single half-buried old bell tower standing up in the middle, a few glints of glass in the sand. Palette: #efe3c8 #c9a46b #8ec9d8 #b55d4c." | as above | `F-none` |
| `horizon_window` (`billboards`, core) | `card` · generate 1024², ship 64 × 64 webp alpha ≤ 6 KB | "A soft round warm glow with a faint four-point twinkle, a bright creamy core fading to nothing. Palette: #ffd98a #f2b45a." | white-key → `slice_sheet.py` → `export_sprites.py … part_00=horizon_window:60` → pad to 64² | canvas radial gradient (above) |

ART BRIEFs for e and f (paste into the PR; they are image-only, no 3D): copy the two `horizon_isle_e` /
`horizon_isle_f` briefs of [../bible/atlas-home.md#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky) (silhouette, palette, "isle sits on
the bottom edge, hazy blue distance") with the ship size above. Core precache grows by about 125 KB.

#### Acceptance criteria

- With every asset loaded, the sky holds exactly six horizon cards at the six `az` values above (each within 0.001
  rad), widths as listed, the sky isle unchanged, and every window quad at its card's plane.
- No card centre lies within 0.32 rad of `az = π` (the plaza-to-Tree line the shipped code protects).
- At night (`G.debug.tod(23)`) with a save where `wennaAwake` is unset, every window of a, b, c and d has alpha ≥ 0.95;
  with `wennaAwake` set and no `beacon:*` flags, every window alpha is 0. By day (`tod(12)`) every window alpha is 0.
- Cards e and f draw with haze 0.95 until `act2done`, then 0.5; their windows stay dark until their stone flag.
- `renderer.info.render.calls` for the sky pass is the same as on `main` (one `skySprites` draw); the realm and wp8
  suites still pass.
- `?noassets=all` renders: no horizon card is drawn (shipped rule), and nothing throws.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island* (no generator change), *Budgets per view*, *Precache*,
  *Assets*, *Original IP only*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/sky-home.test.js` (new): bearings → `az` round trip; `cardState` / `windowLit` truth tables for a
  fresh save, `wennaAwake`, each `beacon:*`, `act2done`, each `stone:*`; `windowAlpha` is 0 at `night = 0` and follows
  `winT` when set.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs wenna` (new suite, this step's four tests).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs wp8:1/3`, `wp8:2/3`, `wp8:3/3` (the sky suite with the new count check).
- `bun run test:smoke` and the `?noassets=all` smoke variant; `node tools/build-pages.mjs` then `bun run test:pwa`.
- Add `wenna` to `.github/workflows/ci.yml`'s `inpage` matrix.

#### Out of scope

The I.6 scene that drives `winT` (S-066); Duskwick's own sky table (S-091); the Ladder billboard and Mote's star
(S-165, S-286); the night-window glow of a lit beacon on other isles' skies (M13 template).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert restores the random placement. Risk: the window quads sitting slightly off the painted windows; the
`u, v` values are data in `js/sky-home.js` and can be nudged without code changes.

<a id="s-065"></a>

### S-065 · feat: Wenna speaks at the pedestal in a soft-gold frame with count-keyed lines

Milestone M09 · Depends on: S-032b, S-038 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none (dormant until `wennaAwake`) · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#wenna-lines](../bible/story.md#wenna-lines), [../bible/cast.md#wenna](../bible/cast.md#wenna),
[../bible/story.md#art](../bible/story.md#art), [../bible/systems.md#hints](../bible/systems.md#hints),
[../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)

#### Why

From I.6 on, the Great Tree is a character the player can visit: the pedestal becomes the place to hear how she feels
about what Pip has done, and Ask the Tree speaks in her voice instead of Shellby's. This step builds her frame, her
pick rule and her first twelve lines (the Interlude and the start of Act II) so S-066 only has to wake her. Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-065 (M09); vision item **The Interlude II: Wenna wakes**.

#### What

1. **Pedestal Talk.** In `js/interact.js` `usePedestal()`: when `S.flags.wennaAwake` is set and Pip carries no
   Starseed, the pedestal offers `[E] Talk · the Great Tree` (R34) and opens Wenna's dialogue. Every other pedestal
   behaviour (placing seeds, the shipped Look lines before `wennaAwake`) is unchanged.
2. **Pure pick rule `js/content/wenna.js`.**

   ```js
   export const WENNA_STAGES = [   // ordered; the current stage is the LAST one whose `when` holds
     { id: 'S1', when: 'flag:wennaAwake', lines: ['w1', 'w2', 'w3', 'w4'] },
     { id: 'S2', when: 'flag:lanternGot', lines: ['w5', 'w6', 'w7', 'w8'] },
     { id: 'S3', when: 'flag:ch8',        lines: ['w9', 'w10', 'w11', 'w12'] },
   ];                               // S-171, S-260, S-291 append S4-S14 and the side lines w58-w60
   export function wennaPick(S) { /* → { line: 'w3', stage: 'S1' } */ }
   ```
   Rule ([../bible/story.md#wenna-lines](../bible/story.md#wenna-lines)): in the current stage, the lowest-numbered line
   without `seen:wenna.w<n>`; when all are seen, `lines[S.flags.wennaLine % lines.length]`. Every Talk applies
   `inc:wennaLine` and `set:seen:wenna.w<n>`. Lines of earlier stages are never shown again; the Lore tab keeps them.
   **Night line:** the first Talk in a game night (hour ≥ 21 or < 6) shows `home.wenna.night` "Oh. Is it morning?
   ...No. Hello anyway." before the stage line (once per in-game night: runtime only, keyed by `floor(S.playTime / 480)`).
3. **Text** (`js/text/en/home.js`, ids `home.wenna.w1` … `home.wenna.w12`, verbatim from story.md):

   | Id | Line |
   |---|---|
   | w1 | Hello again, little Pip. I'm still awake. I keep checking. |
   | w2 | The door's on my north side. I hope it isn't too creaky. I've never had a door before. |
   | w3 | I can feel every footstep on the isle. Yours are the quick little ones. |
   | w4 | Your room is in my roots, you know. You moved in when you were this high. You used to kick in your sleep. |
   | w5 | How does the Lantern feel? Warm? Good. It's supposed to feel like a hand on your shoulder. |
   | w6 | Nell's boat creaks when she's happy. Listen next time you sail. Boats talk too, a bit. |
   | w7 | There's an isle past the lighthouse with a stone tower. Someone old lives there. Someone who remembers me. |
   | w8 | Don't be frightened of the dark beacons. They're not broken. They're only empty. |
   | w9 | Granny Wick! She was a little girl the last time I was properly awake. She used to wave at me from the tower. |
   | w10 | The Lamplighters drew that chart. They drew me on it too, see? The tree in the middle. Not bad, for moths. |
   | w11 | Something went up past the tower, you said? Up. Hm. Up is a long way. |
   | w12 | Three isles, three lights. Go in any order you like. I'd start with the one that sounds nicest. |

4. **The `wenna` frame** (`styles.css` + `ui.say(lines, { frame: 'wenna', name })`; R19): the shipped dialogue panel
   with a 3 px border `#e8c66a`, inner glow `box-shadow: inset 0 0 18px rgba(255, 233, 168, 0.45)`, background
   `rgba(255, 248, 230, 0.92)` (dark theme `rgba(58, 48, 32, 0.92)`), name plate `#b8894a` text on `#fff1c9`, body
   text `#4a3a22`, a slow 4 s border shimmer (off when Settings → Motion comfort is on). Portrait slot: a gold leaf
   glyph (no portrait). The name plate reads **"The Great Tree"** until `flag:wennaAwake` is set or a scene `label`
   event names her (S-066), then **"Wenna"**. Optional painted corner `ui_frame_wenna_corner` (below). A soft-gold
   screen vignette shows while a Wenna box is open: CSS `radial-gradient(ellipse at centre, transparent 55%,
   rgba(255, 233, 168, 0.35))`, or `ui_wenna_glow` when loaded.
5. **Ask the Tree in her voice** (`js/hud/journal.js`, S-038's hint button): from `wennaAwake` on, the nudge and the
   answer show in the `wenna` frame with the opening line `home.hint.wenna.open` "My roots reach a long way, little
   sprout." in place of Shellby's "Hmm, let me think, dear..."; before it, Shellby voices it as today. The leaf card
   animation is unchanged.
6. **Music.** Register the `wenna` track in `TRACKS` of `js/audio.js`, the block from
   [../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data) verbatim (F major, 3/4, 66 bpm, bell lead).
   While a Wenna box is open, the music crossfades to `wenna` at −6 dB over 1.0 s and back to the region track when
   the dialogue closes (use the same call the region music switch uses: `grep -n "music\|track" js/ambient.js
   js/audio-bus.js`).
7. **Sound cue and its visual twin** ([../bible/cast.md#wenna](../bible/cast.md#wenna)): after `wennaAwake`, within
   12 m of the pedestal, a two-note hum every 9 s, SFX `wennaHum`:
   `function (t) { T({ t, f: 196, d: 0.9, v: 0.05 }); T({ t: t + 0.9, f: 262, d: 1.1, v: 0.05 }); return 2.0; }`.
   Its twin: the pedestal's three seed sockets brighten (+0.3 emissive) for 0.6 s with each hum.
8. **Lore tab:** a "Wenna said" list under The Myth showing every `seen:wenna.w<n>` line in number order.
9. **Flags:** register `seen:wenna.*` (family `seen`) and `wennaLine` (`counter`, 0-60) in `js/content/flags.js`.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ui_frame_wenna_corner` (`ui`, core) | `card` · ship 128 × 128 webp alpha ≤ 12 KB | "A small curl of golden leaves and one pink blossom, painted. Palette: #e8c66a #f7b6c8 #9bd46a." | no ornament |
| `ui_wenna_glow` (`ui`, core) | `card` · ship 1024 × 576 webp alpha ≤ 40 KB | "A soft golden light vignette: empty centre, a warm gold glow toward the edges and corners. Palette: #ffe9a8 #f2b45a." (white-key inverted: key the centre) | the CSS gradient above |

#### Acceptance criteria

- Before `wennaAwake` the pedestal behaves exactly as on `main` (`test:playthrough` unchanged).
- With a fixture `act1-bloomed` + `wennaAwake`: successive Talks show w1, w2, w3, w4, then w1, w2 … in rotation; the
  frame has class `frame-wenna` and the name plate "Wenna"; with `lanternGot` added the next Talk shows w5; with
  `ch8` added, w9.
- Ask the Tree shows `home.hint.wenna.open` and the `wenna` frame when `wennaAwake` is set; Shellby's voice before.
- The music returns to the region track within 1.5 s of closing the dialogue.
- `G.debug.hud.audit()` is clean with the `wenna` frame open at 1080p and 390 × 844, and the frame text meets 4.5:1
  contrast.
- Content lint passes (12 lines + 2 new ids, ≤ 220 characters, no reserved markers).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Assets*, *Tone*, *Original IP only*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/wenna.test.js` (new): `wennaPick` for fixtures at each stage, rotation after all seen, earlier
  stages never returned once a later one holds, night line once per day index.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs wenna` (add: pedestal Talk sequence, frame class and name, Ask the Tree voice switch, music
  restore).
- `bun test` (content lint), `bun run test:playthrough` (unchanged pedestal before `wennaAwake`).

#### Out of scope

I.6 itself and the `label` event (S-066); Wenna's face states and decals (R18, ch12); lines w13-w60 (S-171, S-260,
S-291).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. The step is dormant on every shipped save until S-066 sets `wennaAwake`.

### S-066 · feat: I.6 Wenna Wakes: the lights go out and Nell comes home

Milestone M09 · Depends on: S-064, S-065, S-034, S-056 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#I.6](../bible/story.md#I.6), [../bible/story.md#scene-format](../bible/story.md#scene-format),
[../bible/story.md#act-I](../bible/story.md#act-I), [../bible/atlas-home.md#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky),
[../bible/story.md#art](../bible/story.md#art)

#### Why

I.6 is the turn of the whole game: the bloom stops being an ending, the Tree wakes and names herself, the lights on
the horizon go out one by one, a bright streak climbs to the sky isle, and Nell comes home on the lighthouse beam.
It plants T3 (somebody is taking the lights) and T7 (Shellby knows her name). Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-066 (M09); vision item **The Interlude II: Wenna wakes**.

#### What

1. **`bloomDusk` trigger** (pure helper `bloomDuskDue(S, hour)` in `js/story-home.js`, runtime hook in the same
   module registered in `js/modules.js`): set `flag:bloomDusk` the first time the clock enters the dusk band
   (`tod:dusk` as `js/cond.js` defines it; 18:00-21:00 if it has no definition) after the bloom card has closed, **or**
   at once on Continue when `S.ending` is true and the hour is in dusk or night.
2. **Scene `i6-wenna-wakes`** in `js/content/scenes/home.js`: copy the scene block of
   [../bible/story.md#I.6](../bible/story.md#I.6) **verbatim** (40 steps, eight `say` lines `home.scene.i6.01`-`.08`),
   with `title: 'Wenna Wakes'`, `start: 'auto'`, `when: ['flag:bloomDusk', '!flag:wennaAwake', 'tod:dusk|night',
   'in:home.village', 'calm']`, `skippable: true`, `replay: true`, `endFace: 'w:rootDoor'`,
   `after: ['set:wennaAwake', 'quest:mq-ch7.start']` (R1). If Pip is outside Sprout Village at dusk the scene waits
   and starts the moment he enters it at dusk or night. The I.6 lights-out order is d, a, b, c (R2).
3. **Runtime events** (in `js/cutscenes.js` or the scene runtime S-034 created; each is a few lines):

   | Event | Does |
   |---|---|
   | `warp {who:'pip', at, face}` | moves Pip to the anchor on walkable ground, facing the target |
   | `face {who:'wenna', state}` | no-op for `awake` (the bloom GLB already shows it, R18); logs any other state once |
   | `sky.windowsOn {isle:'near4'}` | sets `sky.winT` of a, b, c, d to 1 for the scene |
   | `sky.windowsOut {isle}` | tweens that card's `winT` 1 → 0 over 0.8 s (cards by land: pinewhistle a, archwater b, duskwick c, threemills d) |
   | `sky.streak {from, to, bright}` | an additive `fx_streak` billboard flies from the card's first window (world point at R 700 along its bearing, at the window's height) to the sky isle over 1.6 s, 6 m wide (10 m and white-gold `#fff1c4` when `bright`) |
   | `fx.skyGlint {at}` | a 2 s additive pulse of `fx_streak` rotated 90° at the sky isle |
   | `spawn {actor, model:'boat_far', at}` / `actor.path {actor, to, s}` / `despawn {actor}` | a `boat_far` billboard (6 m wide) moving at `s` m/s along the straight line, bobbing 0.3 m |
   | `label {who:'wenna', name:'Wenna'}` | the frame's name plate reads "Wenna" from this box on (S-065) |
   | `fx.rootDoorOpen {at}`, `light {id:'rootDoor', on}` | plays S-056's root-door open state (the door is placed by `placeRootDoor`; its visibility reads `wennaAwake`, so the scene forces it visible for its last shots) and lights its window |
   | `toast {t, text}` | feed toast |

   Anchors used: `w:greatTree.face` = the trunk point 7.5 m above the base on the south side; `w:pedestal`;
   `w:rootDoor` (S-056's door); `w:landmark:lighthouse`; `sky:isle_a..f` and `sky:skyIsle` = a world point 200 m
   from the camera along the card's bearing at the card's mid height (so shots aim at it). Replays restore Pip and the
   sky's `winT` overrides at the end.
4. **SFX** (`js/audio.js` SFX bank, shipped `T` / `N` / `bell` helpers):
   `leafRustle: N({ t, d: 0.5, v: 0.06, type: 'bandpass', f: 2400, f1: 4200 })` · `lightOut: bell(t, 880, 0.6, 0.25);
   T({ t, f: 880, f1: 440, d: 0.35, v: 0.05 })` · `mote: three T({ t: t + i * 0.06, f: 1320, f1: 2640, d: 0.5, v:
   0.04, type: 'triangle' })` · `boatBell: bell(t, 660, 1.6, 0.4)` · `rootCreak: N({ t, d: 0.6, v: 0.08, type:
   'bandpass', f: 220, f1: 160 })`. Each returns its length.
5. **Quests** (`js/content/quests/home.js`, R1, R31):
   - `mq-act1` gains step 3 `dusk`: text `qt.act1.s3` "Visit the Great Tree at dusk.", done `flag:wennaAwake`,
     on `SCENE_END`, target `w:pedestal`, hint `["She's been sleepy all day. Maybe evening suits her.", "Be in Sprout
     Village when the sky turns orange. Wait by the Tree if you like."]`. The shipped compass strings for steps 0-2
     are untouched.
   - New `mq-ch7` skeleton: `{ id: 'mq-ch7', n: 7, kind: 'main', realm: 'home', title: 'qt.ch7.title' ("Under the
     Roots"), start: 'flag:wennaAwake', doneWhen: 'flag:ch7', steps: [{ id: 'nook', text: 'qt.ch7.s1' ("Go through the
     root door on the Great Tree's north side."), short "Under the Roots", target: { anchor: 'rootDoor' }, done:
     'realm:nook.visited', on: 'REALM', hint: ["She said north. Walk round her trunk.", "The root door is on the far
     side of the Great Tree from the pedestal. Walk up and press {interact}."] }], set: [] }`.
   - **Grown-quest rule** in `js/quests.js` (generic, pure, ~10 lines): on load and on every `advance`, a quest whose
     stored value is `'done'` but whose `doneWhen` condition fails and which has a step whose `done` fails, is reset to
     the index of that first failing step. A unit test covers it. (This is how S-067 and S-068a/b add steps to
     `mq-ch7` without stranding saves.)
6. **The world after I.6** (all read `nellHome` / `wennaAwake`; nothing else changes): cottage 2's door line becomes
   `home.door.nell.back` "A note on the door: 'Back! ...Told you. — N.'" once `nellHome` is set (the shipped line
   stays the text before it; implement as a flag-keyed override of `DOOR_LINES[1]` in `js/interact.js`, the data entry
   untouched); the lighthouse stops burning by day (S-011 already reads `nellHome`); the near horizon windows stay dark
   at night (S-064); the root door shows (S-056).
7. **`test:playthrough` guard:** right after the ending card closes, call `G.debug.tod(12)` (one line, with a comment
   "I.6 is tested by the `wenna` suite and chapter ch6") so ch0 never crosses into dusk mid-walk.
8. **Flags:** register `bloomDusk`, `wennaAwake`, `nellHome` (kind `story`, `setBy: 'I.6 S-066'`) and
   `scene:i6-wenna-wakes`.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `boat_far` (`billboards`, core) | `card` · ship 256 × 256 webp alpha ≤ 16 KB | "A tiny sailing boat seen from far away at night, one small triangular sail, a warm lantern glowing at the masthead, a soft light reflection on dark water. Palette: #ffd36b #f3e3c3 #2a3358." | a 6 px warm dot `#ffd36b` with a 24 px additive glow |
| `fx_streak` (`billboards`, core) | `card` · ship 64 × 512 webp alpha ≤ 20 KB, additive | "A thin vertical streak of soft golden light with a bright head and a fading tail, for additive blending. Palette: #fff1c4 #ffd36b." | canvas vertical gradient `#fff1c4` → transparent |

#### Acceptance criteria

- A bloomed save (`act1-bloomed`) loaded at 18:30 in the village plays `i6-wenna-wakes` within 1 s; at 12:00 it does
  not; loaded at 22:00 on the Shores it plays the moment Pip enters Sprout Village.
- Watching or skipping (hold {interact}) ends with `nellHome`, `wennaAwake`, `scene:i6-wenna-wakes` set, `mq-act1`
  done, `mq-ch7` at step 0, compass "Under the Roots", and Pip facing the root door.
- During the scene the windows go out in the order d, a, b, c (each `winT` reaches 0 after the previous one), and the
  bright streak ends at the sky isle; after the scene every near window is dark at night.
- Cottage 2's door reads the "Back!" note; the lighthouse lamp is dark by day.
- The scene replays from the Lore tab with no flag changes and returns Pip to where he stood.
- A save with `mq-ch7: 'done'` and no `ch7` flag, after a later step adds a step, reopens at that step (unit test).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *One save slot* (no schema change), *Assets*, *Tone*,
  *Original IP only*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/story-home.test.js` (new): `bloomDuskDue` truth table; scene data validates against the runner's
  schema (seven step kinds, known events, text ids exist); skip applies `nellHome` then `after` in order;
  grown-quest rule.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs wenna` (add: dusk trigger, skip end state, window order via `G.debug.sky.cards()`, door line,
  Continue-at-night trigger on entering the village).
- `bun run test:playthrough`, `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope

The Nook first-visit scene and Under the Roots wiring (S-067); Nell as an NPC (S-068a); the jetty (S-068b); Wenna's
face decals (R18).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Risk: a scene that never starts because `calm` never holds near the village at dusk (an enemy chasing Pip
across the village edge). The town leash (charter decision 4) already stops chasers at the village edge; the
in-page test spawns one at the edge to prove the scene still starts within 3 s.

### S-067 · feat: chapter 7 Under the Roots: Wenna explains the beacons and the Lantern

Milestone M09 · Depends on: S-066, S-061, S-036, S-030 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.under_roots.hidden` → `false` ·
Bible: [../bible/story.md#ch7](../bible/story.md#ch7), [../bible/story.md#I.5](../bible/story.md#I.5),
[../bible/story.md#act-cards](../bible/story.md#act-cards), [../bible/dungeons.md#d1](../bible/dungeons.md#d1),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#pips-nook](../bible/atlas-home.md#pips-nook)

#### Why

The first dungeon was built in M08 and has been hidden behind `?realm=under_roots`. This step turns it into chapter 7:
the Nook's first visit, the Glowbud Lantern given by Wenna's own spark, Sir Rollo, and Wenna at the bottom explaining
the beacons. In the same PR the bloom card becomes an act break (owner decision 7, CANON §14: a card ships with the
content behind it). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-067 (M09); vision item **The Interlude II: Wenna wakes**.

#### What

1. **Unhide** `under_roots` (`REALMS.under_roots.hidden = false`). The Nook stair portal `nook:stair`
   (`[E] Enter · Under the Roots`, needs `flag:wennaAwake`) is now offered. A portal whose target realm is still
   `hidden` is never offered: if S-026's portal code lacks that guard, add it (one line) with a unit test.
2. **`mq-ch7` gains three steps** (append to the S-066 skeleton; R31). Texts are `qt.ch7.s2`-`s4`, hints `ht.ch7.*`:

   | Step id | Journal text | Done | On | Target | Hint nudge | Hint answer |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `lantern` | Find what's glowing Under the Roots. | `flag:lanternGot` | `ITEM_GET` | realm `under_roots` · `entry` | Follow the little lights in the walls. | Take the stair down from your Nook and keep going deeper. The glowing bud is in the middle rooms. |
   | `rollo` | Find out what keeps rolling the tunnels flat. | `flag:rolloCalm` | `BOSS_DEFEATED` | room r8 | Listen for the rumbling. | Sir Rollo is in the big round room at the bottom. Hop his rolls and hit him when he's curled and stuck. |
   | `wenna` | Hear what the Great Tree has to say. | `flag:scene:ch7-wenna-bottom` | `SCENE_END` | room r9 | She's listening, down at the very bottom. | Walk into the glowing root chamber past Sir Rollo. |

3. **Chapter banners** (generic, `js/quests.js` runtime): when a main quest with `n ≥ 7` is active and
   `!flag:seen:banner.<questId>`, show the shipped region-card banner with big line "Chapter {n}" and small line the
   quest title for 3.0 s, never during a scene (queued until the scene ends), then set `seen:banner.<questId>`.
4. **Scenes** (`js/content/scenes/home.js` and `js/content/scenes/under_roots.js`; R23 ids):
   - `ch7-nook` (start `enter:nook`, first time; 12 s): `fade in 0.4` · `shot look at the bed (n1 tiles (1, 1)-(1, 2))` ·
     `say wenna` (frame `wenna`, off-screen) `home.scene.ch7nook.01` "Your room's grown, little sprout. I had more space
     than I knew." · `shot look at the stair ^ (1, 6)` · `say wenna` `.02` "There's a stair at the back. Mind the steps.
     I never did get round to steps." `after: []`.
   - `ch7-lantern` (start `event:CHEST_OPEN` for the r5 treasure chest `$` (25, 20); find its id with
     `grep -n "treasure\|(25, 20)\|25, 20" js/realms/dungeons/under_roots.js`): `shot look at the chest` · `say wenna` `ur.scene.lantern.01`
     "It's dark down here, isn't it? I never minded. But you're not a root." · `say wenna` `.02` "Hold still. This might
     tickle." · `emit fx.sparkDown {at: chest}` (a gold spark runs 2 m down the wall root into the bud, 1.2 s) ·
     `emit sfx.sparkle` · `emit item {id: 'lantern', text: 'You got the Glowbud Lantern! It glows with the Great
     Tree\'s own light. Hold it up with {tool}.'}` · `say wenna` `.03` "A little of me, in a bud. It lights the dark. I
     think it can do more than that. We'll see." · `flag lanternGot`. `after: ['give:lantern']`. The chest no longer
     grants the Lantern itself (S-060's grant moves into this `after`, so a skipped scene still gives it once).
     The item-get text above replaces S-057's.
   - Sir Rollo: S-061's handover sets `rolloCalm` (add `set:rolloCalm` to its `after` if it does not). His later Talk
     lines (`ur.npc.rollo.after.1`, priority 20): until `jettyOpen` "Forty years of rolling, and all anybody had to
     say was 'stop'. ...Nobody did, you know. Not once."; from `jettyOpen` (dormant until S-068b) "I'm rolling Digby's
     tunnels now. He asked nicely. Nobody's ever asked nicely."
   - `ch7-wenna-bottom` (start `enter:` the r9 room box [20, 24, 11, 9] tiles; 45 s): `fade out 0.3` · `warp pip to
     (25, 31) facing the heartwood knot I (25, 28)` · `fade in 0.5` · `shot two at the knot` · five `say wenna` lines
     `ur.scene.bottom.01`-`.05` verbatim from [../bible/story.md#ch7](../bible/story.md#ch7) ("Down here is where I'm
     most me. Can you feel it hum?" … "You'll need a boat. Nell needs a lamp to sail at night. Pip, I think you're
     holding one.") with `emit fx.rootGlow` (a gold pulse running along the spiral-marked root, 2.5 s) as a `go: true`
     step just before line 2 · `shot pov`. `after: []`.
   - **The root lift**: the r9 exit `X` (30, 28) becomes `[E] Use · Root Lift` → realm `nook` spawn `lift` (beside the
     stair `^`, n1 tile (2, 6)); first use sets `S.dungeons.under_roots.done = true`.
   - `ch7-keepsakes` (start `enter:nook` with `flag:scene:ch7-wenna-bottom`, first time; 6 s): `shot look at the n2
     shelves` · `emit shelves.show` · `emit toast {t: 'home.toast.keepsakes', text: '✿ Your keepsakes found a home in
     the Nook'}`. Until this scene has played, the Keepsake Hollow shelves show only dotted outlines (change S-056's
     shelf build to read `flag:scene:ch7-keepsakes`); afterwards every found keepsake shows, and new finds appear when
     found.
5. **The act card** (`js/content/cards.js`, new; `js/ui.js` `openEnding(cardId = 'act1')`):

   ```js
   export const ACT_CARDS = {
     act1: { et: '✿ Starseed Isle is blooming again! ✿', es: 'End of Act One · The Sneeze',
             ex: 'But far across the water, the lights are going out...', art: 'ending_art' },
   };
   ```
   `.ex` shows `ex` then a line break then "Press any button to continue." (touch: "Tap to continue."). The strings
   "Thanks for playing" and "The isle is yours to explore." leave `js/ui.js` (line 136 and 855). `grep -rn "Thanks for
   playing\|yours to explore" README.md docs/ assets/` and update any caption that quotes them.
6. **Tests that change:** `tests/playthrough.mjs` asserts the card's `.es` reads "End of Act One · The Sneeze" and
   `.ex` starts with "But far across the water"; `tests/opening.mjs` has no ending check on `main` and gains none.
7. **Chapter test `ch7`**: `tests/fixtures/saves/ch7-start.json` = the act1 end state of
   [../bible/story.md#I.6](../bible/story.md#I.6) (`ending`, `bloom`, `wennaAwake`, `nellHome`, three `calm:*`, three
   `handover:*`, `mq-act1: 'done'`, `mq-ch7: 0`); spec `tests/fixtures/chapters/ch7.json`: `realm nook` →
   `scene.skip` → `realm under_roots entry` → `warpTo` the r5 chest → `use` → `scene.skip` → `killBoss
   under_roots:rollo` → `scene.skip` → `warpTo` r9 → `scene.skip` → `use` the root lift → `scene.skip`; expect flags
   `lanternGot`, `rolloCalm`, `scene:ch7-wenna-bottom`, `scene:ch7-keepsakes`, items `{ lantern: 1 }`, quests
   `{ 'mq-ch7': 'done' }`, realm `nook`. Add `ch7` to the CI chapter matrix. (S-068a and S-068b extend this spec.)
8. **Flags:** register `lanternGot`, `rolloCalm` (story), `seen:banner.*` (seen family).

#### Assets

None new (the Lantern icon, Rollo and the dungeon art shipped in S-057-S-062; `ending_art` is reused by `act1`).

#### Acceptance criteria

- From `ch7-start`, the chapter can be played to the root lift with no debug help: the stair is offered in the Nook,
  every room of `under_roots` is reachable, the Lantern comes from the r5 chest through `ch7-lantern`, and the quest
  objective follows each step (compass "Under the Roots" → the dungeon).
- Skipping `ch7-lantern` still gives exactly one Lantern and sets `lanternGot`.
- "Chapter 7 · Under the Roots" shows once, after I.6, for 3.0 s.
- The bloom card reads "End of Act One · The Sneeze" in `test:playthrough`; nothing in `js/` contains "Thanks for
  playing".
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch7` passes; the realm suite passes for `under_roots` and `nook`.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Frozen realms* (the `under_roots` prints are unchanged:
  only interactions change), *Hidden until whole* (no other realm unhidden; the card ships with its content),
  *Tone*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test` (quest data and hint lint, scene validation, portal hidden-realm guard).
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch7`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `node tests/inpage.mjs wenna` (add: banner once,
  shelves empty before `ch7-keepsakes`).
- `bun run test:playthrough`, `bun run test:opening`, `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope

Nell and the jetty steps (S-068a, S-068b); villager reactions to the Lantern (S-068a); the act card art for later acts
(S-165 onward).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert re-hides the dungeon and restores the shipped card. Risk: the act card and the chapter must land in the
same umbrella merge; the plan job never files S-068a before this step is stacked (INDEX §1.1).

<a id="s-068a"></a>

### S-068a · feat: Nell Pelliwick is home: the bird rig, her look, portrait and lines

Milestone M09 · Depends on: S-067, S-030, S-033a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/cast.md#nell](../bible/cast.md#nell), [../bible/story.md#ch7](../bible/story.md#ch7),
[../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots)

#### Why

Nell is the player's ferry for the rest of the game, and in chapter 7 she is the reason the Lantern matters ("Nobody
sails the Wide Blue at night without a lamp"). This step makes her a person at her cottage door, builds the `bird`
rig family that Rook, Postmistress Pell, Pinfeather, Goody Gander and the ducks will reuse as data only, and adds the
villagers' reactions to the Lantern. Split from S-068 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-068 (M09); vision item **The Interlude II: Wenna wakes**.

#### What

1. **The `bird` rig family** (`js/models.js`, one `npcDef` case reading a look record; [../bible/cast.md](../bible/cast.md)
   "Rig families"): a pear `lathe` body, a `sphere` head, wing arms `armL` / `armR` as thin `rbox` plates (talk wave =
   arm rotation), two flattened-`cone` webbed feet, and a beak by param `beak`: `'pouch'` (a 0.45 m lathe bill plus a
   scaled `sphere` pouch under it), `'flat'` (a short flat duck bill), `'cone'`, `'chisel'`, `'parrot'`. Accessories by
   name in `acc`: `rainhat` (`cyl` crown + flattened `sphere` brim, wide at the back), `scarf` (`torus`), `jumper` (a
   lathe band over the body), `patch` (a 0.12 m disc on the chest), `rod` (an `extra` `cyl` 1.2 m slung across the
   back). Outlined toon materials, `INK` eyes. ≤ 1,800 tris per bird.
2. **Look records** (`js/content/looks.js`): `nell: { rig: 'bird', h: 1.35, beak: 'pouch', pal: { body: '#f7f4ec',
   wingtip: '#8f97a3', bill: '#f2b48a', pouch: '#f6c9a4', feet: '#f29b38', hat: '#e2b33c', jumper: '#3fa79a', patch:
   '#f2c94c', scarf: '#c2563f', rod: '#9b7a52' }, acc: ['rainhat', 'scarf', 'jumper', 'patch', 'rod'] }` and
   `duck: { rig: 'bird', h: 0.5, beak: 'flat', pal: { body: '#f6f1e4', bill: '#f2b45a', feet: '#f2b45a' }, acc: [] }`
   (the five Quackenbush ducks are scene actors in S-068b until S-136 names them).
3. **`placeSpots`** (new home post-pass, row 6, `new RNG(7151 * 83 + 29)`,
   [../bible/atlas-home.md#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots)): first entry `home:spot-nell-door` =
   `near(cottage 2 door (13.3, −14.1) + 2 m along the door's outward direction, 0, 3)` with the plaza-spot `ok()`
   (`pathDAt ≥ 2.2`, ≥ 3 m from every other spot, not on the z = 9 line within |x| < 14). Writes `W.spots` only.
4. **Nell in the world:** visible from `flag:nellHome`. Until S-068b she stands at `home:spot-nell-door` at every hour
   (facing the plaza); S-068b adds her jetty spot. Prompt `[E] Talk · Nell Pelliwick`. Her first Talk sets
   `met:nell`. Sound cue: a reel's click-whirr every 12 s within 10 m (SFX `reel`: `N({ t, d: 0.35, v: 0.05, type:
   'highpass', f: 2600 })` repeated 4 × at 0.07 s); its twin: her rod tip twitches 8° with each click.
5. **Dialogue** (`js/content/dialogue/home.js`, npc `nell`, speaker "Nell Pelliwick"):

   | Entry | When | Priority | Lines |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `nell.lantern` (once, sets `seen:home.nell.lantern`) | `quest:mq-ch7>=4`, `item:lantern` | 80 | "Now that's a lamp. Clip it on the mast and we'll see the reef from a mile off." / "I'll need a jetty, mind. The old one washed out before you were born. Ribbit's been 'about to build it' for years." |
   | `nell.home` | `flag:nellHome`, `!item:lantern` | 20 | "Pelliwick. Out past the reef longer than I meant. Pinch kept his lamp burning the whole time, bless his shell." / "The Dandelion's sound. Her lamp's not. Nobody sails the Wide Blue at night without a lamp, and the nights are getting darker out there." |

   (Italics in story.md are dropped: text tables are plain.)
6. **`mq-ch7` gains step `nell`** (index 4; R31): "Show Nell your Lantern.", done `flag:seen:home.nell.lantern`, on
   `TALK_END`, target `npc:nell`, hint ["Who needed a lamp to sail at night?", "Nell Pelliwick stands at her cottage
   door by day, east of the Tree. Talk to her."]. Saves where `mq-ch7` is already `'done'` reopen here (S-066's rule).
7. **Villagers react to the Lantern** (the ch7 column of [../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions),
   priority 50, `when: ['flag:lanternGot', '!flag:ch8']`, verbatim): Shellby "A spark of her, in a bud. I saw one of
   those once. ...A long time ago, dear." · Ribbit "A lantern from the Tree herself! I shall have it added to the town
   crest. We have a town crest now." · Miso "That Lantern's not for sale, is it? ...Pity. I'd have given you a fair
   price~" · Clover "You went UNDER the Tree? Was it dark? Were there bugs? Was one of them huge? I knew it!" · Hoot "A
   glowbud! Notoriously rare. I have written about them extensively. I have never seen one." · Digby "A lantern? I'll
   take your word it's bright. Smells nice, though. Like her blossom."
8. **Charter:** add Nell's "...eventually." (end of a promise) to the reserved-markers table if it is not there.
9. **Chapter spec `ch7`:** insert `talk nell` after the root lift; expect `met:nell`, `seen:home.nell.lantern`;
   `mq-ch7` still ends `'done'` (5 steps).

#### Assets

- **`portrait_nell`** (`ui`, core, `assets/portraits/nell.webp`, 512² webp ≤ 60 KB): kind `portrait`, subject (cast.md
  Look + palette): "A tall, calm white pelican sailor woman with a long peach bill and soft pouch, a mustard-yellow
  rain hat with a wide back brim, a chunky teal fisherman's jumper with a little yellow dandelion patch, a red
  knitted scarf, a fishing rod over her shoulder, a dry knowing half-smile. Palette: #f7f4ec #f2b48a #e2b33c #3fa79a."
  Process with the cast.md portrait recipe. Fallback: name only.
- **Turnaround** `docs/art/ref/nell.webp` (kind `sheet`, docs only).
- **Model slot** `// npc_nell: { src: 'assets/models/npc_nell.glb', height: 1.35 },` (commented). ART BRIEF
  `npc_nell` (paste from [../bible/cast.md#nell](../bible/cast.md#nell): silhouette tall teardrop body, long horizontal
  bill 0.45 m and a wide hat brim; 1.35 m; soft-painted feathers, knit jumper, oilcloth hat; standing, one wing on the
  rod strap; ≤ 6,000 tris; the 3D prompt with the 3D preamble; fallback the `bird` rig above).

#### Acceptance criteria

- With `nellHome` set Nell stands at `home:spot-nell-door`, reachable and facing the plaza; without it she is absent.
- Talk lines follow the table; with the Lantern and `mq-ch7` at step 4, one Talk completes the step.
- A save with `mq-ch7: 'done'` from S-067 reopens at `nell` on load.
- Each of the six villagers says their Lantern line once `lanternGot` is set and before `ch8`.
- `placeSpots` passes the four home-pass tests (seeds 1-40); world prints byte-identical.
- The `bird` rig is ≤ 1,800 tris and ≤ 6 materials; `npcDef('nell')` and `npcDef('duck')` build without a GLB.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Assets*, *Tone*, *Original IP only*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

- `node tests/inpage.mjs wenna` (add: Nell visible after `nellHome`, Talk lines, quest step).
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch7`.

#### Out of scope
