<a id="s-068b"></a>

### S-068b · feat: Nell's Jetty: Ribbit's speech, the moored Dandelion and a fog-bank chart

Milestone M09 · Depends on: S-068a, S-026, S-030 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-home.md#placeJetty](../bible/atlas-home.md#placeJetty), [../bible/story.md#ch7](../bible/story.md#ch7),
[../bible/systems.md#chart](../bible/systems.md#chart), [../bible/systems.md#brief-boat](../bible/systems.md#brief-boat),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-jetty](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-jetty)

#### Why

The chapter ends with a speech, a jetty and a boat: the moment the game's map grows past the home isle. Before
Duskwick exists the chart shows only fog ("Nell won't risk the fog yet."), which keeps the release honest while
promising the sea. Split from S-068 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-068
(M09); vision item **The Interlude II: Wenna wakes**.

#### What

1. **`placeJetty`** (home post-pass row 5, `new RNG(7151 * 79 + 23)`, rules of
   [../bible/atlas-home.md#placeJetty](../bible/atlas-home.md#placeJetty)):
   - Root = `near((−14, 126), 0, 6)` with `ok()` plus: the 26 m run due south from the root crosses no bridge, chest,
     interactable or arena ring, stays ≥ 6 m from the lighthouse collider and ≥ 2 m from every collider, and its last
     6 m are over water deeper than −1.2 m (on seed 7151 the root lands within 1 m of the anchor).
   - Deck `{ ax: root.x, az: root.z, bx: root.x, bz: root.z + 22, w: 2.4, h0: 1.3, h1: 1.3 }`; T-head deck 4 × 4 m
     centred at `(root.x, root.z + 24)`, top Y 1.3. Both are post-decks through `attachPost(W)` with `active` read from
     `flag:jettyOpen` (the reachability tests run with `active: true`).
   - Pilings every 3 m on both sides (x = root.x ± 1.3); those standing in walkable shallows get `solid: { r: 0.18 }`.
     The first three east-side pilings (z = root.z + 3, + 6, + 9) are **the old posts**: drawn from `wennaAwake`; the
     rest only from `jettyOpen`.
   - **Old mooring post** at `root + (0, −0.6)` on the sand, drawn from `wennaAwake`: `[E] Read · Mooring post` →
     `home.jetty.post.1` "Nell's mooring. A note: 'Back... eventually. No sailing without a lamp. — N.'" before
     `lanternGot`; `home.jetty.post.2` "Nell's mooring. A new note: 'Lamp: found. Jetty: Ribbit. — N.'" from
     `lanternGot` until `jettyOpen`; removed at `jettyOpen`.
   - **Mooring post** at the T-head's east corner `(root.x + 2.0, root.z + 26)`, drawn from `jettyOpen`, carrying the
     portal `home:jetty` (`how: 'sail'`, `verb: 'Sail'`, `name: 'the Dandelion'`, `needs: 'flag:jettyOpen'`; R3, R28).
     Spawn `jetty_end` on the T-head.
   - **The *Dandelion*** moored, no collider: from `wennaAwake` at `(root.x + 3.0, root.z + 7)`, bow south, tied to the
     old posts; from `jettyOpen` at the T-head's east side `(root.x + 3.4, root.z + 24)`, bow south.
   - `placeSpots` appends `home:spot-nell-jetty`: on the deck 3 m short of the T-head `(root.x, root.z + 19)`. From
     `jettyOpen` Nell stands there 05:00-18:00 and at `home:spot-nell-door` otherwise (static rule until schedules,
     S-174).
2. **Jetty kit** (`js/props-kit.js`, new module; [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-jetty](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-jetty)):
   `kit_jetty_plank` (planks as `bridgeGeo`-style boxes every 0.45 m, `#c8a878`, gaps `#5e4a34`), `kit_jetty_post`
   (`cyl` 0.3 m, 1.0 m above deck, `#8a6a48`), `kit_bollard` (0.5 m iron `#4a4a52` with a rope coil `#e8dcc0`), a
   crate of nets on the T-head (`#a87a48`). One instanced type each. Whole jetty ≤ 2,400 tris. (The lamp post joins the
   kit with the beacon in S-082a.)
3. **The *Dandelion*** (code-built, `js/props.js`; brief [../bible/systems.md#brief-boat](../bible/systems.md#brief-boat)):
   hull a flattened lathe 5.5 × 2.0 m, cream `#f3e3c3` with a leaf-green stripe `#7aa63a`; deck box `#c9a06b`; mast
   `cyl` 6.0 m; one curved triangular sail `#f6d36b` with a white seed-clock emblem `#fffbe8` (canvas decal); tiller;
   bow lantern hook. Bob 0.12 m / 2.4 s, roll ±3°. ≤ 2,500 tris. Commented slot
   `// model_dandelion: { src: 'assets/models/dandelion.glb', height: 6.0 },`.
4. **Ribbit's call** (priority 80, `quest:mq-ch7>=5`, `!flag:jettyOpen`): `home.npc.ribbit.jetty.1` "Ah, Pip! Nell says
   we need a jetty. I have been about to build it for years. To the beach below the lighthouse! There will be a
   speech." (new line written here in his voice; the plan job keeps it). From this Talk Ribbit is not at his plaza spot
   but on the sand at the jetty root until the scene ends.
5. **Scene `ch7-jetty`** (`js/content/scenes/home.js`; start `enter:` circle (root, r 8) with `quest:mq-ch7>=5` and
   `calm`; 40 s; replayable). Scene actors spawned at the first fade: Ribbit `root + (−2, −3)`, Nell `root + (2, −3)`,
   Clover `root + (0, −5)`, five `duck` actors at `root + (−4, −6)`, `(−2, −6.5)`, `(0, −7)`, `(2, −6.5)`,
   `(4, −6)`, and Captain Pinch (the `boss_crab` model as an actor, idle) on the lighthouse gallery at `(6.9, Y 13.9,
   118.6)` facing the jetty (his handover is always done by now). Steps: `fade out 0.4` · spawns · `shot wide at
   root from root + (0, −12) up 3 m` · `fade in 0.5` · `say ribbit` `home.scene.jetty.01` "Citizens! Ducks! Small frog
   in the well, if you can hear me! Ahem." · `say ribbit` `.02` "Today, on behalf of the town treasury, the town, and
   the treasury..." · `shot two at npc:ribbit` · `say ribbit` `.03` "I hereby declare this jetty... jettied. RIBBIT!" ·
   `fade white 0.3` · `flag jettyOpen` · `emit jetty.build` (deck, T-head, pilings, mooring post and the boat at the
   T-head appear) · `emit sfx.croak` · `emit sfx.duckRipple` · `fade in 0.4` · `shot look at the T-head` · `say nell`
   `.04` "It's a jetty, Mayor. ...It's a good jetty. Hop aboard, Pip." · `fade out 0.3` · despawn every actor · `fade in
   0.3`. `after: ['set:jettyOpen']`. SFX `duckRipple`: five `T({ t: t + i * 0.12, f: 520, f1: 380, d: 0.12, v: 0.06,
   type: 'square', lp: 1800 })`; `croak` reuses the shipped frog sound if `grep -n "croak" js/audio.js` finds one,
   else `T({ t, f: 180, f1: 120, d: 0.25, v: 0.12, type: 'sawtooth', lp: 900 })`.
6. **`mq-ch7` gains step `jetty`** (index 5) and its `set: ['set:ch7']` (R31): "Meet Mayor Ribbit at the beach below
   the lighthouse.", done `flag:jettyOpen`, on `SCENE_END`, target the jetty root, hint ["There's a speech happening.
   Speeches always happen.", "Go south past the lighthouse to the old posts on the beach. Mayor Ribbit is waiting
   there."].
7. **The chart, fog-bank version** (built here, made a map-card tab in S-080):
   - Pure `js/chart.js`: `CHART_NODES`, the node table of [../bible/systems.md#chart](../bible/systems.md#chart)
     verbatim (id, name, chart xy in 1600 × 1000 units, `listed` condition, lane, fog line), and `chartView(S, REALMS)`
     → `{ mode, caption, nodes: [{ id, name, x, y, state: 'home' | 'open' | 'fog', line }] }`. Before `item:chart`:
     `mode: 'sketch'`, caption "Nell knows the way to Duskwick.", nodes home and Duskwick only. With `item:chart`:
     `mode: 'chart'`, every node whose `listed` holds. A node is `fog` when its realm is `hidden` or its registry `open`
     condition fails, else `open`.
   - `js/hud/chart-card.js`: `ChartCard`, an overlay (`ui.menu = 'chart'`) with a canvas drawn at 1600 × 1000 and
     scaled to fit: parchment `#efe3c8`; home as a green blob `#9cc79a` r 60 at (800, 520) with a tree dot `#6f8f3a`;
     dashed lanes `#b8a07a` 3 px, dash 10/8, from home to each node; open nodes a 18 px circle `#f2b45a` with a cream
     ring; fog nodes three overlapping `#c9c3d6` circles (r 34, 28, 24) at alpha 0.85; names in the HUD font. Controls:
     arrows / stick move the selection between nodes, {interact} / Enter selects, Esc / pad B closes; touch taps a node.
     Selecting a `fog` node toasts its line ("Nell won't risk the fog yet."); an `open` node does the same until S-081.
   - `[E] Sail · the Dandelion` (the `home:jetty` portal, kind `sail`) opens the ChartCard.
   - **To be continued** (while no isle realm is unhidden, i.e. every chart node except home is `fog`; releases M09-M11
     ship this way): once `mq-ch7` is done the compass reads **"To be continued ✿"** (not the shipped "The isle is
     blooming ✿"), and the journal's Quests tab shows a greyed entry "Chapter 8" with the body "To be continued ✿"
     (side-content rule 5's "past the fog" placeholder, reused for a main quest; `js/journal.js` renders it from a
     `placeholder: true` quest row `mq-ch8-tbc` that has no steps and never starts). The test is data:
     `tbc(S, REALMS) = flag:ch7 && every isle realm hidden`.
8. **Nell's line after the jetty** (priority 50, `flag:jettyOpen`, `!flag:ch8`): while `tbc` holds, "The fog's thick
   past the reef this season. When it lifts, we sail. I'll know before you do."; once Duskwick is unhidden, "Lamp's on
   the mast. She's ready when you are." Rollo's second line (S-067) becomes live. S-091 removes the `tbc` compass
   text, the placeholder row and the fog line in the PR that unhides Duskwick.
9. **Chapter spec `ch7`:** after `talk nell`, `talk ribbit` → `warpTo` the jetty root → `scene.skip`; expect `ch7`,
   `jettyOpen`, `mq-ch7: 'done'`; a final `use` of `home:jetty` asserts `G.ui.menu === 'chart'`.
10. **Flags:** register `jettyOpen`, `ch7` (story, `setBy: 'ch7 S-068b'`).

#### Assets

- Code-built: the jetty kit pieces and the *Dandelion* (briefs: `home_jetty` from
  [../bible/atlas-home.md#placeJetty](../bible/atlas-home.md#placeJetty) and `model_dandelion` from
  [../bible/systems.md#brief-boat](../bible/systems.md#brief-boat), pasted in the PR).
- Turnaround reference `docs/art/ref/dandelion.webp` (kind `sheet`, the brief's image prompt; docs only).
- No new manifest images (the painted chart arrives in S-080).

#### Acceptance criteria

- `placeJetty` passes the four home-pass tests on seeds 1-40; on 7151 the root is within 1 m of (−14, 126) and the
  run's last 6 m are over water deeper than −1.2 m; world prints byte-identical.
- Before `wennaAwake` nothing of the jetty is drawn; from `wennaAwake` the old posts, the old mooring post and the
  moored boat show; from `jettyOpen` the deck is walkable from the sand to the T-head and Pip can stand at `jetty_end`.
- From `ch7` at step `jetty`, talking to Ribbit and walking to the beach plays `ch7-jetty`; watched or skipped it ends
  with `jettyOpen` and `ch7` set and `mq-ch7` done.
- `[E] Sail · the Dandelion` opens the chart: sketch mode with home and a fogged Duskwick and the caption; selecting
  Duskwick toasts "Nell won't risk the fog yet."; the chart closes with Esc, pad B and a tap outside.
- `G.debug.hud.audit()` clean with the chart open at 1080p and 390 × 844.
- With `ch7` done and every isle hidden, the compass reads "To be continued ✿", the journal shows the greyed
  "Chapter 8 · To be continued ✿" row, and Nell says the fog line; with Duskwick unhidden by debug, none of the three
  appear (unit test on `tbc(S, REALMS)` plus the `wenna` in-page case).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Hidden until whole* (every isle a fog bank), *Budgets per
  view* (the jetty view at the Shores stays ≤ 200 main-pass calls), *Assets*, *Tone*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/world.test.js` (placeJetty, placeSpots), `bun test tests/chart.test.js` (new: `chartView` modes,
  node states for fixtures with and without `item:chart`, hidden realms → fog).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs wenna` (add: jetty states by flag, deck walkability, scene end state, chart open and fog
  toast, audit); `node tests/chapter.mjs ch7`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs wp7` (the Shores fixed view's draw calls).

#### Out of scope

Sailing anywhere (S-081); the map card's World tab and the painted chart (S-080); the moonpath (S-218); Nell's boat
upgrades (M23).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Risk: the boat or the old posts sitting on the playthrough's walking line at the Shores; the pass's
`ok()` keeps 2.5 m from interactables and the playthrough walks the path band, which the jetty does not touch.

<a id="s-069"></a>

### S-069 · test: chapter tests ch1-ch6 cover Act I in any seed order

Milestone M09 · Depends on: S-067, S-030 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness), [../bible/story.md#act-I](../bible/story.md#act-I),
[../bible/story.md#beat-index](../bible/story.md#beat-index)

#### Why

`test:playthrough` proves one route through Act I. The Act I beats added since M01 (handovers, instalments, healed
regions, boughs, the bloom card, I.6) depend on counts and order numbers, so they need proof in every order the
player can choose. These chapter tests are also the first links of the chain S-310 runs end to end. Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-069 (M09); vision item **The Interlude II: Wenna wakes**.

#### What

1. **Chapter specs** (`tests/fixtures/chapters/ch1.json` … `ch6.json`) and start fixtures
   (`tests/fixtures/saves/ch1-start.json` … `ch6-start.json`, v8, the canonical order Woods → Frost → Sea):

   | Chapter | Beat | Start fixture | Steps (harness verbs) | Expect |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `ch1` | I.1 | fresh New Game | `talk shellby` ×4 (intro pages + box 4), `assert introDone` | `introDone`, `mq-act1: 1` |
   | `ch2` | I.2 Woods | `ch1` end | `killBoss woods` → `scene.skip` (handover) → `warpTo pedestal` → `use` | `calm:woods = 1`, `handover:woods`, `seeds.woods = 2`, Shellby `inst:1` after one `talk shellby` |
   | `ch3` | I.3 Frost | `ch2` end | as ch2 for `peaks` | `calm:peaks = 2`, `seeds.peaks = 2`, `inst:2` |
   | `ch4` | I.4 Sea | `ch3` end | `killBoss shores` → `scene.skip`; `talk shellby` | `calm:shores = 3`, `seeds.shores = 1` (carried), `inst:3` |
   | `ch5` | I.5 the bloom | `ch4` end | `warpTo pedestal` → `use` → wait for the card → close it | `S.ending`, `bloom`, `mq-act1` at step 3, card `.es` "End of Act One · The Sneeze" |
   | `ch6` | I.6 | `ch5` end with `tod` 18.5 | `wait 1` → `scene.skip` | `bloomDusk`, `nellHome`, `wennaAwake`, `mq-act1: 'done'`, `mq-ch7: 0`, every near window dark at 23:00 |

2. **Order runs** (`tests/fixtures/chapters/act1-orders.json`, run with `node tests/chapter.mjs act1-orders`): from
   `ch2-start`, play the three Catcher chapters in the orders **Frost → Sea → Woods** and **Sea → Woods → Frost**
   (with `ch2`-`ch4` in the canonical order, each Catcher is first once). Each run asserts `calm:<id>` equals its
   position, the instalment heard after the n-th seed is `inst:n` whichever seed it was, the bough of the placed seed's
   side blossoms (`G.debug` bough state), the village music layer equals the number placed (S-010), and after the
   third placement the bloom card shows.
3. **Chain unit test** (`tests/chapters.test.js`, S-030's file): add `ch1`→`ch2`→…→`ch6`→`ch7`: each end deep-equals the
   next start on `flags`, `items`, `quests`, `seeds`, `collect` (once it exists) and `realm`.
4. **CI**: add `ch1`, `ch2`, `ch3`, `ch4`, `ch5`, `ch6` and `act1-orders` to the chapter matrix (each ≤ 15 min; the
   `killBoss` shortcut plays the handover, not the fight). `test:playthrough` stays as `ch0`, unchanged.

#### Assets

None.

#### Acceptance criteria

- All seven new chapter jobs pass locally and in CI, each under 15 min on the GitHub runners.
- The chain test passes for ch1-ch7.
- A deliberately broken fixture (an extra flag in `ch3-start`) fails the chain test with the field named (shown in
  the PR, then reverted).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Machine* (one browser at a time locally; the PR lists wall times).

#### Verification plan

`node tests/chapter.mjs ch1` … `ch6`, `node tests/chapter.mjs act1-orders`, `bun test tests/chapters.test.js`,
`bun run test:playthrough` (unchanged).

#### Out of scope

Chapter 8 and later chapters (their own steps); the six Act II isle orders (S-150).

#### Risk & rollback

Test-only; one revert. Risk: CI time. If a job passes 15 min, shard `act1-orders` into one job per order.

<a id="s-070"></a>

### S-070 · chore: close M09: Act I + Interlude release notes, DESIGN gains the act structure

Milestone M09 · Depends on: S-069, S-068b, S-001 · Size: S · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/charter.md#close-out](../bible/charter.md#close-out), [../bible/story.md#beat-index](../bible/story.md#beat-index)

#### Why

Close the milestone so the owner's umbrella merge ships a whole, documented chapter: the Interlude and the act card
together. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-070 (M09); vision item **The Interlude II: Wenna wakes**.

#### What

Follow [../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone) items 1-10 in order, with
these milestone specifics:

1. **Suite run** (after `free -h` ≥ 6 GB): `bun test`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs` + `bun run test:pwa`;
   `bun run test:smoke` (and `?noassets=all`); `bun run test:opening`; `bun run test:playthrough`; every in-page suite
   in the CI matrix including `wenna` and `realm`; chapters `ch1`-`ch7` and `act1-orders`.
2. **`docs/DESIGN.md`:** a new "Acts" chapter: the four acts and their counts (Act I three Catchers in any order; Act II
   three isles in any order then the Tower; Act III three Stair Stones in any order; Act IV one climb), the rule "plot
   keyed to counts, lines keyed to places", I.6 and the Interlude, the act cards and chapter banners, and the
   "grown quest" rule. Section 8 notes the handover-to-Wenna flow; section 9 (Field Notes) gains Wenna's voice.
3. **`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`:** `js/sky-home.js` and the sky window quads (one draw), scene events added in M09, the
   chart module (`js/chart.js`, `js/hud/chart-card.js`), `js/props-kit.js`, the `bird` rig family and look records,
   and the post-pass list with constants `placeJetty (79, 23)`, `placeSpots (83, 29)`.
4. **README / CHANGELOG:** a play summary: "Act One now ends at the bloom; at dusk the Great Tree wakes..." (5-8 lines).
5. **`docs/roadmap/progress.md`** entry from the template: hours +3.0, cumulative 7.0; save v8; the Shores and village
   fixed views' calls and tris; core precache size (S-064 and S-066 art).
6. **Bible reconciliations:** copy R1, R2, R3, R18, R23, R27, R28, R31, R33 and R34 into the bible files that lost
   (story.md quest ids and the `mq-ch7` shipping parts; atlas-home's window points and jetty states; dungeons.md scene
   ids; cast.md's prompt-vs-plate note), each as a one-line edit with "(reconciled S-070)".
7. **Release check:** every realm except `home`, `nook` and `under_roots` is still `hidden: true`; the chart shows only
   fog; the `act1` card ships with ch7.

#### Assets

None (no brief batch is scheduled here; M09's models go into batch 03 at S-079).

#### Acceptance criteria

Every item of the charter's milestone close-out list is ticked in the PR body with its numbers; the docs test passes.

#### Verification plan

The suite run above; `bun test tests/docs.test.js`.

#### Out of scope

Any feature code.

#### Risk & rollback

Docs and records only; one revert.

<a id="s-071"></a>

### S-071 · feat: save v9 adds collect maps for the new collectible kinds

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-024, S-025 · Size: S · Save: v8 → v9 · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#save-v9](../bible/systems.md#save-v9), [../bible/systems.md#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles),
[../bible/systems.md#save-plan](../bible/systems.md#save-plan)

#### Why

Nine collectible kinds (acorns, bottles, Lamp Logs, heart petals, vistas, fish, Hollows, Dash times, Dreams) need one
home in the save before any of them ships, so later steps add content, never schema. Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-071 (M10 Collections, hearts and the Completion page); vision item **Collections,
hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. `js/save.js`: `SAVE_VERSION = 9`; `MIGRATIONS[8] = (s) => ({ ...s, collect: { acorn: {}, bottle: {}, log: {},
   petal: {}, vista: {}, fish: {}, hollow: {}, dash: {}, dream: {} } })`; `freshState` includes the same empty maps.
   The backup copy to `starseed-isle-save-v2-bak` (S-003) runs before this migration like any other.
2. `normalize` keeps `collect` only in this shape (anything else dropped, never a failure):

   | Map | Key pattern | Value |
   |---|---|---|
   | `acorn` | `^[a-z_]+:a\d{1,3}$` | `true` |
   | `bottle` | `^[a-z_]+:([1-9]|1[0-2])$` | `true` |
   | `log` | `^[a-z_]+:([1-9]|[12]\d|30)$` | `true` |
   | `petal` | `^([a-z0-9_]+:p\d{1,2}|act-[a-z-]+)$` | `true` |
   | `vista` | `^[a-z_]+:v\d{1,2}$` | `true` |
   | `fish` | `^fish:[a-z_-]+$` (species ids are kebab case, `fish:puddle-minnow`) | integer 1-999 |
   | `hollow` | `^hollow_[a-z]+_\d$` | `true` |
   | `dash` | `^dash:[a-z_]+$` | integer 1-36000 (tenths of a second) |
   | `dream` | `^dream_[a-z]+$` | `true` |

   Unknown kinds are dropped. The hollow key is always the Hollow's realm id (`hollow_archwater_1`, never
   `archwater:1`), and a Hollow's petals are `<hollow realm id>:p<n>` (`hollow_archwater_1:p1`).
   **Idempotent carry-over** (in `normalize`, so it also covers a bottle opened later):
   `if (S.secrets.bottle === true) S.collect.bottle['home:12'] = true` (the shipped bottle is #12 of 12).
3. **Heart cap rides this bump** (INDEX §3 clamp-widening rule): `CFG.maxHp` 20 → **32** (`js/data.js` line
   16). `normalize` already clamps `maxHp` to `CFG.maxHp`, so the clamp widens with the constant. Miso's heart and the
   heart chests already use `Math.min(CFG.maxHp, …)`. No petal source exists yet, so nothing in play changes.
4. Pure helpers `js/content/collect.js`: `has(S, kind, key)`, `count(S, kind)`, `mark(S, kind, key, v = true)`
   (returns false if already marked or the key fails its pattern). `js/cond.js` gains (if S-025 did not ship them) the
   atom `collect:<kind>>=<n>` and the effect `collect:<kind>:<key>`; both registered in the content lint.
5. Fixture `tests/fixtures/saves/v8-ch7.json` (copy of `ch7`'s end state at v8) Continues in `test:smoke`.

#### Assets

None.

#### Acceptance criteria

- v2, v3, v4, v5, v6, v7 and v8 saves migrate to v9 keeping every field; `collect` holds the nine empty maps.
- A v6 save with `secrets.bottle: true` has `collect.bottle['home:12'] === true` after load; without it, no key.
- Garbage keys and values (wrong pattern, a string value, `fish` 0 or 1000, an unknown kind) are dropped.
- Kebab-case fish ids survive: `collect.fish['fish:puddle-minnow'] = 3` round-trips through `normalize`; so do
  `collect.hollow['hollow_archwater_1']`, `collect.petal['hollow_archwater_1:p1']`, `collect.petal['act-juggling']`
  and `collect.petal['act-fishing']`. `collect.hollow['archwater:1']` and `collect.petal['act:juggling']` are dropped.
- `CFG.maxHp === 32`: a v9 save with `maxHp: 32` keeps 32; `maxHp: 40` normalises to 32; `maxHp: 22` stays 22.
- The smoke test Continues `v8-ch7.json` into the Nook region with no errors.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *One save slot* (bump + migration + tests from every older version + backup),
  *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/save.test.js` (new cases for v2-v8 → v9, the carry-over, the patterns including
`'fish:puddle-minnow'`, the realm-id hollow and petal keys, and the 32 heart cap); `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope

Any pickup, toast or UI for these kinds (S-072 to S-077).

#### Risk & rollback

A revert of a save bump strands v9 saves on an older build: `migrate` returns null for `v > SAVE_VERSION`. The
rollback is therefore a forward fix, never a revert, once the umbrella has merged; before the merge one revert is
safe. State this in the PR.

<a id="s-072"></a>

### S-072 · feat: heart petals make a heart per six, and the HUD shows up to 14 hearts in two rows

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-071 · Size: M · Save: none (the cap widened in S-071's v9 bump) · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#hearts](../bible/systems.md#hearts), [../bible/systems.md#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles),
[../bible/systems.md#brief-collect](../bible/systems.md#brief-collect)

#### Why

Hearts are the game's long progress line: 3 at the start, 14 at 100 %. Twenty-four heart petals (six to a heart) come
from Hollows, activities and two optional dungeons; the HUD needs a second row and a petal ring before the first
petal ships. Owner decision 3 (heart cap 14, two rows of 7; CANON §10.3, §15.3) is at its default: 16 hearts made
tier-2/3 combat harmless. If the owner has restored 16 in the vision's Owner decisions, use `PETALS_PER_HEART = 4`
and rows of 8 instead; everything else is the same. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-072
(M10); vision item **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. **Cap:** the `normalize` clamp is already 32 since S-071 (headroom inside the v9 bump, so the owner can restore 16
   hearts without another bump). The content cap is 14 hearts: `CFG.maxHp` stays the clamp, and `js/content/collect.js`
   exports `PETALS_PER_HEART = 6` and `HEART_GOAL = 14` (a unit test sums every heart source to exactly 28 HP).
2. **Petals** (`js/content/collect.js` + `js/interact.js`):
   - `givePetal(S, key)`: `collect.mark(S, 'petal', key)`; let `n = count(S, 'petal')`. If `n % 6 === 0`: apply the
     `heart` effect (`maxHp += 2` capped, `hp = maxHp`), toast "✿ Your max hearts grew by one!", `ui.bumpHearts()`;
     else toast "Heart petal {n % 6}/6" (for example "Heart petal 3/6").
   - The item-get beat for a petal: "You found a heart petal! Six petals make a whole heart." (one box), then the toast.
   - **World pickup kind `petal`** (`W.pickups` entry `{ kind: 'petal', key, x, y, z }`): a floating pink heart-shaped
     petal 0.6 m above ground (`col_petal` fallback below), spins 60°/s, bobs 0.1 m; walk within 0.8 m to take it.
     Cue: a bright chime within 8 m once per approach (SFX `petalChime`: `bell(t, 1319, 0.9, 0.5); bell(t + 0.1, 1760,
     1.1, 0.45)`) and a pink glint sprite (its visual twin).
   - `G.debug.collect.petal(key)` grants one (tests).
3. **HUD rows of 7** (`js/ui.js` `topFit`, `styles.css`):
   - Desktop: `const cols = room(7) >= full ? 7 : 4;` (was 10 / 5). The heart size stays the shipped `36px * u`
     (the bible's "22 px" is not adopted; the shipped size is kept so nothing shrinks).
   - Phones (`lay-land`, `lay-port`): `#hearts { --hw: 18px; }` and `grid-template-columns: repeat(var(--hcols), 18px)`
     with `--hcols = min(n, 7)` set by `fitTop` for every layout.
   - A second row starts at the 8th heart; the counters and the boss bar sit below the hearts block as `fitTop`
     already measures.
4. **Petal ring** (`#petalring`, right of the last heart, same row): a CSS six-petal flower (six rounded `div`s
   rotated 0/60/120/180/240/300°, six petals, 40 % of the heart size), petals filled `#ff9fc0` for
   `count(S, 'petal') % 6`, outlines
   `#e9d6b0` otherwise; hidden when the remainder is 0. It bumps (scale 1.4 → 1, 0.35 s) when a petal is taken.
5. **New in-page suite `collect`** (`js/debug/tests/collect.js`; CI matrix entry). This step's tests: rows for 3, 8,
   7, 8, 14 hearts; `hud.audit()` with 14 hearts and a boss bar at 1080p and 390 × 844; petal pickup, toast, the 6th
   petal adds a heart; the ring shows 1-5 petals.

#### Assets

`col_petal` is code-built (a heart-shaped extruded `Shape` 0.3 m, `#ff9fc0` with a `#fff0f5` rim emissive 0.4,
≤ 40 tris) with the brief of [../bible/systems.md#brief-collect](../bible/systems.md#brief-collect) pasted in the PR;
no image (the ring is CSS).

#### Acceptance criteria

- With 14 hearts the HUD shows two rows of 7 on desktop and on phones, with no overlap in `hud.audit()` at 1080p and
  390 × 844, boss bar shown.
- Six petals in a row give exactly one heart (+2 `maxHp`, full `hp`), with the toasts above ("Heart petal 3/6" after
  the third); a seventh shows "Heart petal 1/6".
- A unit test sums every heart source in the content tables (start 3, 4 shipped chests, Miso's, the Tower's, Kinako's,
  24 petals / 6) to exactly `HEART_GOAL` 14.
- `bun test tests/save.test.js`, `tests/shop.test.js` pass unchanged except for the new cap cases.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *One save slot* (no schema change here; the cap rode S-071's v9), *Tone*,
  *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (`givePetal` unit test); `node tests/inpage.mjs collect`; `node tests/inpage.mjs wp6`
(HUD suite); `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope

Where petals come from (S-073 Hollows and later steps); Kinako's heart container (S-092).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. The 32 clamp stays (it shipped with S-071's v9 bump), so no save loses hearts; the owner can switch to 16
hearts later by changing `PETALS_PER_HEART` to 4 and rows to 8 (no save change).

<a id="s-073"></a>

### S-073 · feat: Hollows: one-room puzzle caves behind spiral doors; two open on home

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-071, S-057, S-053 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h01](../bible/dungeons.md#h01),
[../bible/dungeons.md#h02](../bible/dungeons.md#h02), [../bible/atlas-home.md#hollows](../bible/atlas-home.md#hollows),
[../bible/systems.md#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry)

#### Why

Twenty-four Hollows hold 16 of the 24 heart petals and 8 gem bags: small, self-contained puzzles that reward a
look behind things. This step builds the realm kind and the two Lantern Hollows in the Woods fairy rings, so the
Lantern opens home finds the day it arrives (pacing rule 5). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-073 (M10); vision
item **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. **Realm kind `hollow`** (`js/realms/index.js`): the dungeon recipe with one 11 × 11-tile room in a 48 m grid, far
   120 m, fog 6-60 m, frozen time, no map/Chime Stone/keys, parent = its land (resident), arrival spawn `entry` on `@`.
   Files `js/realms/hollows/<realmId>.js`, maps in the fenced `map` format of
   [../bible/dungeons.md#format](../bible/dungeons.md#format) (S-049 compiles them).
2. **Prize entities:** `+` = a heart petal on a root pedestal (S-072's `petal` pickup with key `<realm>:p1`, R22);
   `*` = a 60-gem bag (`gems:+60`, "+60 gems!"). Taking the prize sets `collect.hollow['<realmId>'] = true` (R22) and
   drops the Hollow's puzzle state from `S.dungeons['<realmId>']` (kept there until solved). Hidden bridges `=` rise in
   1.0 s when the Hollow's condition holds and stay (except a live plate bridge, below).
3. **`hollow_home_1` "The Glow-Ring Hollow"** — the H01 map verbatim from
   [../bible/dungeons.md#h01](../bible/dungeons.md#h01). Dark. Four wall-lanterns (1, 6), (9, 6), (1, 8), (9, 8);
   kindling all four raises the bridge (5, 3)-(5, 4) to the petal (5, 1). Wave: two Dimlings spawned at tiles (3, 6) and
   (7, 6) on first entry. Hint ["Light the four lanterns.", "Kindle all four lanterns with the Glowbud Lantern; the
   root bridge grows to the petal."].
4. **`hollow_home_2` "The Moonring Hollow"** — the H02 map verbatim from
   [../bible/dungeons.md#h02](../bible/dungeons.md#h02). Dark; lanterns (1, 9), (9, 9) optional. Push block (3, 6):
   east × 4, then south × 2 onto the hold plate (7, 8): the bridge rises **while the plate is held** (live). Reset
   lever (9, 5). Hint ["The square plate wants the stone.", "Push the block four times east and twice south onto the
   square plate; walk the bridge to the petal."].
5. **Doors on home: `placeHollowDoors`** (home post-pass row 7, `new RNG(7151 * 89 + 31)`, R5, R29):
   - Entry 1 → `hollow_home_1`: the stump at the centre of the fairy ring (−16.9, −86.7). Entry 2 → `hollow_home_2`:
     the stump of the ring (23.6, −106.9). The ring at (−36.1, −44.8) gets nothing.
   - The door is a spiral-carved bark panel (0.9 × 1.1 m) on the stump's side facing the ring's game trail; the pass's
     only draw picks which of the two trail-facing quarters when both are clear. Spawns `hollow_1`, `hollow_2` on the
     ring floor 1.5 m in front of each panel.
   - States: when the **lit** Lantern is within 6 m the spiral glows (`#ffcf7a`); within 3 m the panel slides open in
     0.8 s and stays open (`gate:home:hollow1` / `gate:home:hollow2`). Then the portal `home:fairyring1` / `2`
     (`[E] Enter · Fairy ring`, needs `item:lantern` and the gate flag) is offered.
   - On entry a toast shows the Hollow's name ("✿ The Glow-Ring Hollow"); no name card. A solved Hollow's panel glows
     gold outside, and the map card shows a gold spiral at its door.
6. **Music:** register track `hollow` (A minor, 90 bpm, harp lead) in `TRACKS` from
   [../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data) verbatim; every Hollow plays it.
7. **Ask the Tree inside a Hollow** (no main step in reach there) uses the Hollow's own hint pair.
8. **Registry, prints, suites:** `REALMS.hollow_home_1/2` (`kind: 'hollow'`, `size: 48`, `parent: 'home'`, `tod`
   frozen 0.5, 4 `views`: entry looking north, prize looking south, the two near corners looking across);
   `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/hollow_home_1.json`, `…_2.json`; both in the realm suite.

#### Assets

- The door panel is code-built (`js/props.js`: a rounded bark `rbox` `#6e4526` with a carved spiral drawn as a canvas
  decal in `#c9a24a`, the glow an emissive copy of the decal); ART BRIEF `kit_spiral_door_stump` in the PR
  (silhouette: a bark panel set into a stump side with a two-and-a-half-turn spiral ending in an outward tick; 0.9 ×
  1.1 m; palette `#6e4526`, `#8a5a3c`, spiral `#c9a24a` / glow `#ffcf7a`; toon bark + emissive inlay; slides into the
  stump 0.8 s; ≤ 300 tris; 3D prompt "low-poly toon bark door panel set into a tree stump, carved glowing spiral, 300
  tris"). The shared `kit_spiral` decal image arrives in S-082b and replaces the canvas spiral by key.
- Hollow walls and floors reuse the dungeon kit and Under the Roots' root-wall textures (no new textures).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Both Hollows compile; the lock-and-key solver proves prize and exit reachable from `entry` with the Lantern, and
  H02's reset lever recovers every block position (no soft-lock).
- From a save with the Lantern, walking to each ring with the Lantern lit opens its panel; Enter, solve, take the
  petal: `collect.hollow` and `collect.petal` are set, the second petal shows "Heart petal 2/6".
- `placeHollowDoors` passes the four home-pass tests; world prints byte-identical.
- Realm suite: both Hollows within budget at their 4 views, both cameras; no leaks after enter/leave × 3.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Frozen realms* (new prints written), *Budgets per view*,
  *Assets*, *Tone* (Dimlings poof, never block a landing tile), *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

- `bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` (S-049: the two maps equal dungeons.md), `bun test` (solver, prints,
  world.test placeHollowDoors).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs collect` (add: open panel with the Lantern, enter, kindle four, bridge, petal, exit to
  `hollow_1`); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Isle Hollows and the stone `kit_hollow_door` (S-082b, S-093); Completion rows (S-074).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. New realms only; the home post-pass is additive.

<a id="s-074"></a>

### S-074 · feat: Completion v2: Field Notes groups rows by land from a generated stats file

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-071, S-037 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#completion](../bible/systems.md#completion), [../bible/systems.md#journal](../bible/systems.md#journal),
[../bible/art-audio.md#ui](../bible/art-audio.md#ui)

#### Why

The shipped tracker counts one island. At 100 hours the player needs a page that says, per land, what is left, and
the totals cannot come from the live world because only one realm is loaded at a time. Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-074 (M10, ENGINE F25); vision item **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. **`tools/realm-stats.mjs`** (node script, no browser): generates every realm in `REALMS` except kind `debug`
   (hidden realms included, so stats are ready when a land unhides) and writes `js/realms/stats.js`:

   ```js
   // js/realms/stats.js — generated by tools/realm-stats.mjs; do not edit
   export const STATS = {
     home: { chests: ['…'], chestGems: { '<id>': 15 }, keepsakes: ['acorn', 'stone', 'bottle', 'pouch'],
             acorns: [], hollows: ['hollow_home_1', 'hollow_home_2'], vistas: [], regions: ['0','1','2','3','4'],
             benches: [], toolFinds: [], bottles: ['home:12'], logs: [], petals: ['hollow_home_1:p1', 'hollow_home_2:p1'] },
     // one entry per land; dungeons, Hollows and variants roll up into their land (systems.md#completion)
   };
   ```
   Roll-ups: `under_roots`, `hollow_home_*`, `puddles_grotto`, `lighthouse` (and later `snowglobe`, `undercap`,
   `remembered`) into `home`. Each list holds the save ids exactly as the game stores them.
2. **`tests/stats.test.js`**: regenerates in memory and deep-compares with `js/realms/stats.js`; a stale file fails
   with the realm and list named. (Run `node tools/realm-stats.mjs` after any step that adds a find.)
3. **`completionV2(S, STATS, REALMS)`** in `js/completion.js` (pure). The shipped `COMPLETION_ROWS` and
   `completion(W, S)` stay unchanged (their tests keep passing).
   - **Land groups**, only for lands with `!REALMS[id].hidden`; a land with no `visited` key shows "Not yet visited"
     and still counts its totals. Rows (a row whose total is 0 is not shown and not counted): Chests (`S.chests`),
     Keepsakes (`S.secrets`), Acorns (`collect.acorn`), Hollows (`collect.hollow`), Vistas (`collect.vista`), Regions
     (`visited`), Benches (`travel` bench keys), Tool finds (home: `gate:`/find flags from `toolFinds`, see
     [../bible/atlas-home.md#finds](../bible/atlas-home.md#finds)); home also keeps Guardians and Errands (LOCKED rows).
   - **Game-wide rows**: Story (3 Starseeds placed + each `beacon:`/`stone:` beat whose land is unhidden +
     `mainEnding` once the Crown is unhidden), Hearts (heart containers and petals that exist: `maxHp` counted against
     the reachable total), Side stories, Activities, Plushies, Rematches, Dreams, Bestiary, Bottles, Lamp Logs, Fish —
     each counts only ids that exist in shipped (unhidden) content (R21).
   - `pct` = distinct things done over distinct things, floored, at most 99 until every shown row is full; `done` when
     every shown row is full.
4. **Field Notes Completion tab** (`js/hud/journal.js`): one collapsible group per land (land roundel from
   `icons_lands` once S-080 ships it, else a coloured dot), its rows as "Chests 9/14" with a row icon from
   `icons_collect` (glyph fallback), then a "Whole game" group; keyboard, pad and touch navigation as the other tabs.
5. **Tracker chip:** `js/hud/tracker.js` shows `completionV2(...).pct` (and "★ 100%" when `done`); the per-row chips
   stay the v6 rows.
6. **`tests/smoke.mjs` finished save** (line 182): build the "finished" save from `STATS` (every listed id set) plus
   the v6 fields, so "★ 100%" stays true as content grows.

#### Assets

`icons_collect` (`ui`, core, R25): kind `sheet-items` N = 16 generated at 2048 × 512 (two rows of 8), sliced, exported
at 120 px, packed with `tools/pack_atlas.py` into a 1024 × 256 webp ≤ 90 KB; cells and subjects exactly as
[../bible/art-audio.md#ui](../bible/art-audio.md#ui) "icons_collect" lists them (`acorn` … `dream`), palette
"#8aa83c #c98b4b #fdf6e3 #ffd98a". Fallback per cell: a glyph (`▣ ❖ ✉ ✎ ♥ ✐ ◓ ♡ ◎ ⚑ ⌂ ▣ ◈ ✺ ☼ ◡`). If
`tools/pack_atlas.py` does not exist yet, this step adds the minimal version (packs N same-size PNGs into a grid and
writes `{ cell: [x, y, w, h] }` JSON beside the webp).

#### Acceptance criteria

- `node tools/realm-stats.mjs` is deterministic (two runs, identical file); `bun test tests/stats.test.js` passes and
  fails on a hand-edited stats file.
- `completion.test.js` passes unchanged; new `tests/completion-v2.test.js` covers groups, hidden lands excluded,
  zero-total rows hidden, the 99 cap and `done`.
- The Completion tab opens and navigates with keyboard, pad and touch; `hud.audit()` clean at 1080p and 390 × 844.
- `test:smoke` shows "★ 100%" for the STATS-built finished save.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Hidden until whole* (hidden lands never listed), *Precache* (+≤ 90 KB core),
  *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`node tools/realm-stats.mjs`; `bun test tests/stats.test.js tests/completion.test.js tests/completion-v2.test.js`;
`node tests/inpage.mjs collect` (add: Completion tab navigation and audit); `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope

The land roundels (`icons_lands`, S-080); per-land progress rings on the chart (S-080).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert restores the v6 tracker percentage.

<a id="s-075a"></a>

### S-075a · feat: Lamp Logs and the Bottle Chain are collectibles with letters and a Lore home

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-071, S-038 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/lore.md#bottles](../bible/lore.md#bottles), [../bible/lore.md#bottle-table](../bible/lore.md#bottle-table),
[../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [../bible/lore.md#log-table](../bible/lore.md#log-table),
[../bible/systems.md#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles), [../bible/art-audio.md#ui](../bible/art-audio.md#ui)

#### Why

Twelve bottle letters (one per century) and thirty Lamp Log pages carry the game's history; chapter 22 needs eight
bottles. This step gives both kinds their pickup, cue, letter card, toast and Lore tab home, and makes the shipped
bottle Bottle 12. Split from S-075 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-075
(M10); vision item **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. **Data `js/content/letters.js`** (pure):
   - `BOTTLES`: the 12 rows of [../bible/lore.md#bottle-table](../bible/lore.md#bottle-table) (number, writer label,
     land, key `<realm>:<n>`, hand class, paper hex). Text ids `lore.bottle.<n>.head` / `.body` / `.sign`; only shipped
     letters have text (now: 12). Bottle 12's body is the LOCKED shipped text from `SECRETS.bottle`; its `.sign` is
     empty and the Lore tab shows its writer as "?" until `flag:trueEnding`, then "Elder Shellby, aged about ten".
   - `LOGS`: the 30 rows of [../bible/lore.md#log-table](../bible/lore.md#log-table) (number, title, keeper, land, key).
     Text ids `lore.log.<n>.title` / `.body` / `.sign`; no log text ships in this step (Duskwick's arrive with S-086b
     and S-091).
2. **The letter card** (frame `letter`, systems.md#dialogue): a centred paper card with `.head`, `.body`, `.sign`;
   bottles use `ui_letter_paper` tinted toward the bottle's paper hex, logs `ui_logpage`. **Hands** are CSS classes
   over system fonts, no font files (lore decision 26): `hand_shellby` (ink `#4a6a3a`, small, rounded, 4° upward slant
   via `transform: rotate(-4deg)` on the body block) ships now; every later hand is a row in
   `styles.css` `.hand_<id>` from the bottle table (ink, size, slant, letter-spacing). Logs use "Lamplighter print":
   ink `#3d2f22`, upright, the HUD font at 1.05×.
3. **Pickups** (`js/interact.js`):
   - `bottle`: a world interactable `[E] Open · A bottle` (the shipped verb and label) on the shipped bottle model
     (`col_bottle`), bobbing 0.08 m at the waterline, glinting every 2.5 s; a glassy clink within 15 m once per approach
     (SFX `bottleClink`: `bell(t, 2349, 0.4, 0.3); bell(t + 0.07, 2793, 0.5, 0.25)`). Opening marks
     `collect.bottle[key]`, shows the letter card, then toasts "✿ A letter in a bottle! (n/N)" (N from `STATS`, R21,
     R35).
   - The **shipped keepsake bottle** keeps its LOCKED flow ("...The paper inside is salty and curled." then the text,
     toast "Secret found n/4"); S-071's normalize marks `home:12`; no second toast.
   - `log`: a world interactable `[E] Read · Lamp Log` on a niche (the model arrives in S-082b; until then any
     `kit_log_niche` key falls back to a box niche); the niche's spiral chimes three soft notes within 8 m once per
     approach (SFX `logChime`: `bell(t, 784, 0.6, 0.3); bell(t + 0.15, 988, 0.6, 0.28); bell(t + 0.3, 1175, 0.8,
     0.26)`) with a faint spiral glow as its twin. Reading marks `collect.log[key]`, shows the card, toasts
     "✿ Lamp Log {number} (n/N)".
   - Items `log:1` and `log:2` (`kind: 'key'`, the pages Granny Wick hands over) also mark their `collect.log` keys
     when given.
   - `G.debug.collect.place(kind, n, x, z)` spawns a test pickup in the current realm (tests only).
4. **Lore tab sections** (`js/hud/journal.js`): **Bottles** (12 numbered slots; found ones show the writer label and
   open the card; unfound "Bottle n · ?"), **Lamp Logs** (30 numbered slots with titles once found; unfound
   "Lamp Log n"). A "Read again" button reopens the card.
5. **Flags:** none new (collect maps only).

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ui_letter_paper` (`ui`, core) | ship 768 × 1024 webp ≤ 90 KB | "A single sheet of old cream letter paper, soft deckled edges, faint water stain at one corner, a tiny spiral watermark in the centre, seen flat. Palette: #f6ecd2 #d9c49a #7fb6c9." (the `tile` suffix without "seamless") | `#f6ecd2` with a 2 px `#d9c49a` inset border |
| `ui_logpage` (`ui`, core) | ship 768 × 1024 webp ≤ 90 KB | "A single ruled logbook page with a brass-cornered binding edge on the left, faint moth-wing watermark in the centre, seen flat. Palette: #efe6d0 #c9a24a #7fb6c9." | `#efe6d0`, ruled lines `#d9cba8` every 28 px |

#### Acceptance criteria

- `G.debug.collect.place('bottle', 1, x, z)` in the sandbox: approach → clink; Open → card with the (test) text in its
  hand → toast with the right n/N → Lore tab lists it.
- The shipped bottle still plays its LOCKED lines and toast exactly; Lore → Bottles shows Bottle 12 with "?" as writer.
- A test log placed with `G.debug.collect.place('log', 4, …)` shows the print style and "✿ Lamp Log 4 (1/N)".
- `hud.audit()` clean with the letter card open at 1080p and 390 × 844; card text meets 4.5:1 contrast.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Tone* (LOCKED text untouched), *Precache* (+≤ 180 KB core), *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (letters table lint: 12 + 30 rows, keys match the v9 patterns, every shipped text id exists ≤ 450 / 560
characters per lore.md); `node tests/inpage.mjs collect` (add: bottle and log pickups, the shipped bottle unchanged,
Lore sections); `bun run test:playthrough` (the shipped keepsake flow).

#### Out of scope

The lighthouse shelf (S-075b); placing any bottle or log in a land (S-086b, S-091, S-106 and later).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. No content placed, so nothing can be lost.

<a id="s-075b"></a>

### S-075b · feat: Pinch's Lighthouse opens, with the Bottle Chain shelf in the lamp room

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-075a, S-054 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-home.md#lighthouse](../bible/atlas-home.md#lighthouse), [../bible/atlas-home.md#placePortals](../bible/atlas-home.md#placePortals),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors](../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors)

#### Why

Found bottles need a place to be seen again (CANON §10.3: every collectible has a home), and the lighthouse where the
shipped bottle waited a hundred years is that place. Split from S-075 (atlas-home decision 9). Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-075 (M10); vision item **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. **Interior realm `lighthouse`** (R28; `kind: 'interior'`, 48 m grid, parent `home`; card "Pinch's Lighthouse" /
   "Mind the stairs" on first entry), two rooms from [../bible/atlas-home.md#lighthouse](../bible/atlas-home.md#lighthouse)
   verbatim:

   ```
   l1 Keeper's Room     l2 Lamp Room
   ###w###              #######
   #.....#              #.....#
   #...^.#              #.....#
   #.....#              #..O..#
   #.....#              #.....#
   #..@..#              #.^...#
   ###X###              #######
   ```
   (`u` at l2 (0, 3) is plain wall until S-102 adds the Lookout door.) `^` (4, 2) ↔ l2 `^` (2, 5) with a fade.
   `X` → `lighthouse_door`. l1: Pinch's old hammock, a ship in a bottle on a shelf, a coil of rope, using the
   `cottage_kit` pieces from `js/props-interior-home.js`; `Look` lines: hammock `home.lh.hammock` "A hammock with a
   crab-shaped dent in it. Pinch naps here between shifts."; ship `home.lh.ship` "A tiny ship in a bottle. The ship has
   a tiny crab on it. Of course it does." l2: the great lamp `O` (solid), its `glow` following the outside lamp state
   (on at night; on by day until `nellHome`, S-011).
2. **The bottle shelf** on l2's north wall: 12 slots in two rows of six (slot n at row `n <= 6 ? 0 : 1`, column
   `(n − 1) % 6`), each a small shelf niche; a slot shows the bottle model, tinted by the bottle table's paper hex,
   when `collect.bottle` holds a key with that number; `[E] Read · Bottle n` reopens its letter card. Slot 12 holds
   the shipped bottle once `home:12` is set. Unfound slots show nothing.
3. **Door:** `placePortals` entry 7 (append; it draws no random numbers, so entry 9's draws are unchanged):
   `home:lighthouse` at the lighthouse door, model-space z 1.52 → world (8.5, 118.7), facing south;
   `[E] Enter · Pinch's Lighthouse`, needs `flag:lanternGot` (R30); spawn `lighthouse_door` 1.5 m south on walkable
   sand. Before `lanternGot` the door shows nothing new (the shipped look).
4. **Music and light:** the `nook` interior track (S-054), the shipped 4-light pool (two lamps); palette
   `home_interior` (atlas-home §6).
5. **Registry, prints, suites:** `REALMS.lighthouse` with 4 views; `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/lighthouse.json`;
   realm suite entry.

#### Assets

ART BRIEF `lighthouse_inside` from [../bible/atlas-home.md#lighthouse](../bible/atlas-home.md#lighthouse) pasted in the
PR (code-built from the interior kit; set ≤ 4,500 tris). No images.

#### Acceptance criteria

- With `lanternGot`, the lighthouse door offers Enter; l1 and l2 load, the stair fades between them, `X` returns to
  `lighthouse_door` in < 100 ms (resident parent).
- A save with `collect.bottle['home:12']` shows one bottle in slot 12; adding `duskwick:1` (debug) shows slot 1;
  Read reopens each letter.
- `placePortals` still passes its home-pass tests; world prints byte-identical; the interior's prints are written.
- Realm suite: the interior within budget (< 120 total calls per room).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Frozen realms*, *Budgets per view*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (prints, compiler, `placePortals` tests); `node tests/inpage.mjs collect` (add: enter, shelf slots, read);
`node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Pinch's Lookout and the glide launch (S-102); the 13th letter (S-290).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Additive interior and portal.

<a id="s-076"></a>

### S-076 · feat: Vistas: sketch Hoot's spots and pin them to Hoot's Board

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-071, S-074 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-home.md#vistas](../bible/atlas-home.md#vistas), [../bible/systems.md#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-vista](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-vista), [../bible/art-audio.md#home](../bible/art-audio.md#home),
[../bible/side-content.md#act-vistas](../bible/side-content.md#act-vistas)

#### Why

Thirty vistas reward climbing to a view and looking: the land as the progress bar. Each sketch becomes a painting on
Hoot's Board in the plaza, so the village shows how far Pip has been. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-076
(M10); vision item **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. **`placeVistas`** (home post-pass row 8, `new RNG(7151 * 97 + 37)`):
   - **Hoot's Board** first: a corkboard on two posts 2.2 × 1.4 m (`solid` box 2.2 × 0.4), at the plaza edge on
     bearing 300° from (0, 0), r 13-17 (nearest valid); `[E] Read · Hoot's Board`.
   - Vista posts (`kit_vista`, R13), ids and placements exactly as atlas-home: `home:v1` "The Isle from the Bridge"
     `near(Peaks bridge east end (54.7, 2.2) + 3 m east, 0, 4)`, looking at the Great Tree, pitch +6°;
     `home:v2` "The Lighthouse from Islet 1" `near(islet 1 (−40, 139), 0, 5)`, at Pinch's Lighthouse, pitch +8°;
     `home:v3` "Where the River Starts" `near(source pond (37, −78) south edge, 6, 9)`, down the river toward the Peaks
     bridge, pitch −4°. `home:v4` "From Wenna's Crown" is data only (its post comes with the Crown Balcony, S-248).
2. **Sketching:** standing within 1.5 m of a post with the view inside ±20° yaw and ±15° pitch of its target offers
   `[E] Sketch · <vista name>` (the `Sketch · {view}` prompt of systems.md#collectibles). Sketching plays a 1.2 s pencil-scratch sound (SFX
   `sketch`: `N({ t, d: 1.2, v: 0.04, type: 'bandpass', f: 3200, f1: 2600 })`) with a paper-card flourish in the
   lower right, marks `collect.vista[id]`, applies `gems:+5` (first sketch of that id only), shows the sketch
   (512 × 288) in a small card for 3 s, toasts "✿ Sketched: {name} (n/N) · +5 gems" (N = vistas with a placed post,
   R21; 3 now). The post's blank card shows the sketch
   thumbnail afterwards.
3. **Hoot's Board** shows 30 slots (6 × 5) on a cork texture; found sketches pinned in id order, each with Hoot's
   caption below (`home.vista.v<n>.caption`, new text in Hoot's voice, ≤ 100 characters):
   v1 "The village from the bridge. The Tree is the large green thing. I am fairly sure." ·
   v2 "The lighthouse from the islet. Stripes: many. Crabs: one, waving." ·
   v3 "Where the river starts. It is very small here. Rivers, like owls, start small." ·
   v4 "From the Crown. Everything is tiny and I am, for once, tall." The board's title line counts "n of 30".
4. **Field Notes:** the Lore tab gains "Hoot's Sketchbook" listing sketches; a Hoot margin note unlocks at 10, 20 and
   30 vistas (`home.hoot.sketch.10` "Ten sketches! I have started a second sketchbook. It is mostly owls." ·
   `.20` "Twenty! My board is full of places I have never been. I have been to none of them." · `.30` "Thirty. Every
   view in the Wide Blue. I shall frame them. And then I shall frame the frames.").
5. **Tiers** ([../bible/side-content.md#act-vistas](../bible/side-content.md#act-vistas)), generic so every land's
   finds step pays them with no code: each new sketch pays `gems:+5` (item 2; 30 × 5 = 150 gems, inside CANON §10.4's
   1,000 activity gems); at `collect:vista>=10`, `>=20` and `>=30` a Lore page unlocks under "Hoot's Sketchbook" (flags
   `vista:ch1`..`vista:ch3`, family registered in `js/content/flags.js`), each one text id in Hoot's prose:
   `lore.vista.ch1` "I. Near Shores": "Chapter I: Near Shores. Ten views, all within a good flap of home. A view, I have decided, is a place that agrees to hold still while you look at it." ·
   `lore.vista.ch2` "II. High Places": "Chapter II: High Places. Twenty views. Pip climbs; I stay below and imagine. From up high, the paths must look like doodles. I suspect the Lamplighters doodled them." ·
   `lore.vista.ch3` "III. Far Lights": "Chapter III: Far Lights. Thirty views, and a small light far off in every one: a beacon, a window, a star. The Wide Blue is mostly water, Pip. Lights hold it together."
   The page unlock toasts "✿ A new page in Hoot's Sketchbook!". The all-30 framed print is S-302a's
   `nook_gift_print`, not this step. Unit test: a fake save with 9 → 10 sketches unlocks `vista:ch1` once and pays
   5 gems for the 10th sketch only.
6. **`STATS`:** `vistas` lists for home (`home:v1`-`v3`); rerun `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject (after preamble + `sketch` suffix) | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sketch_home_1` (`ui`, core) | ship 512 × 288 webp ≤ 45 KB | "A small island village seen from a wooden bridge, a giant friendly tree in the middle, a tiny owl doodle in the corner. Palette: #f4e9c4 #8aa83c #5aaef2 #f6a6c0." | cream card with the vista's name in the HUD font |
| `sketch_home_2` | same | "A striped cream-and-coral lighthouse on a beach seen from a tiny islet. Palette: #f4e9c4 #8aa83c #5aaef2 #f6a6c0." | same |
| `sketch_home_3` | same | "A small forest pond where a stream begins and winds away toward snowy hills. Palette: #f4e9c4 #8aa83c #5aaef2 #f6a6c0." | same |
| `sketch_home_4` | same | "Looking down from high in a great tree over a flower meadow with a windmill and the sea beyond. Palette: #f4e9c4 #8aa83c #5aaef2 #f6a6c0." | same |
| `prop_home_hoots_board` texture (`textures`, core) | 512 × 256 webp ≤ 40 KB | `tile`-like: "A cork pinboard surface with a few brass pins and paper corners, flat." | flat `#c98b4b` |

Code-built: `kit_vista` (a 1.3 m wooden easel `#9b7b56`, a card `#f4efe2`, an owl-face stamp `#7a5a3e` / `#e8c46a`,
≤ 160 tris) and Hoot's Board (posts, a little roof, cork face, brass owl knob, ≤ 300 tris); briefs `kit_vista`
([../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-vista](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-vista)) and `hoots_board`
([../bible/atlas-home.md#vistas](../bible/atlas-home.md#vistas)) pasted in the PR.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `placeVistas` passes the four home-pass tests; each post is reachable and its target lies in view (a heightfield
  ray from 1.5 m at the post reaches the target's top); world prints byte-identical.
- Sketching each of v1-v3 marks it once, pays 5 gems once, shows its sketch and toast "(n/3)"; Hoot's Board lists
  them with captions; re-sketching pays nothing.
- The tier pages unlock at 10/20/30 sketches (unit test with fake saves) and list in the Lore tab.
- The sketch prompt appears only inside the yaw/pitch window (in-page test turns 25° away: no prompt).
- `STATS.home.vistas` equals the placed posts; `tests/stats.test.js` passes.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Precache* (+≤ 220 KB core), *Assets*, *Original IP only*,
  *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/world.test.js tests/stats.test.js`; `node tests/inpage.mjs collect` (add: sketch v1 at its post,
board slots, 25° away no prompt); `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope

Isle vistas (each land's finds step; they pay the tiers through this step's generic code); the all-30 print
(S-302a); v4's post (S-248).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Additive pass.

<a id="s-077"></a>

### S-077 · feat: Puddle's Grotto under the well (side story 7)

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-073, S-054, S-068a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/side-content.md#ss-07](../bible/side-content.md#ss-07), [../bible/atlas-home.md#puddles-grotto](../bible/atlas-home.md#puddles-grotto),
[../bible/cast.md#puddle](../bible/cast.md#puddle), [../bible/side-content.md#data-shape](../bible/side-content.md#data-shape)

#### Why

The well frog has waved at every player since day one. The Lantern now lets Pip go down to his dark little grotto,
light it, and find his lily pad: the first side story of the expansion and a promise kept from the shipped text.
Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-077 (M10); vision item **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. **Interior realm `puddles_grotto`** (R28; `kind: 'interior'`, 48 m grid, parent `home`, card "Puddle's Grotto" /
   "Drip, drip, splash"), three rooms from [../bible/atlas-home.md#puddles-grotto](../bible/atlas-home.md#puddles-grotto)
   verbatim (g1 at [0, 0], g2 at [−1, 6], g3 at [0, 14]). Every room dark (ambient 0.25) until lit; the Lantern
   auto-lights.
   - g1: `X` (3, 1) `[E] Climb · Bucket rope` back to the `well` spawn; `D` (3, 5) a curtain of sleeping glowbuds that
     opens for good when the lit Lantern comes within 3 m; two wall lanterns `t` to `Kindle`.
   - g2: pond rows are water (stepping in splashes and puts Pip back at the north door, no harm); `S` = shy stones,
     under water until **both** g2 lanterns are kindled, then rise 0.6 s and stay; `c` (1, 5) = Puddle's lost lily pad
     (`[E] Pick up · Lily pad`, key item `lily_pad`). Two Dimlings in g2 only (R6), drifting over the pond's north
     half; the rest of the grotto is a safe zone.
   - g3: `P` Puddle on his mushroom stool by the pool (his face at 0.5 m); `c` (3, 4) is **Puddle's treasure box**, a
     curio, no reward (R6): `[E] Look · Puddle's treasure box` → `home.grotto.box` "Puddle's treasures: one shiny
     pebble, a beetle wing, and a very small spoon."
2. **The well** (`js/interact.js` `well` case, line 138): before `lanternGot` the shipped LOCKED Peek lines play
   unchanged. With the Lantern: `[E] Peek · Well` → `side.ss07.peek` "Puddle waves. Then he points down, very
   seriously." and a bucket rope appears; from then the well offers `[E] Enter · The well` (portal `home:well`, needs
   `flag:ss07.peeked`; R28). At night before ss-07 is done the Peek line is `side.ss07.peek.night` "The lily pad is
   empty. A tiny light glows far below."
3. **Quest `ss-07`** (`js/content/quests/home.js`, n 107, kind `side`, owner `puddle`, unlock `flag:lanternGot`,
   started by the Peek): steps

   | Id | Text | Done | On |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `peek` | Peek into the well with the Lantern | `flag:ss07.peeked` | `TALK_END` |
   | `light` | Light Puddle's grotto (n/4) | `flag:ss07.lamps>=4` (counter of the four `t` kindled) | `PUZZLE` |
   | `pad` | Find Puddle's lily pad | `item:lily_pad` | `ITEM_GET` |
   | `return` | Give Puddle his lily pad | `flag:ss07.done` | `TALK_END` |

   Reward `['gems:+30', 'take:lily_pad', 'set:ss07.done']`. Journal (Hoot's voice): open "A frog lives in the well. The
   frog waves. I have written a short paper on the wave." / done "The well is lit. The frog croaked. My paper on the
   wave is now a paper on the croak."
4. **Puddle's lines** (R27; speaker "Puddle"; every line starts "(tiny voice)"):
   `puddle.1` "(tiny voice) Oh! Hello. You waved back, once. ...Nobody waves back." ·
   `puddle.2` "(tiny voice) It's very dark down here. Uncle Ribbit says frogs don't mind the dark. Uncle Ribbit sleeps
   with a lamp." · `puddle.3` (on `[E] Give · Lily pad`) "(tiny voice) My lily pad! It's the best one. It's got a notch
   where I sit. ...Thank you, Pip. ...RIBBIT. Oh. That was loud." Ribbit afterwards (priority 40, once):
   `ribbit.1` "My nephew croaked! A real croak! He gets it from me. Croak. ...Don't tell him it took me forty years."
5. **World mark** (within 10 s of the last step): the four lanterns stay lit; Puddle's lily pad sits under a shaft of
   light from the well (an additive cone `#fff4d6`); `[E] Peek · Well` now shows `side.ss07.peek.done` "Puddle is
   sitting on his favourite lily pad, in the light. He waves twice."; the well's water glows faintly gold at night (an
   emissive disc `#ffe9a8` at 0.4 inside the well, home overworld).
6. **Puddle model** (`js/content/looks.js`): `puddle: { rig: 'frog', h: 0.12, pal: { skin: '#9bd46a', belly:
   '#f4e8b0', cap: '#8a5a30', pad: '#3f8f5a', flower: '#f7b6c8' }, acc: ['acornCap', 'lilyPad'] }` — the shipped frog
   case scaled 0.1 with no coat or top hat, an acorn cap (half `sphere` + `cyl` stem), a lily pad (flat `cyl` with a
   wedge cut, 0.4 m) and a pink flower. Sound cue: a tiny *plip* from the well every 20 s (SFX `plip`:
   `T({ t, f: 1800, f1: 900, d: 0.08, v: 0.05 })`) with a ripple ring on the well water as its twin.
7. **Charter:** add Puddle's "(tiny voice)" marker to the reserved-markers table.
8. **Registry, prints, suites:** `REALMS.puddles_grotto` (4 views), prints fixture, realm suite entry; solver: exit,
   pad and Puddle reachable from the entry with the Lantern; `STATS` regenerated.

#### Assets

- `portrait_puddle` (`ui`, core, 512² webp ≤ 60 KB): subject "A tiny bright green frog wearing an acorn cap as a hat,
  sitting on a round lily pad with a pink flower, waving one little hand, very proud, big shiny eyes. Palette: #9bd46a
  #f4e8b0 #8a5a30 #3f8f5a." Fallback: name only.
- Code-built grotto set from `js/props-interior-home.js` plus three pieces (glowbud curtain, shy stone with a sleepy
  face, lily pad); ART BRIEF `grotto_kit` from atlas-home pasted in the PR; `npc_puddle` brief from cast.md.

#### Acceptance criteria

- Before `lanternGot` the well behaves exactly as shipped (`test:playthrough` unchanged).
- With the Lantern: Peek → Enter → light four lanterns (the shy stones rise after g2's two) → pad → Give: quest done,
  +30 gems, the world mark present within 10 s, Ribbit's line once.
- Dimlings spawn only in g2 and poof; g1 and g3 never spawn an enemy.
- Puddle's lines pass the lint, including the reserved "(tiny voice)" marker; exactly one line contains "RIBBIT".
- The economy is unchanged except ss-07's 30 gems (no heart petal anywhere in the grotto).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Frozen realms*, *Tone* (no peril to a child; nothing
  missable), *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (quest lint, solver, prints, stats); `node tests/inpage.mjs collect` (add: the full ss-07 path with
`G.debug` warps; the night Peek line); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope

Puddle's sunny-afternoon perch on the well rim (S-174 schedules).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Additive realm and quest.

<a id="s-078"></a>

### S-078 · test: the economy ledger sums content tables and keeps sources ≥ 105 % of sinks

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-074, S-076 · Size: S · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#economy](../bible/systems.md#economy), [../bible/side-content.md#index](../bible/side-content.md#index),
[../bible/side-content.md#act-vistas](../bible/side-content.md#act-vistas)

#### Why

Gems must stay meaningful across 100 hours: every one-time purchase reachable, nothing trivial. The ledger becomes code
so every later step that adds a chest, a story or a shop item is checked automatically. Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-078 (M10); vision item **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**.

#### What

1. **`js/content/economy.js`** (pure), derived, never hand-totalled:
   - **Shipped sources:** chest gems from `STATS[*].chestGems` (overworld, caches, dungeon chests), keepsakes × 25
     (`STATS[*].keepsakes`), errands (`ERRANDS` rewards), side-story `reward` `gems:+n` from `QUESTS`, activity tiers,
     rematch first wins (`REMATCH` table), Hollow gem bags (`*` prizes), Dream first clears, and vista sketches
     (5 × the shipped vista posts in `STATS[*].vistas`, S-076; 15 at M10).
   - **Shipped one-time sinks:** `SHOP` (heart 120, sword 150), every plushie price, every other seller's one-time
     items (from S-092 on, Kinako's table), Village Works, upgrades.
   - **Planned ledger** constant `PLANNED` copied from [../bible/systems.md#economy](../bible/systems.md#economy) plus
     R24's home tool finds: sources 10,925 + 555 = **11,480** (96 gem chests × 30 after the 24 prize chests), sinks
     **9,590** (16 bought furnishings, no hood-trim sales) (each row an object `{ id, count, each, total }`).
   - **`js/content/prizes.js`** (pure data, new here): `CHEST_PRIZES`, the 24 rows of the prize-chest table in
     [../bible/systems.md#economy](../bible/systems.md#economy), each `{ chest: '<realm>:c<n>', item, kind:
     'furnishing' | 'trim' | 'sticker', name, line }` (for example `{ chest: 'duskwick:c4', item: 'nook:lantern-string',
     kind: 'furnishing', name: 'Wickhaven Lantern String', line: 'Moth-made! Every lantern hand-folded.' }`). The
     planned ledger subtracts 30 gems per row (−720) and counts the 8 furnishings and 8 trims as found, not sold.
2. **`tests/economy.test.js`**: (1) planned sources ≥ 1.05 × planned sinks (11,480 / 9,590 = 119.7 %); (1b)
   `CHEST_PRIZES` has exactly 24 rows, three per Act II-III land (one furnishing, one trim, one sticker), no chest id
   twice, and every furnishing id is one of side-content.md#nook-furnishings #13-#20; (2) shipped
   content (R41): fixed sources plus drops at 15 gems per shipped hour (`HOURS_SHIPPED` from INDEX's cumulative
   column, 8.0 at M10) against shipped one-time sinks whose stock condition can hold: **fail below 0.9, warn below
   1.05** (today: 150 + 100 + 100 + 120 + 60 + 120 + 60 + 30 = 740, + 15 vista gems + 120 drops = 875 against
   510 + 100 = 610, 143 %;
   the test prints the numbers); (3) per act, warn only: a `console.warn` line when the sources reachable by
   the end of an act are < 90 % of the sinks unlocked by then; (4) no single one-time price > 1,000; (5) every shop
   item has a price, a stock condition and a description ≤ 120 characters.
3. `docs/DESIGN.md`'s economy table points at the test instead of hand numbers.

#### Assets

None.

#### Acceptance criteria

`bun test tests/economy.test.js` passes and prints the shipped and planned ratios; doubling a sink price in a scratch
edit makes it fail (shown in the PR, reverted).

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/economy.test.js`.

#### Out of scope

New prices or rewards.

#### Risk & rollback

Test-only; one revert.

<a id="s-078b"></a>

### S-078b · test: a playtime model sums modelled hours from the content tables

Milestone M10 · Depends on: S-078, S-049, S-036 · Size: M (about 420 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness), [../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone),
[../bible/side-content.md#index](../bible/side-content.md#index), [../bible/dungeons.md](../bible/dungeons.md)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): nothing measured playtime, so the owner's core ask (80-120 h)
was never verified and close-outs copied budget numbers.

#### Why

Part of **Collections, hearts and the Completion page**. The game must really take 80-120 hours, and the only honest
way to know before playtesters is to count what the content tables contain. This step turns CANON §11's per-beat model
into code, so every later step that adds a room, a quest step, a scene or a side story moves the modelled total, and
every close-out records a modelled number instead of a hoped-for one.

#### What

1. **`tools/hours.mjs`** (pure ES module, no DOM, no Three.js; also runnable: `node tools/hours.mjs [--json]` prints the
   table below). It imports only content data (`js/content/**`, `js/realms/index.js` `REALMS`, `js/realms/stats.js`,
   the dungeon map texts through S-049's compiler, `js/content/scenes/**`, `js/content/quests/**`) and exports
   `model({ planned = false } = {}) → { buckets: { [bucket]: minutes }, acts: { [act]: minutes }, rows: [...] }`.
   Constants (minutes; CANON §11, exported as `RATES` so tests can read them):

   | Content | Rate |
   |---|---|
   | simple puzzle beat (a single entity solve: one switch, one block, one plate, one key door) | 3 |
   | compound puzzle beat (two mechanics interacting, or a multi-step sequence; tagged `compound: true` in the room entity table, else detected: ≥ 2 entity kinds that reference each other's ids) | 6 |
   | combat wave (one spawn group in a room or a scripted wave) | 1.5 |
   | story boss with one retry | 4.5 |
   | act-closer boss (`closer: true` in the boss def) | 7 |
   | room traverse and pickups (per dungeon room) | 1 |
   | scene | its scripted seconds × 1.2 / 60 |
   | overworld quest step | 5 + (path length from the previous step's target to this one ÷ 3.5 m/s ÷ 60), capped at 10, plus 1 per `TALK_END` step |
   | `before` sub-objective (set piece, S-091b) | its own `minutes` field (15-25) |
   | side story | its `minutes` field (side-content#index) |
   | activity | 30 per activity to top tier (fishing and vistas use their rows in CANON §11) |
   | off-path find (each chest, keepsake, acorn, vista, bottle, Lamp Log, Hollow door not on a main-quest target) | 2.5 |

   - **Dungeons**: for each map block, count rooms, beats (from the room entity tables: each `D` locked door, `L`, `P`,
     switch, block, `o`, `Q` and the like counts once; compound tags as above), spawn groups and the boss.
   - **Buckets** exactly as CANON §11: main story (per act: I, Interlude, II, III, IV), side stories, optional dungeons
     and Hollows, exploration, collectible hunting, activities, post-game.
   - **`planned: true`** adds the rows of a constant `PLANNED_HOURS` (one row per not-yet-built step from INDEX,
     with its CANON §11 bucket and its modelled minutes as the bible specifies them: rooms × beats for dungeons,
     quest steps × the rate, scene seconds), so the full game can be modelled before it is built. Each built step
     deletes its row from `PLANNED_HOURS` in its own PR (the test below fails if a realm, quest or dungeon is both built
     and planned).
2. **`tests/hours.test.js`**:
   - (a) **per act**: the modelled main path of each act in the planned model is within ±20 % of CANON §11 (Act I 4.0,
     Interlude 1.25, Act II 13.5, Act III 17.25, Act IV 4.0 hours); a failure prints the act, both numbers and the
     five largest rows.
   - (b) **total**: the full planned model at 100 % is ≥ 90 h. A failure prints each bucket.
   - (c) **built vs planned** never double-counts an id.
   - (d) **rates**: a fixture dungeon (the S-049 test dungeon) models to a hand-computed number (state the arithmetic in
     the test).
3. **The index columns.** Add `docs/roadmap/hours.json` (written by `node tools/hours.mjs --json >
   docs/roadmap/hours.json`) shaped `{"milestones": {"M01": <modelled hours added>, ...}}`, holding modelled hours per
   milestone (built content attributed to the milestone of the step that built it; planned rows by their step's
   milestone). `docs/roadmap/tools/build-index.py` already reads `../hours.json` when it exists: run
   `sh docs/roadmap/tools/build-step-index.sh` in this PR, so `INDEX.md`'s overview columns "Play added" and
   "Cumulative play" come from the model and CANON's budget moves to a third column "Budget (CANON §11)". Update the
   same two columns in `docs/roadmap/README.md`'s milestone overview by hand from the regenerated INDEX.md, since §6 of
   INDEX already says hours are modelled. `tests/docs.test.js` gains a check that INDEX.md's Cumulative column equals
   `hours.json` (± 0.1 h).
4. **Close-out hook.** Add to `docs/roadmap/README.md`'s close-out checklist (and `charter.md#close-out-milestone` item 7
   already says so): "run `node tools/hours.mjs` and record the modelled hours per bucket in progress.md".
5. **Debug** (CLI only): `node tools/hours.mjs --act II` prints Act II's rows; `--bucket side` prints side stories.

#### Assets

None.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `node tools/hours.mjs` prints the seven buckets, the five acts and the 100 % total for the built game and (with
  `--planned`) the full game.
- `tests/hours.test.js` passes on this branch: every act within ±20 % of CANON §11 and the planned total ≥ 90 h; it
  fails (show it in the PR) when one planned dungeon's rooms are halved.
- INDEX's overview play columns come from `docs/roadmap/hours.json`; the docs test checks them.
- The test needs no browser and runs in under 2 s.
- Invariants: *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/hours.test.js tests/docs.test.js && bun test
node tools/hours.mjs && node tools/hours.mjs --planned
```

#### Out of scope

Raising content if the model comes in low (CANON §11's two levers: an optional mini-dungeon per Act II land and
2-room Dreams; filed as new steps by the plan job if `tests/hours.test.js` fails after a land ships). Measuring real
players.

#### Risk & rollback

Test and tooling only. Risk: the model and CANON disagree from the start; then the PR records the modelled numbers in
the vision Log and the owner decides whether to add content or accept the hours. Rollback: revert the PR.
