<a id="s-080"></a>

### S-080 · feat: per-land map card, the Lamplighter's Chart, benches and fast travel

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-021, S-026 · Size: M · Save: none (uses `S.travel`, `S.flags`) · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#travel](../bible/systems.md#travel), [../bible/systems.md#chart](../bible/systems.md#chart),
[../bible/systems.md#benches](../bible/systems.md#benches), [../bible/systems.md#dizzy-rules](../bible/systems.md#dizzy-rules),
[../bible/systems.md#brief-chart](../bible/systems.md#brief-chart), [../bible/art-audio.md#ui](../bible/art-audio.md#ui)

#### Why

A 100-hour world of eleven lands needs a way to get around inside a land and a map of the whole sea. This is ENGINE
F24: Lamplighter benches as waystones, a travel menu, fast travel from the map card, and the map card's "This land" /
"World" tabs with the painted chart. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-080 (M11 Travel and the Duskwick
land); vision item **Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **Pure `js/travel.js`:**
   - `benchKey(realm, n)` → `'bench:<realm>:b<n>'` (R4, R8); `foundBenches(S, realm, W)` → found `W.waystones`
     entries in id order.
   - `travelMenu(S, realm, W)` → `[{ id, label, x, z, facing }]`: found benches labelled `'<Region card name> · <bench
     name>'`, plus "The Great Tree" (home, spawn `tree` (0, 13)) or "The jetty" (any other overworld, spawn `jetty`).
     After `flag:mainEnding` it adds an "Other lands" group of every found bench of every land (dormant now).
   - `canTravel(game)` → false during a boss fight or intro, a scene, a modal other than the map, while airborne or
     swimming, aboard the boat, in a realm of kind `dungeon`, `hollow`, `interior`, `variant` or `crown`, or when the
     scene runner's `calm` test fails (an enemy within 24 m).
2. **Benches** (interactable kind `bench` built from `W.waystones = [{ id: 'b1', name, region, x, z, facing }]`):
   `[E] Sit · Bench`. Sitting: Pip sits (FP eye lowers 0.3 m; TP shows the sit pose), `hp = maxHp` (potions untouched),
   autosave, and the first time `travel[benchKey] = true`, toast "✿ Bench found: {name}" and the bench's marked state
   (S-082a's inlay fill). Outside home, sitting also sets `flag:wake:<realm> = n` (family `wake:*`, kind `choice`,
   rewritable; register it): getting dizzy in that land wakes Pip at that bench (else at the arrival spawn); home always
   wakes Pip by the Great Tree (LOCKED). While seated, a small travel panel lists `travelMenu()`; {interact} on an entry
   travels, Esc / B stands up.
3. **Fast travel** within a land: a 1.0 s leaf-swirl fade (a full-screen CSS overlay of drifting leaf glyphs on
   `#f4e9c4`), then Pip stands 1.2 m in front of the chosen bench facing its `facing`; no realm swap; an autosave.
4. **Map card tabs** (`js/hud/nav.js` `MapCard`, `js/ui.js`): the shipped card becomes the **This land** tab (title
   "Starseed Isle" stays for home; other lands show their name); found benches show as a spiral marker
   (`icons_hud` `marker_bench`, glyph `@` fallback) that can be selected, then a "Travel" button (disabled with the
   reason when `canTravel` is false: "Not while something's chasing you." / "Not from in here."). The **World** tab is
   S-068b's `ChartCard` view inside the card; in chart mode (`item:chart`) it draws `ui_chart_world` under the nodes
   (canvas fallback otherwise), a land roundel from `icons_lands` on each listed node, and a progress ring for each
   visited land (`completionV2` land group, S-074). Tab switching: Q/E keys or clicking the tab; pad LB/RB (buttons
   4/5) while the card is open (the card already opens on Back, button 8); touch taps the tab.
5. **Sandbox test realm:** `js/realms/recipes/sandbox.js` gains two waystones `b1` (−20, 0) "Sandbox · West Bench" and
   `b2` (20, 0) "Sandbox · East Bench" (debug realm only).
6. **New in-page suite `travel`** (`js/debug/tests/travel.js`; CI matrix entry): sit at b1 (hp restored, key set,
   toast), sit at b2, open the map card, This land → b1 → Travel (Pip within 1.5 m of b1's front, facing its
   `facing`), a spawned enemy within 10 m disables Travel, the World tab switches with LB/RB and Q/E, `hud.audit()` at
   1080p and 390 × 844 for both tabs.
7. **Flags:** register `wake:*`.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject / source | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ui_chart_world` (`ui`, core, lazy) | `map` · generate 1792 × 1024, ship 1600 × 1000 opaque webp q80 ≤ 200 KB (R20) | the prompt of [../bible/systems.md#brief-chart](../bible/systems.md#brief-chart) verbatim ("An old hand-painted sea chart on parchment, a small island with a great tree in the middle, little painted islands around it …, a compass rose made of a spiral, no text") | the canvas chart of S-068b |
| `icons_lands` (`ui`, core) | `sheet-items` N = 16 → 1024 × 256 webp ≤ 80 KB, cells as [../bible/art-audio.md#ui](../bible/art-audio.md#ui) "icons_lands" (`home` … `margins`) | as listed there | a coloured dot per land |
| `icons_hud` (`ui`, core) | `sheet-items` N = 16 → 1024 × 256 webp ≤ 70 KB, cells as art-audio "icons_hud" (`chime` … `fog`) | as listed there | glyphs |

**Chart alignment check:** after generating `ui_chart_world`, overlay the node table on it (a 10-line Pillow script in
the job's scratch folder, output attached to the PR) and regenerate until each near isle (Duskwick, Pinewhistle,
Archwater, Threemills) is painted within 60 px of its node; if three tries fail, ship the canvas fallback and note it.

#### Acceptance criteria

- In the sandbox, benches mark, heal and save; fast travel lands Pip on walkable ground in front of the bench; travel
  is refused in the listed states with the listed lines.
- Getting dizzy in the sandbox after sitting at b2 wakes Pip at b2 with full hearts; on home he still wakes by the Tree.
- The map card's two tabs work with keyboard, pad and touch; `hud.audit()` clean.
- The World tab shows sketch mode without `item:chart` and the painted chart with it; listed hidden lands are fog.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Hidden until whole* (fog for hidden lands), *Precache* (+≤ 350 KB core),
  *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/travel.test.js` (new: `benchKey`, `travelMenu` labels and order, `canTravel` truth table);
`node tests/inpage.mjs travel`; `node tests/inpage.mjs wp6` (HUD); `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope

Home's benches and the bench model (S-082a); sailing (S-081); root-gates (S-182).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Benches exist only in the sandbox until S-082a.

<a id="s-081"></a>

### S-081 · feat: chart sailing on the Dandelion with a painted sailing card

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-080, S-068b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/systems.md#travel](../bible/systems.md#travel), [../bible/systems.md#realm-swap](../bible/systems.md#realm-swap),
[../bible/art-audio.md#loading-cards](../bible/art-audio.md#loading-cards), [../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data),
[../bible/cast.md#nell](../bible/cast.md#nell)

#### Why

Act II is sailed: every new land is reached by picking it on the chart and crossing on the *Dandelion*. This step
makes an open chart node a real voyage with a painted card and a sea shanty, while unbuilt lands stay fog. Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-081 (M11); vision item **Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **Selecting an `open` node** in the ChartCard (opened by any `[E] Sail · the Dandelion`): a confirm "Sail to
   {land}?" with `Yes` / `Not yet`. `Yes` → `RealmManager.enter(id, REALMS[id].arrive, 'sail')`. Selecting the
   current land's node closes the chart. The home node is always `open` from another land (sail home to spawn
   `jetty_end`).
2. **The sailing card:** during the swap the loading card shows `load_sailing` (lo version on phones and low quality)
   for **4.0 s minimum, 6.0 s maximum** unless generation takes longer (systems.md#realm-swap row 2), with the track
   `sailing` playing (register it in `TRACKS` of `js/audio.js` from
   [../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data) verbatim: D, 3/4, 100 bpm, uke lead) and the
   card's tip line "Nell hums. The Dandelion creaks happily." (text id `home.tip.sailing`).
3. **Arrival:** at the land's `arrive` spawn; `travel['chart:<id>'] = true`; `EV.REALM { how: 'sail' }`. Nell stands
   by the land's bollard (spot `<realm>.nell`, from the land's recipe; the sandbox gets one) while Pip is in that land,
   saying (priority 20) `home.npc.nell.away.1` "I'll be here, first mate. Back... eventually. You, I mean. Not me."
4. **Every overworld's jetty** carries the portal `<land>:jetty` (`how: 'sail'`, `[E] Sail · the Dandelion`) on its
   bollard; the realm recipe declares its position (Duskwick: the bollard (−6, 140), S-083).
5. **Fog banks** unchanged from S-068b: a `fog` node toasts its line and never sails.
6. **Debug and test:** `G.debug.chart.open(id)` force-opens a node for tests (never in play); the sandbox registry
   entry gains `arrive: 'jetty'` and a bollard spawn. In-page `travel` suite: from home's jetty, sail to the sandbox
   (card time ≥ 4.0 s and ≤ 6.0 s plus measured generation time, Pip on walkable ground at `jetty`, `chart:sandbox`
   set, Nell at her spot), sail home (arrive at `jetty_end`), × 2 with no leak (`renderer.info.memory` within ±2).

#### Assets

`load_sailing` (`ui`, core): kind `loading`, generate 1792 × 1024, ship 1600 × 900 webp q80 ≤ 200 KB plus `lo`
960 × 540 ≤ 70 KB; subject from [../bible/art-audio.md#loading-cards](../bible/art-audio.md#loading-cards) verbatim
("A little sailing boat with a dandelion-yellow sail and a pelican at the tiller crossing a calm sparkling sea toward
a palm island on the horizon, gulls overhead, a small boy in a green hood at the bow. Palette: #5aaef2 #ffd98a #f6ecd2
#3fa79a."). Fallback: the shipped loading background (`F-home:loading_bg`).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Sailing between home and the sandbox works both ways twice in one session with no leak and no `modErrs`.
- The card holds 4.0-6.0 s; the `sailing` track plays during it and the arrival region's track after.
- Every hidden land is still fog and never sails (Duskwick included).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Hidden until whole*, *Precache* (+≤ 270 KB core), *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`node tests/inpage.mjs travel`; `bun test` (audio track validator over `sailing`, chart unit tests);
`node tools/build-pages.mjs` + `bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope

Free sailing (S-198/S-199); Nell's upgrades (M23); the Duskwick arrival scene (S-091).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. No reachable land is open yet, so players see no change but the card art in tests.

<a id="s-082a"></a>

### S-082a · feat: Lamplighter kit I: the bench, the beacon and the spiral; home's five benches

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-080, S-068b, S-074 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-bench](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-bench), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-beacon](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-beacon),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-spiral](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-spiral), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-jetty](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-jetty),
[../bible/atlas-home.md#benches](../bible/atlas-home.md#benches)

#### Why

One look for the old order of lamp-keepers on every land: the bench (waystone), the beacon (the thing each isle
relights) and the Spiral (the way home). Home gets its five benches so travel is useful from day one. Split from
S-082 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-082 (M11); vision item **Travel
and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **`js/props-kit.js`** gains (kit palette of [../bible/atlas-isles.md#shared](../bible/atlas-isles.md#shared):
   limestone `#d9cdb4` / `#a89a80`, moss `#7f9a5a`, brass `#b08a45` / `#6e5530`, moth-glass `#6b6478`, lamp `#ffd27a`
   core `#fff1c4`, spiral inlay `#c9a24a`; R12):
   - **`kit_bench`** exactly as [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-bench](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-bench): 1.8 m long,
     seat 0.45 m high and 0.55 m deep, back slab top 1.35 m with a half-disc top and the Spiral pierced through it
     (the `kit_spiral` decal with alpha), two moth-wing armrests, moss on the seat corners; collider box 1.8 × 0.55;
     ≤ 420 tris, LOD ≤ 120. Marked state: inlay emissive `#ffd27a` at 1.4 and a 0.6 m additive glow; the first Sit
     fills the inlay from the centre out over 0.8 s with a soft chime (SFX `benchMark`: `bell(t, 659, 1.0, 0.4);
     bell(t + 0.2, 988, 1.2, 0.35)`).
   - **`kit_beacon`** exactly as [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-beacon](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-beacon): an
     eight-sided brass cage 1.6 m tall, 1.0 m across (scale 0.8-2.4), round moth-glass panes, a pointed cap with a
     spiral finial, a short limestone plinth; dark panes `#6b6478` at 0.55 opacity; lit panes emissive `#ffd27a` 2.2
     with core `#fff1c4`, a 6 m additive `fx_beacon_glow` sprite, and one of the 4 pooled point lights only while Pip
     is within 40 m. API `beacon.setLit(bool, animate)`: the kindle animation lights panes clockwise over 1.2 s, then
     the glow blooms 0 → 1 over 0.6 s; lit glow breathes ±8 % over 3 s. ≤ 900 tris.
   - **`kit_jetty_lamp`**: a 2.6 m post with `kit_beacon` at 0.4 scale on top (joins S-068b's jetty kit; home's jetty
     gets one at its root).
2. **`placeBenches`** (home post-pass row 9, `new RNG(7151 * 101 + 41)`; R4): `W.waystones` entries in this order,
   each `near()` with the home `ok()` and facing the listed view:

   | Id | Name (region) | Placement | Faces |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `b1` | Shellby's Bench (Sprout Village) | `near((−6.5, 9.5), 0, 3)`, at least 1.2 m off the z = 9 test line | the Great Tree |
   | `b2` | Woods Bench (Whispering Woods) | `near(Hoot's spot (4.3, −61.6) + 4 m east, 2, 6)` | the Woods path |
   | `b3` | Peaks Bench (Frostcap Peaks) | `near(Peaks bridge east end (54.7, 2.2) + 6 m east, 2, 8)` | the river |
   | `b4` | Shores Bench (Sunny Shores) | `near(jetty root + 3 m north, 0, 4)` | the jetty and the sea |
   | `b5` | Meadow Bench (Bloom Meadow) | `near(windmill (−108.1, −6.6) + (collider r + 5) m on bearing 150°, 0, 5)` | the windmill |

   Benches are solids through `attachPost(W)`. `STATS.home.benches` lists `bench:home:b1`-`b5`; rerun
   `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`.
3. **Sandbox showcase:** the sandbox recipe places one `kit_bench` and one `kit_beacon` (with a debug toggle
   `G.debug.kit.beacon(on)`) so the realm suite measures the kit's cost.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kit_spiral` (`billboards`, core) | `decal` · ship 256² + 128² webp alpha ≤ 12 KB | "A single hand-carved spiral glyph, two and a half turns, ending in a short outward tick, chiselled into pale stone with soft golden light in the groove, centred. Palette: #c9a24a #d9cdb4 #ffd27a." | canvas `arc` spiral, stroke `#c9a24a`, 6 px |
| `fx_beacon_glow` (`billboards`, core) | `card` · ship 256² webp alpha ≤ 20 KB, additive | "A soft round warm-gold lamp glow, bright creamy centre fading to transparent edges, gentle painterly bloom, no hard rim. Palette: #ffd27a #fff1c4." | canvas radial gradient |

ART BRIEFs `kit_bench` and `kit_beacon` pasted from atlas-isles (both images are reference sheets only: generate the
two "reference sheet" prompts there and save as `docs/art/ref/kit_bench.webp`, `docs/art/ref/kit_beacon.webp`).
Commented model slots `// kit_bench: { …, height: 1.35 }`, `// kit_beacon: { …, height: 1.6 }`. S-073's canvas spiral on
the fairy-ring panels switches to `kit_spiral` by key.

#### Acceptance criteria

- `placeBenches` passes the four home-pass tests on seeds 1-40; world prints byte-identical; every bench reachable,
  `b1` ≥ 1.2 m from the z = 9 line.
- Sitting on each home bench heals, saves, marks it once ("✿ Bench found: Shellby's Bench"), and the travel panel lists
  the found ones plus "The Great Tree"; fast travel between two home benches works.
- The beacon's dark and lit states and the kindle animation run in the sandbox; lit uses at most one pooled light.
- `test:playthrough` unchanged (no bench on the scripted route).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island*, *Budgets per view* (home's four WP7 views within budget with
  the benches), *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/world.test.js tests/stats.test.js`; `node tests/inpage.mjs travel` (add: home benches); `node
tests/inpage.mjs wp7`; `bun run test:playthrough`.

#### Out of scope

Ruins, log niche, spiral door, root-gate and the star-door stand-in (S-082b); beacons placed on isles (their land
steps).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Additive pass.

<a id="s-082b"></a>

### S-082b · feat: Lamplighter kit II: ruins, log niche, spiral door, root-gate, star-door stand-in and curios

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-082a, S-068b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-ruins](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-ruins), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-log-niche](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-log-niche),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-hollow-door](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-hollow-door), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-root-gate](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-root-gate),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-curio](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-curio), [../bible/systems.md#brief-gates](../bible/systems.md#brief-gates)

#### Why

The rest of the shared kit every land's finds step places: ruin stamps that show the Lamplighters were there, the
niche a Lamp Log waits in, the round spiral door of an isle Hollow, the root-gate stub, and the generic "curio" (a
`Look` with one line) that fills the map with small things to find. Split from S-082 (see
[Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-082 (M11); vision item **Travel and the
Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **`js/props-kit.js`** gains, each an instanced type with the sizes, tris and colliders of atlas-isles:
   `kit_ruin_pillar` (fluted limestone pillar snapped at 1.2 / 2.2 / 3.2 m by instance scale, Spiral at 1 m, 0.7 m
   across, ≤ 260 tris, collider circle r 0.45) · `kit_ruin_arch` (half an arch: one 2.8 m post and a curved lintel
   stub, 2.2 m wide, ≤ 320, collider on the post) · `kit_ruin_lampstump` (a 1.1 m square lamp-stand with an empty
   bent brass hook, ≤ 180) · `kit_ruin_ring` (8 tilted kerb stones round a cracked spiral slab, 3.4 m across, walkable,
   ≤ 400) · `kit_log_niche` (0.6 × 0.45 × 0.35 m arched niche with a candle stub, a rolled ribbon-tied page `#efe3c8`
   / `#b55d4c`, the Spiral on the keystone; unread: the spiral emissive `#ffd27a` 0.6; ≤ 200) · `kit_hollow_door`
   (a 2.0 m round stone door in a 2.6 m frame with a full-face Spiral and a moth knocker `#b08a45`; a small lamp above
   lights `#ffd27a` when its Hollow is cleared; ≤ 500) · `kit_rootgate` (a 12 × 2.8 m root arch, 1.4 m thick, bark
   `#8a5a3c` / `#5e3c28`, an oval 1.4 × 2.2 m bark door with the Spiral; sealed: a plug of packed earth `#7a5a3e` with
   stones `#a89a80`; open: the door glows `#ffcf7a` and a glow strip runs under the root; ≤ 1,400 tris; colliders two
   circles at the root's ends, walk-under; API `setOpen(bool)`; a placer rotates its long axis to the land's bearing
   toward home) · `gate_star_door` **stand-in** (R32; the code fallback of
   [../bible/systems.md#brief-gates](../bible/systems.md#brief-gates): a dark stone door 2 × 2.8 m `#2a3358` with a
   crescent moon and seven stars `#f6f3d8` that twinkle at night; sealed only; ≤ 400 tris; collider box).
2. **Curios** (interactable kind `curio`, [../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-curio](../bible/atlas-isles.md#kit-curio)): a
   record `{ id, kind: 'curio', prop, verb: 'Look' | 'Read', label, lines: [textId, textId?], x, z }` shows
   `[E] Verb · Label` and one or two lines, no reward, no save state. Curios count as points of interest.
3. **Keepsakes for any land:** generalise the shipped keepsake code so a land's keepsakes (ids `<realm>:k1..k4`, kept
   in `S.secrets`, accepted by normalize through `SECRET_IDS`, S-024) use the shipped twinkle within 6 m (LOCKED), a
   two-note shimmer (SFX `keepShimmer`: `bell(t, 1047, 0.7, 0.3); bell(t + 0.12, 1568, 0.9, 0.28)`), the shipped prompt
   pattern (`Look · <label>`), two lines, 25 gems once, and the LOCKED toast format "Secret found n/N" where N counts
   keepsakes that exist (R21). Home's four are unchanged.
4. **Sandbox showcase:** one of each new piece in the sandbox; the realm suite measures them.

#### Assets

Code-built only. ART BRIEFs pasted from atlas-isles (`kit_ruin_*`, `kit_log_niche`, `kit_hollow_door`, `kit_rootgate`)
and systems.md (`gate_star_door`); generate their reference sheets to `docs/art/ref/` (the atlas prompts; docs only).
Commented model slots for each key with its height.

#### Acceptance criteria

- Each piece builds without a GLB, within its tris budget, and the sandbox's four views stay ≤ 200 main-pass calls.
- A test curio and a test keepsake in the sandbox behave as specified (prompt, lines, twinkle, 25 gems once, toast).
- The root-gate's `setOpen` and the Hollow door's cleared lamp switch states.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Assets*, *Budgets per view*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (kit unit tests: tris counts from the built geometry, keepsake id acceptance in normalize);
`node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (sandbox views); `node tests/inpage.mjs travel` (a curio and a keepsake in the sandbox).

#### Out of scope

Placing any of this on an isle (S-084b, S-086a/b); opening star doors (S-208) and root-gates (S-182).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert; nothing is placed in play yet.

<a id="s-083"></a>

### S-083 · feat: Duskwick lagoon recipe: terrain, Wickhaven's decks, regions and name cards (hidden)

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-027, S-018 · Size: M (split rule: if over 500 lines, S-083b = the Wickhaven decks and
the Vault Stair) · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-card](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-card),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-recipe](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-recipe),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-wickhaven](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-wickhaven),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-regions](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-regions),
[../bible/systems.md#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry), [../bible/art-audio.md#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes)

#### Why

Duskwick is the first land beyond home: the palm isle with the stone tower that the shipped horizon has shown
since day one. This step generates its ground (a ring isle round a lagoon, a headland plateau, coral terraces, black
dunes and hot pools), the moth town's stilt decks and the four regions, and freezes its prints. Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-083 (M11); vision item **Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **Registry entry** (`js/realms/index.js`; R9, R11):

   ```js
   duskwick: { id: 'duskwick', name: 'Duskwick', kind: 'overworld', size: 448, seed: 40211, recipe: 'lagoon',
     palette: 'duskwick', music: 'duskwick', hidden: true, open: 'flag:ch7', parent: null, arrive: 'jetty',
     tod: null, bundle: 'duskwick', enemies: 'duskwick', dash: true, benches: 5, rootgate: true,
     bollard: { x: -6, z: 140 },
     views: [ { x: -8, z: 128, yaw: 0, pitch: 0 },               // town: the jetty spawn, the Wick Mast and the square
              { x: 66, z: -72, yaw: 45, pitch: 12 },             // landmark: the Tower from the saddle
              { x: -90, z: 40, yaw: 90, pitch: -2 },             // densest: the Coco Grove toward Wickhaven
              { x: 120, z: -118, y: 33.5, yaw: 220, pitch: -12 } ] } // widest: from the Tower's window-ledge height
   ```
   `yaw` is a compass bearing in degrees (0 north, clockwise); every later realm uses this encoding. Spawns (filled by
   the recipe): `jetty` (−8, 128) facing 0°; `tower` (124, −104) facing 225°; `rootgate` (52, −104) facing 157°;
   `vault` (146, −134) facing 200°.
2. **Recipe `js/realms/recipes/lagoon.js`** (a `RealmWorld` subclass like the sandbox; every stage on its own
   `new RNG(40211 * 3 + c)` stream, `c` = 1 outline, 3 knoll, 5 plateau, 7 dunes, 9 terraces; S-084a/b use
   `40211 * 5 + c`). Numbers from [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-recipe](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-recipe),
   with the two additions marked (R39):
   - **Outline:** shore radius `r(θ) = 168 + 10·fbm(θ; 3 octaves, 3 cycles per turn) + 30·max(0, cos(θ − 45°))^10`
     **+ 28·max(0, cos(θ − 115°))^6** (the Coral Steps lobe, R39). Shore profile: `h` falls from 1.2 at `r − 8` to
     −0.4 at `r`, to −6 at `r + 30`. Inland base ground `h = 1.2 + 0.6·fbm(8 m)`. Sand layer wherever slope < 0.15
     within 14 m of the shore.
   - **Candle Knoll:** dome at (18, −28), `h += 16·(1 − (d/64)²)²` for d < 64, plus 1.5 m fbm.
   - **Tower Head:** plateau `h = 14` at (112, −112), radius 42; landward (bearings 120°-330° around its centre) it
     blends to the base ground over 20 m (slope ≤ 0.7); a saddle 10 m wide from (66, −72) at h 6 to (92, −94) at h 14
     joins the knoll; cliffs (slope 1.1-1.6) on the seaward arc, bearings 330°-120°. Sea stacks: **Gull Stack**
     (160, −98) r 5 top h 20; **Lantern Stack** (98, −176) r 6, flat top h 8. **Vault ledge** (R39): flatten a disc
     r 6 at (146, −140) to h 1.2.
   - **Lagoon:** ellipse centre (−8, 92), rx 74, rz 58; floor by normalised radius `e`: 0.80-1.00 → h −0.3 (the wading
     rim, walkable), 0.40-0.80 → h −1.0 (the lagoon flats, water at high tide), < 0.40 → h −2.4 (the Deep Lamp Pool).
     Mouth channel (−8, 150) → (−8, 182), 26 m wide, h −2.6. **The Mouth Rocks**: two rows of breakwater boulders
     (solid circles r 1.2-2.0, every 3.5 m) along x = −24 and x = +8 for z 150-176.
   - **Ember Sands:** a dune band 40 m wide inside the west shore (bearings 175°-315°), `h = 0.6 … 4` with directional
     noise along 20° (wavelength 30 m). **Hot pools:** six flat basins at h 2.0-3.5 centred around (−118, −52) within
     r 18, radii 3, 3.5, 4, 4, 4.5, 5 m, floor 0.3 m below the rim. **The Three Chimneys:** basalt stacks at
     (−152, −22) top 18 m r 5, (−160, −8) top 14 m r 4, (−145, −2) top 11 m r 3.5, slope > 1.5.
     **Sizzle Sands Tube** (R39: a heightfield cannot overhang): a basalt ridge 12 m wide and 4.4 m above the ground
     along (−62, −40) → (−86, −58), with a 4 m-wide channel cut through it at ground height, ending in a 10 m disc
     chamber at (−86, −58) walled by the ridge; one entrance at (−62, −40). Mark every channel and chamber cell
     `W.dark = 1` (the roof is a visual mesh in S-084b).
   - **Coral Steps** (bearings 70°-175°), by distance `d = r(θ) − ρ` from the shore: the **Reef Shelf** d 0-14
     (h 0.4-1.0, 22 tide-pool decals), terraces h 3 (d 14-26), h 6 (d 26-38), h 9 (d 38-50), h 12 (d ≥ 50); risers are
     cliffs (≥ 2.6 m, slope ≥ 1.1); 3 m-wide ramp cuts through every riser at bearings 85°, 120°, 160°. **The Coral
     Cupboard** (R39): a notch 4 m wide and 4 m deep into the h-3 riser on bearing 120°, floor at the Reef Shelf's
     height, cells `W.dark = 1`; its mouth is `W.sites.coralCupboard`.
   - **Paths** (2.4 m, dirt slot): **P1** (−8, 30) → (40, −20) → (66, −72) → (121, −119) (the Tower door);
     **P2** (−8, 30) → (−64, 28) → (−112, −46) → (−140, −14); **P3** (68, 88) → (64, 36) → (120, 30) → the ramps →
     (160, 90); **P4** (40, −20) → (18, −28); **P5 the Old Shore Walk** (1.8 m, half-strength splat) (−140, −14) →
     (−120, −90) → (−60, −140) → (52, −112) → (66, −72).
   - **The Vault Stair:** six straight flights (decks 1.6 m wide, each dropping ≈ 2.1 m) zig-zagging down the
     plateau's north cliff from (138, −128) h 14 to (146, −140) h 1.2; at its top a lattice-gate collider
     (`active: true`, record `W.gates.vaultGate`, opened at `mainEnding` by S-297).
3. **Wickhaven's decks** (recipe bridge decks, deck top h 1.6, walkable at any tide, rope rails; R12 jetty width 3.0 m;
   [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-wickhaven](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-wickhaven) verbatim):
   Wick Square 28 × 20 m centred (−8, 88); north boardwalk (−8, 78) → (−8, 30); west boardwalk (−22, 90) → (−84, 92);
   east boardwalk (6, 90) → (68, 88); the jetty (−8, 98) → (−8, 140) with the bollard at (−6, 140); the bazaar pier
   (12, 96) → (20, 112); the Long House deck at (−34, 108) (house 14 × 10 m) joined to the square's south-west corner by
   a 10 m plank; eight 8 × 8 m house decks at W1 (−36, 80), W2 (−52, 102), W3 (−66, 80), N1 (6, 60), N2 (−22, 52),
   E1 (22, 102), E2 (38, 78), E3 (52, 104), each joined to the nearest boardwalk by a 4 m plank. `W.reserved` discs
   for every house, the mast (−8, 86) and the Long House.
4. **Regions** (`W.regions`, S-027 shape; R9): `wickhaven` = the lagoon, the mouth channel's shores and every land cell
   within 26 m of the lagoon ellipse; else by bearing from (0, 0): `towerhead` [315°, 70°), `coralsteps` [70°, 175°),
   `embersands` [175°, 315°); weights blend over 12 m. Cards (name · subtitle): **Wickhaven** · "Stilts, moths and
   lamplight", **Tower Head** · "The last light", **Coral Steps** · "Mind the coral", **Ember Sands** · "Black sand,
   warm toes". Entry points: jetty spawn (−8, 128), P1 at (44, −40), P3 at (64, 36), P2 at (−64, 28). Fog rows
   (art-audio, mapped by name): wickhaven `[30, 230, #f6e2c0, .12]`, embersands `[34, 250, #f2d6b8, .18]`, coralsteps
   `[32, 245, #e9f2ee, .15]`, towerhead `[36, 262, #f4e8d8, .12]`. `wickhaven` has `town: true`, `max: 0`; the others
   `enemies: [], max: 0` in this step (S-089 fills them).
5. **Palette** `W.palette.duskwick` (art-audio row): swatch `#2b2238 #f2b45a #ff8f7a #3fa79a`; sky day
   `#6fb3e8`/`#ffe6c2`, gold `#ffc27a`, dusk `#5a4a9a`/`#ff9f7a`, night `#2b2238`/`#4a3560`; clouds `#fff4e6` /
   `#ffc8b0` / `#6a5a8a`; water deep `#1f8f95`, shallow `#3fa79a`, foam `#fff4e0`; layers `duskwick_palmgrass`,
   `duskwick_jungle`, `duskwick_blossom`, `duskwick_goldsand`, `duskwick_coral`, `duskwick_redearth`,
   `duskwick_basalt`, cliff `duskwick_cliff` (each falls back to home's layer of the same slot tinted by the swatch
   until S-085 ships the textures).
6. **Prints:** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/duskwick.json` (placement print, FNV of `H` and `reg`, `walk` / `reach`
   checksums). S-084a and S-084b refresh it (same milestone).
7. **New in-page suite `duskwick`** (`js/debug/tests/duskwick.js`; CI matrix entry): enter with
   `G.debug.realm('duskwick', 'jetty')`, Pip on walkable ground, fog colour applied, walking to each entry point
   shows its region card, leave and re-enter with no leak. The `realm` suite picks the realm up from `REALMS`.

#### Assets

None (tinted home layers; S-085 paints).

#### Acceptance criteria

- `generateRealm('duskwick')` is deterministic and `W.genMs` ≤ 1,200 ms on the laptop (printed by the test).
- Reachable land at high water is 74,000-86,000 m²; `W.unreachable` is empty; the spawns `jetty`, `tower`, `rootgate`
  and the four entry points are reachable; `vault` is reachable only with `W.gates.vaultGate` inactive.
- Region samples: (−8, 88) `wickhaven`; (112, −112) `towerhead`; (126, 30) `coralsteps`; (−118, −52) `embersands`;
  (−60, −140) `towerhead`.
- Coordinate checks: Wick Square deck top at h 1.6; the Tower site (126, −124) on the plateau at h 14 with a clear disc
  r 6; the Reef Shelf at (160, 90) has h 0.4-1.0; the Old Fan site (136, 40) is on the h-12 terrace; the Tube chamber
  and the Cupboard are `W.dark`; every path cell has h ≥ 0.2.
- `REALMS.duskwick.hidden === true`; the chart still shows Duskwick as fog.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen home island* (world prints byte-identical), *Frozen realms* (new prints),
  *Hidden until whole*, *Budgets per view* (genMs), *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/realm-duskwick.test.js` (new: prints, genMs, land area, reachability, region samples, coordinate
checks, deck connectivity from the jetty spawn to every house deck and the bazaar pier); `node tests/inpage.mjs
duskwick`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `bun test tests/world.test.js` (home unchanged).

#### Out of scope

Props and landmarks (S-084a, S-084b); textures and music (S-085); finds (S-086a/b); enemies (S-089); the tide
(S-122).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. The realm is hidden; only tests and `?realm=` reach it.

<a id="s-084a"></a>

### S-084a · feat: Duskwick scatter kit: palms, coral, basalt, driftwood and hot-pool rims

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-083, S-082a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-kit](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-kit),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-coral](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-coral),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-small](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-small),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#sightlines](../bible/atlas-isles.md#sightlines)

#### Why

A land reads as a place through what grows on it. This step dresses Duskwick's ground with its own plants and
stones within the per-realm budget (≤ 24 instanced types, ≤ 12 new material programs) and cuts the first-person
sightline corridors to its landmarks. Split from S-084 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-084 (M11); vision item **Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **Instanced types** (`js/realms/props/duskwick.js`, scatter stream `new RNG(40211 * 5 + 1)`): palm (the shipped
   `tree_palm` recoloured: trunk `#7a5a3e`, leaves `#3fa79a`, LOD proxy beyond 42 m), basalt rock (shipped `rock`,
   `#3b3540`), coral bush (shipped `bush`, flowers `#ff8f7a`), `duskwick_coral_shrub` (knobbly rounded coral
   0.6-1.4 m, `#ff8f7a` / `#ffb38a` / `#e8708a` by instance tint, ≤ 220 tris, LOD at 38-48 m), `duskwick_coral_small`
   (a small fan 0.8 m, ≤ 140), `duskwick_driftwood` (bleached log 2-4 m `#c8b8a0`, ≤ 120), grass card, flower card,
   shell deco, `duskwick_coconut_pile` (10 coconuts `#7a5a3e`, ≤ 200), `duskwick_hot_pool_rim` (a ring of black
   stones round each of the six basins, r 3-5 by scale, ≤ 300, `#3b3540`, plus a steam card), `duskwick_lamp_hook_post`
   (a 1.4 m brass hook on a stone stub, ≤ 120), jetty post (S-068b's `kit_jetty_post`), pot (shipped). 15 types here;
   S-084b adds the rest (22 in all, atlas-isles).
2. **Scatter tables** (instances per 100 m² of eligible ground; none on paths, decks, the lagoon, `W.reserved` or
   sightline corridors):

   | Region | Palm | Coral shrub | Coral fan | Coral bush | Basalt rock | Driftwood | Grass card | Flower card | Shell |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `wickhaven` (land rim) | 0.6 | 0.2 | 0 | 0.4 | 0.1 | 0.1 | 6 | 3 | 0.5 |
   | `embersands` | 0.3 | 0 | 0 | 0.1 | 0.4 | 0.15 | 3 | 1 | 0.3 |
   | `coralsteps` | 0.15 | 0.8 | 0.6 | 0.4 | 0.2 | 0.05 | 4 | 2 | 0.4 |
   | `towerhead` | 0.25 | 0 | 0 | 0.3 | 0.5 | 0 | 6 | 3 | 0 |

   Fixed placements: the **Coco Grove**, 14 palms on a ring r 9 round (−90, 40); the **Lamp Hooks**, one
   `duskwick_lamp_hook_post` every 2 m along the top terrace edge from (124, 20) to (148, 60); hot-pool rims on the
   six basins.
3. **Sightline corridors** ([../bible/atlas-isles.md#sightlines](../bible/atlas-isles.md#sightlines)): 6 m-wide
   scatter reservations from each entry point and each bench anchor of S-086a (jetty (−2, 97), knoll (24, −26),
   saddle (80, −88), pools (−108, −44), fan (126, 30)) to its region's landmark top; and from the jetty spawn to the
   Tower top (bearing ≈ 28°). A unit test casts a heightfield ray (eye 1.5 m) along each and asserts no tree or tall
   prop instance lies within 3 m of it.
4. **Prints** refreshed (same milestone).

#### Assets

Code-built; the briefs `duskwick_coral_shrub`, `duskwick_coral_small`, `duskwick_hot_pool_rim`,
`duskwick_lamp_hook_post`, `duskwick_coconut_pile`, `duskwick_driftwood` from atlas-isles pasted in the PR
(each with its 3D prompt "stylised <thing>, toon shading, game-ready"); manifest keys carry `realm: 'duskwick'`;
commented model slots for the coral shrub and the coconut pile.

#### Acceptance criteria

- At most 15 instanced types from this step; the realm's four views in both cameras stay ≤ 200 main-pass calls and
  ≤ 1.3 M tris (measured by the realm suite; numbers in the PR).
- `W.genMs` ≤ 1,200 ms; the sightline test passes for every listed corridor.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen realms* (prints refreshed within M11), *Hidden until whole*, *Budgets per
  view*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/realm-duskwick.test.js` (prints, genMs, sightlines); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`;
`node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick`.

#### Out of scope

Landmarks, houses and lanterns (S-084b); textures (S-085).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Hidden realm.

<a id="s-084b"></a>

### S-084b · feat: Duskwick landmarks: the Lamplighters' Tower, the Wick Mast, the Chimneys, the Old Fan and the stilt town

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-084a, S-082b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-landmarks](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-landmarks),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-tower](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-tower),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-wick-mast](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-wick-mast),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-chimneys](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-chimneys),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-old-fan](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-old-fan),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-stilt-house](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-stilt-house),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-long-house](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-long-house),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-moth-lantern](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-moth-lantern)

#### Why

Four landmarks, one per region, are how a first-person player finds the way; the Tower is the one the whole act
points at. This step builds them, the stilt town, the moth lanterns and the kit ruins at the land's named sites.
Split from S-084 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-084 (M11); vision item
**Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **The Lamplighters' Tower** (`duskwick_tower`, a unique mesh at (126, −124) on the plateau h 14): a round
   stacked-stone tower 28 m tall, radius 4.5 m at the base tapering linearly to 3.0 m at the top, one round window
   1.4 m across at 18 m on the south-west face (bearing 225°), a glass lamp room 3.5 m tall at the top holding the
   great lamp (`kit_beacon` at 2.4×, dark), a conical slate cap `#4a4458` with the Spiral finial; stone `#d9cdb4`,
   mortar shadow `#a89a80`, moss `#7f9a5a` at the base; ink hull; ≤ 7,000 tris, LOD proxy ≤ 600 beyond 120 m.
   - **The outer stair** (R40; post-decks on the realm, 1.2 m wide, rope rail): a two-line switchback on the **west
     face**. With `C = (126, −124)`, line A is `x = 120.0`, line B is `x = 118.6`, both from `z = −120.5` (south end)
     to `z = −127.5` (north end). Flights: F1 A south→north h 14→17 · F2 B north→south 17→20 · F3 A 20→23 · F4 B
     23→26 · F5 A 26→29 · F6 B 29→32. Landings 2.6 × 1.6 m spanning both lines at the north end (h 17, 23, 29) and the
     south end (h 20, 26). F1's foot (120.0, −120.5) is 1.8 m from the door. From F6's top (118.6, −120.5) a 1.2 m
     walkway at h 32 runs east to **the window ledge**, a 3.0 m (tangent) × 2.4 m (radial) balcony at h 32 under the
     window, centred at `C + 6.2·dir(225°)` = (121.6, −119.6). The ledge is open from day one (the glide find and the
     vista use it later). Record the top of F4 as `W.sites.towerF4` (the ch8 glimpse trigger, R15) and the ledge as
     `W.sites.towerLedge`.
   - **The door** (121, −119), facing 225°: a brass gear-lock with a moth face; `[E] Knock · Tower Door` →
     `cc.tower.door` "Locked. From the inside. Something in there is ticking." (R16). Portal record `duskwick:tower`
     (to `lamplighters_tower`, `needs: 'flag:towerOpen'`, not offered while its realm does not exist, S-067's guard).
   - States (read by S-091 and later): before ch8 nothing on this isle is visible to players; from ch8 the window and
     lamp room are dark; S-165 lights them.
2. **The Wick Mast** (`duskwick_wick_mast`, (−8, 86) on Wick Square): three lashed driftwood poles 16 m tall
   `#b8a080` / `#7a6450`, three crossbars, a decorative rope ladder up the north face to the first crossbar (3.2 m, not
   climbable), ≤ 1,800 tris.
3. **Moth lantern strings** (`duskwick_moth_lantern`, instanced, ≤ 60 tris each, 0.35 m paper moths `#f6e6c4`,
   glow `#f2b45a`, vertex-shader sway ±6°): from the mast top to 8 rail posts round the square, and along every
   boardwalk one every 8 m. **Dim state** (until `beacon:duskwick`): at night 1 in 3 lit (by a fixed per-instance
   hash), the mast's top lantern dark; lit state (S-165) all lit.
4. **The Three Chimneys** (`duskwick_chimney`, one instanced type ×3, ≤ 900 tris each) on S-083's stack sites, each
   with a thin steam plume card (`duskwick_card_steam`, S-085; a `fx_atlas` puff until then) from its top.
5. **The Old Fan** (`duskwick_old_fan`, (136, 40) on the h-12 terrace, facing south-east): a branching coral frame
   (12 tubes in one plane) carrying 6 alpha cards (`duskwick_fan_card`, S-085; a coral-pink tube lattice until
   then), 15 m tall, 13 m wide, stalk collider r 1.4, ≤ 2,400 tris + 6 cards.
6. **The stilt town:** eight `duskwick_stilt_house` (6 × 6 m cabins on eight thin stilts, thatch roofs with two
   antenna-curled tips, round teal doors `#3fa79a`, round windows glowing `#f2b45a` at night; ≤ 1,600 tris; instanced)
   on S-083's house decks, door facing the boardwalk; the **Long House** (`duskwick_long_house`, 14 × 10 m, 9 m tall,
   a round window end facing the Tower, red book-painted shutters `#b55d4c`, a loft dormer with a crooked chimney;
   ≤ 3,200 tris) with its door at (−34, 101) (portal record `duskwick:library` to `wick_library`, `needs:
   'flag:ch8'`, not offered until S-167 builds the interior); the bazaar pier's empty mooring.
7. **Roofs and ruins:** the Sizzle Sands Tube and the Coral Cupboard get rock roof meshes (visual only, `occ: true`);
   `kit_ruin_ring` at Candle Knoll (20, −32); `kit_ruin_arch` at the Saddle Arch (84, −86); the jetty's
   `kit_jetty_lamp` at the square end (−8, 98); the Vault door (146, −140) (a round stone door carved with a hundred
   little dark lamps, unique mesh, ≤ 800 tris) and the lattice gate at the Vault Stair's top.
8. **Counts:** instanced types total 22 (atlas-isles); new material programs ≤ 12 (coral, basalt, moth-glass emissive,
   brass, limestone, plank, steam additive, lagoon tint); prints refreshed.

#### Assets

Code-built; ART BRIEFs pasted from the seven atlas-isles anchors above (each with its reference-sheet image prompt,
generated to `docs/art/ref/` and never shipped, and its 3D prompt). Commented model slots `prop_duskwick_tower`
(28 m), `prop_duskwick_wick_mast` (16 m), `prop_duskwick_old_fan` (15 m), `prop_duskwick_stilt_house` (6.5 m),
`prop_duskwick_long_house` (9 m), all `realm: 'duskwick'`.

#### Acceptance criteria

- From the jetty spawn the Wick Mast is in view straight ahead and the Tower's top to its right (heightfield ray
  clear; the in-page suite screenshots both cameras at view 1).
- The outer stair is walkable from the door to the window ledge; F4's top is at h 26 and the ledge at h 32 (deck
  heights asserted); the ledge is reachable on foot.
- The Tower door shows `[E] Knock · Tower Door` with the line above; no portal is offered.
- The realm's 4 views in both cameras: main-pass calls ≤ 200, total ≤ 300, tris ≤ 1.3 M; 22 instanced types; material
  programs ≤ 12 (realm suite warm-up count).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen realms*, *Hidden until whole*, *Budgets per view*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/realm-duskwick.test.js` (prints, stair deck heights, ledge reachability, type and material counts);
`node tests/inpage.mjs realm`; `node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick` (add: walk the stair to the ledge; dim lantern ratio
at night).

#### Out of scope

Textures and cards (S-085); the Tower interior (S-162); people (S-088); lighting the Tower (S-165).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Hidden realm.

<a id="s-085"></a>

### S-085 · art: Duskwick look: terrain layers, cards, loading card, map art and music

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-083, S-029, S-028 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/art-audio.md#duskwick](../bible/art-audio.md#duskwick), [../bible/art-audio.md#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes),
[../bible/art-audio.md#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles), [../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data),
[../bible/art-audio.md#ambience](../bible/art-audio.md#ambience)

#### Why

Paint the land and give it its sound, inside the 2.5 MB bundle, so Duskwick is whole before people move in.
Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-085 (M11); vision item **Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **Terrain** (eight `tile` textures, each 1024² `_hd` ≤ 110 KB + 512² `lo` ≤ 40 KB, `realm: 'duskwick'`, files
   under `assets/realms/duskwick/`), subjects verbatim from [../bible/art-audio.md#duskwick](../bible/art-audio.md#duskwick):
   `duskwick_palmgrass`, `duskwick_jungle`, `duskwick_blossom`, `duskwick_goldsand`, `duskwick_coral`,
   `duskwick_redearth`, `duskwick_basalt`, cliff `duskwick_cliff`. Process with the seamless recipe
   (`make_seamless.py --size 1024 --band 0.22` and `--size 512`), check a 3 × 3 contact tile for seams, webp q82.
   `W.palette.duskwick.layers` / `cliff` now resolve to them (S-083 already names the keys).
2. **Cards** (`billboards`, `realm: 'duskwick'`): `duskwick_card_palmfrond` (a 1024 × 512 atlas of 4 cells
   `sprout`, `frond`, `beachgrass`, `coralflower`; `sheet-items` N = 4), `duskwick_card_lagoonglow` (512² alpha),
   `duskwick_card_moth` (a 256² 4-frame strip via `process_strip.py --fw 256 --fh 256 --frames 4`, fps 8; the
   Wicklings' ambient moth particles), `duskwick_card_steam` (512² alpha; the Chimneys and the hot pools),
   `duskwick_card_lantern_string` (1024 × 256 alpha), plus `duskwick_fan_card` (1024² alpha, the atlas-isles Old
   Fan prompt). The grass and flower cards of S-084a switch to the palm-frond atlas cells.
3. **Loading card** `load_duskwick` (1600 × 900 q80 ≤ 200 KB + `lo` 960 × 540 ≤ 70 KB) and **map art**
   `map_duskwick` (1024² ≤ 200 KB), subjects verbatim from art-audio. The This-land map card draws `map_duskwick`
   under the markers in Duskwick (fallback `buildMapCanvas(W)`).
4. **Music and ambience** in the realm module `js/realms/duskwick.js` (`onRealm` hook): `G.audio.addTrack('duskwick',
   …)` with the block from [../bible/art-audio.md#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data) verbatim (Bb, 4/4, 96 bpm,
   pluck lead, `lead2: 'marimba'`, `layers: { lead2: 1 }`, `startLayer: 0`); on entry call `G.audio.layer(0)` while
   `!flag:beacon:duskwick` (the dim state: the marimba is the missing voice, art-audio decision 12) and `layer(1)`
   once it is set. `G.audio.addAmbience('duskwick_town', { waves: [480, 0.07], wind: [520, 0.6, 0.03, 0.02], ev:
   [['ambMoth', 0.3, 1], ['ambBird', 0.2, 0], ['ambChime', 0.03, -1]], dim: { ev: 0.3 } })` for `wickhaven` and
   `addAmbience('duskwick_shore', { waves: [520, 0.1], wind: [450, 0.6, 0.04, 0.03], ev: [['ambGull', 0.08, 0],
   ['ambCricket', 0.3, 1], ['ambBubble', 0.1, -1]] })` for the other three regions (`W.palette.ambience` by region).
   Any one-shot (`ambMoth`, `ambChime`, `ambBubble`) missing from the SFX bank is added from
   [../bible/art-audio.md#ambience](../bible/art-audio.md#ambience).
5. **Bundle:** this step's share of `assets/realms/duskwick/` ≤ 2,100 KB (the rest of the 2.5 MB is for the
   portraits, bestiary cards, sketches and the Hollows' extras of M12); `node tools/build-pages.mjs` prints it.

#### Assets

As listed; every prompt is preamble + kind suffix + subject; every key gets its fallback in `js/realms/fallbacks.js`
(terrain `F-home:<layer>` tinted, cards `F-canvas`, loading `F-home:loading_bg`, map `F-canvas`).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Every key exists, carries `realm: 'duskwick'`, is within its size limit (`tests/assets.test.js`), and has a
  fallback; `?realm=duskwick&noassets=all` renders.
- The bundle is ≤ 2,100 KB after this step; core is unchanged.
- In Duskwick the `duskwick` track plays at layer 0 (no marimba) and the right ambience bed per region; leaving
  restores home's tracks.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Hidden until whole*, *Precache* (bundle), *Assets*, *Original IP only*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/assets.test.js` and the audio validator test; `node tools/build-pages.mjs` + `bun run test:pwa`
(offline replay of the bundle); `node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick` (add: track and layer, ambience bed per region,
textures loaded).

#### Out of scope

Portraits and bestiary cards (S-088, S-089); vista sketches (S-086b); the Tower interior's textures (S-162).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Hidden realm; the fallbacks keep it rendering.

<a id="s-086a"></a>

### S-086a · feat: Duskwick finds I: benches, chests, keepsakes, Hollow doors, the root-gate and tool gates

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-084b, S-075a, S-076, S-074 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden: true` ·
Bible: [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-finds](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-finds),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-keepsakes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-keepsakes),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-gates](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-gates),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#passes), [../bible/systems.md#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids)

#### Why

The rewards and promises of the land: ten chests (half behind tools Pip will get later, so the isle is worth
revisiting), four keepsakes round the backs of the landmarks, five benches, two Hollow doors, the sealed root-gate.
Split from S-086 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-086 (M11); vision item
**Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

All placements use the isle anchor rule (nearest valid 1 m cell to `(x, z)` within `R`, chosen among the five nearest
by the pass's RNG; R8 ids):

1. **`placeIsleBenches`** (`new RNG(40211 * 71 + 1)`) → `W.waystones`: `b1` "Jetty Bench" Wick Square south rail
   (−2, 97) on deck; `b2` "Knoll Bench" (24, −26); `b3` "Tower Bench" (80, −88); `b4` "Pool Bench" (−108, −44); `b5`
   "Fan Bench" (126, 30); each `kit_bench` facing its region's landmark. Travel labels "Wickhaven · Jetty Bench",
   "Tower Head · Knoll Bench" (the knoll is in Tower Head by bearing) etc.
2. **`placeIsleChests`** (`+ 3`) → `W.chests`, `kit_chest_lamplighter` look (the shipped chest with brass corners and a
   spiral lock plate), 30 gems each:

   | Id | Spot | Anchor · R | Gate (solver) | Shown |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `duskwick:c1` | the Black Cove behind the Chimneys | (−138, −34) · 10 | none | always |
   | `c2` | the Tube's dark chamber | (−86, −58) · 5 | Lantern (dark zone) | always |
   | `c3` | inside the Coral Cupboard | `W.sites.coralCupboard` · 4 (R39) | Lantern (dark zone) | always |
   | `c4` | centre of the Candle Knoll ruin ring | (20, −32) · 6 | none | always |
   | `c5` | lagoon flats under the west decks | (−46, 112) · 6 | Tide Bell (low tide; S-122) | visible through the water from the boardwalk |
   | `c6` | top of Gull Stack | (160, −98) · 3 | Thistle Hook (hookbloom, S-143) | always |
   | `c7` | Lantern Stack top | (98, −176) · 4 | Leaf Glider from the window ledge (58 m; S-102) | always |
   | `c8` | soft earth on the h-6 terrace | (150, 70) · 8 | Digging Paws (S-181) | the mound only |
   | `c9` | soft earth beside the hot pools | (−104, −66) · 8 | Digging Paws | the mound only |
   | `c10` | the top terrace's north end | (120, 4) · 8 | Starsteel (thornglass, S-233) | behind a temporary coral-rock collider |

3. **`placeIsleKeepsakes`** (`+ 5`) → `W.secrets` (S-082b's keepsake behaviour), texts verbatim from
   [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-keepsakes](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-keepsakes):
   `duskwick:k1` (`keep-wick`) seaward side of the Tower base (131, −130) · 4, `Look · Something waxy`;
   `k2` (`keep-lantern`) under the mast's bottom rung (−8, 82) · 2 on deck, `Look · Something papery`;
   `k3` (`keep-pebble`) between the stacks (−156, −15) · 4, `Look · Something warm`;
   `k4` (`keep-hook`) the Old Fan's seaward foot (142, 44) · 4, `Look · Something brass`. Text ids
   `cc.keep.k<n>.1/.2`. The fifth line ("A wick, a paper lantern, a warm pebble and an empty hook. Duskwick used to
   light the way for everyone. It still remembers how.") shows on Duskwick's Keepsake Hollow shelf plaque
   (`[E] Read · Plaque`) once all four are found.
4. **`placeIsleHollowDoors`** (`+ 15`) → two `kit_hollow_door` records with portals `duskwick:hollow1` →
   `hollow_duskwick_1` (Ember Sands dune bank (−96, 4) · 8) and `duskwick:hollow2` → `hollow_duskwick_2` (the
   h-9 riser (128, 92) · 8), prompt `[E] Enter · Spiral door` (R29); not offered until S-093 builds the realms.
5. **`placeIsleRootGate`** (`+ 17`) → `duskwick:rootgate`: `kit_rootgate` sealed on Tower Head's north plain
   (52, −112) · 6, long axis on bearing 337.5° (toward home); `Look` → `cc.rootgate.sealed` "A great root breaks out of
   the ground here and dives back in. It points north-north-west. There's a little door in it, packed shut with earth."
6. **`placeIsleGates`** (`+ 23`) → `W.gates`, visible promises the solver models: the tide stone on the Mouth Rocks'
   west row (−24, 156) (`gate_tide_stone` code fallback: a basalt standing stone 0.8 × 1.6 m `#36414f` with a wave
   spiral `#7fd3d0`); a hookbloom on Gull Stack (code fallback of `gate_hookbloom`, stalk 6 m); the two soft-earth
   mounds over c8 and c9 (`gate_soft_earth` fallback); the coral-rock stand-in before c10; and the **star-door stand-in**
   (R32) closing a 2 × 2.8 m alcove in the Reef Shelf rock at (170, 80) · 8 for S-086b's Log 10.
7. **`STATS.duskwick`** regenerated (chests with `chestGems`, keepsakes, benches, Hollows, regions, petals of the
   Hollows to come are added by S-093).

8. **Prize chests** (CANON §10.4; `CHEST_PRIZES` from S-078): opening a chest whose id has a row gives that item
   (`items.give(S, item, 1)`) with the item-get "You found <name>!" plus its `line`, instead of 30 gems; the chest still
   counts in `STATS.<realm>.chests` (its `chestGems` share is 0). Item kinds `furnishing` and `trim` already exist
   (systems.md#items); this step adds the kind **`sticker`** to `js/items.js` (no save bump: stickers are key items in
   `S.items`) and draws an owned sticker in the corner of its land's page in the journal's Map tab (`sticker_<land>`
   image, canvas fallback: a circle in the land's first palette hex with the landmark's initial). Duskwick's three:
   `c4` Wickhaven Lantern String, `c6` Candle Gold hood trim, `c7` the "Lit Tower" sticker. Later finds steps need no
   code for their prizes (the rows exist); each adds one test that its three prize chests give their item.
   `G.debug.prizes()` lists every row with owned / not owned.

#### Assets

None new (kit pieces from S-082a/b, gate stand-ins as code fallbacks of their systems.md briefs, pasted in the PR).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Every find lies within `R` of its anchor (unit test) and is reachable or gated exactly as the table says: the
  progression solver (S-048) proves c1, c4 and every bench, keepsake and the root-gate reachable with no tools; c2, c3
  with the Lantern; c5-c10 unreachable until their tool or flag.
- The four keepsakes give 25 gems once each with the LOCKED toast format; the plaque line shows after all four.
- `STATS` matches regeneration; `tests/stats.test.js` passes.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen realms* (finds are post-passes on their own streams; recipe prints
  unchanged), *Hidden until whole*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/realm-duskwick.test.js` (anchors, solver); `bun test tests/stats.test.js`;
`node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick` (add: sit at b1, open c1, find k2 on deck).

#### Out of scope

Acorns, vistas, the bottle, Lamp Logs, sites, curios and coverage (S-086b); the Hollows (S-093).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Hidden realm; post-passes only.

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-086a, S-074 · Size: M (split rule: if over 500 lines, S-086c = the coverage module and its

#### Why

The rest of Duskwick's finds and the proof that no 40 × 40 m patch of the island is empty: CANON's "the map alone
takes hours" rule, tested. Split from S-086 (see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Roadmap:
`docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-086 (M11); vision item **Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

1. **`placeIsleAcorns`** (`new RNG(40211 * 71 + 7)`) → `W.acorns`, ids `duskwick:a1`-`a6` at (12, −24) · 8,

   (82, −84) · 6. Each is a **sleeping** acorn (`col_acorn_sleeping`, code-built: a 0.25 m acorn `#c98b4b` with a cap
   `#7a5a3a`, closed eyes, breathing ±4 % every 3 s, ≤ 200 tris) with its cue: a soft snore within 10 m (SFX `snore`:
   `N({ t, d: 1.4, v: 0.03, type: 'lowpass', f: 400, f1: 250 })` every 4 s) and a slow golden "z" puff
   (`card_fx_zpuff`, canvas "z" fallback). No wake prompt until the Sunglass (S-161).
2. **`placeIsleVistas`** (`+ 9`) → `duskwick:v1` "The Last Light" on the Tower window ledge (S-084b's
   `W.sites.towerLedge`), looking over the isle with home's Great Tree to the N-NW; `v2` "Steam and Stacks" on the
   Ember Sands dune top (−110, −8) · 8, looking at the Chimneys. `kit_vista` posts; Hoot's captions verbatim from
   [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-finds](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-finds) (`cc.vista.v1.caption`,
   `.v2.caption`).
3. **`placeIsleBottle`** (`+ 11`) → `duskwick:1` (R7): Ember Sands shore at (−170, 30) · 10 on sand within 3 m of the
   water. Letter (lore.md bottle 1, `hand_oona`: ink `#5b4a7a`, small, upright, very neat; paper `#ece0c2`): head
   "Bottle 1 · about twelve hundred years old"; body and sign verbatim from
   [../bible/lore.md#bottle-1](../bible/lore.md#bottle-1).
4. **`placeIsleLogs`** (`+ 13`) → three `kit_log_niche` (R7): Log 4 "The Firstlight" (Taper) at the Tower base west wall
   (118, −126) · 3; Log 9 "The Spiral" (Glim) on the Ember Sands hot-pool terrace wall (−112, −58) · 6; Log 10
   "Wickhaven" (Chandry) inside the Reef Shelf alcove behind S-086a's star-door stand-in (170, 80) · 8 (R32: not
   reachable until S-208). Texts verbatim from lore.md (`lore.log.4/9/10.title/.body/.sign`).
5. **`placeIsleSites`** (`+ 19`) → `W.sites`, the 15 named sites of
   [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-poi) with their `Look` lines where the
   table has one (`cc.site.*`): the Hot Pools sign, the Tube mouth, Gull Stack, the Vault door; the others are site
   anchors only.
6. **`placeIsleCurios`** (`+ 21`) → the 16 curios of that table: 6 empty lamp-stands (`kit_ruin_lampstump`) along P5
   every ~45 m from (−140, −30) to (40, −118); 3 chalk moths (−4, 32), (70, 84), (−100, −40); 3 coconut stashes
   (−84, 46), (−98, 30), (70, −130); 2 moth kites (−60, −10), (150, 0); 2 warm-toe signs (−64, 22), (−130, −30); each
   with its prompt and line verbatim (`cc.curio.*`).
7. **`placeIsleFill`** (`+ 29`, runs last): for every land cell that still fails the coverage rule, one curio from the
   fill pool (empty lamp-stand, moth kite) at the cell's most central valid cell.
8. **Coverage module** `js/realms/coverage.js` (pure) and `tests/realm-coverage.test.js`, exactly
   [../bible/atlas-isles.md#coverage-rule](../bible/atlas-isles.md#coverage-rule): 40 m grid from (−224, −224); a land
   cell has ≥ 160 reachable 1 m cells; it passes by **find** (any POI anchor kind), **view** (a high point ≥ 2 m above
   each of its 8 neighbours' highest reachable cell, or a bench / vista within 60 m with an unoccluded heightfield ray
   from 1.5 m to 1.0 m above some reachable cell of it) or **path** (a path cell with `pathD < 1.5` from which the path
   network reaches a POI within 80 m); the test fails if the fill pass filled more than 12 % of land cells. It prints
   `landCells, passFind, passView, passPath, filled` and a failing-cell map. A second assertion: POI anchors ≥ land
   area / 1,250 m² (≥ 64 here; the step records the count, expected 74 before people).
9. **`STATS.duskwick`** regenerated (acorns, vistas, bottle, logs); `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`.

<a id="s-087"></a>

### S-087 · perf: checkpoint 2: the first overworld realm meets every budget

Milestone M11 · Depends on: S-086b, S-085 · Size: S · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden: true` · Bible:
[../bible/charter.md#budgets](../bible/charter.md#budgets), [../bible/charter.md#close-out-perf](../bible/charter.md#close-out-perf),
[../bible/charter.md#budgets-checkpoints](../bible/charter.md#budgets-checkpoints)

#### Why

Duskwick is the first full overworld built from the realm engine. Measuring it now, before people and enemies,
tells every later land what a land costs, and closes M11. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-087 (M11, perf
checkpoint 2); vision item **Travel and the Duskwick land**.

#### What

The charter's perf-checkpoint close-out (milestone close-out items 1-10 plus perf items 1-6), with:

1. **Measure** Duskwick's 4 fixed views in both cameras: main-pass and total draw calls, main and total triangles;
   locally (not in CI) the main-pass GPU median and 90th percentile with the heavy perf sweep.
2. **genMs** for `duskwick` (5 runs, median) and swap times home → Duskwick → home via the sailing path (with
   `G.debug.chart.open('duskwick')`), and the sandbox pair.
3. **Precache:** core MB and the `duskwick` bundle MB from `node tools/build-pages.mjs`.
4. **Fix** anything over budget in the charter's order (cull, instance or merge, LOD, fewer types, shadows, move
   content), in this PR if under ~500 lines, else as the next step naming the view.
5. **Record** everything in `docs/roadmap/progress.md` (template) and the DESIGN §14 performance table; ARCHITECTURE
   gains "How to build a land" (recipe → props → art → finds → coverage, with Duskwick's files as the example) and
   the isle post-pass stream table; DESIGN gains a travel chapter (benches, the chart, sailing).
6. **Reconciliations:** copy R7-R13, R17, R20, R29, R32, R36, R39, R40 into the bible files that lost.

#### Assets

None.

#### Acceptance criteria

All four views ≤ 200 main / ≤ 300 total calls and ≤ 1.3 M tris in both cameras; genMs ≤ 1.2 s; bundle ≤ 2.5 MB;
core ≤ 30 MB; the numbers are in the PR body and `progress.md`; every M11 close-out item ticked.

#### Verification plan

The full local suite of the charter (adding `travel`, `duskwick` and `realm`); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`;
the heavy perf sweep locally; `node tools/build-pages.mjs`.

#### Out of scope

New content.

#### Risk & rollback

Measurement and docs; fixes are each revertible.

### S-088 · feat: Wickhaven's people: Granny Wick, Flick and the drowsy Wicklings

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-086b, S-032b, S-068a · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden: true` (until S-091) ·
Bible: [../bible/cast.md#granny-wick](../bible/cast.md#granny-wick), [../bible/cast.md#flick](../bible/cast.md#flick),
[../bible/cast.md#the-wicklings](../bible/cast.md#the-wicklings), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-poi](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-poi),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-interiors](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-interiors)

#### Why

A dim isle is felt through its people: moths drooping on their decks in bright sun, one wide-awake child, and the last
Lamplighter at her door. This step builds the `moth` rig family (later users are data only) and Wickhaven's town.
Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-088 (M12 Duskwick and chapter 8); vision item **Duskwick and chapter 8:
Act II opens**.

#### What

1. **The `moth` rig family** (`js/models.js`, one `npcDef` case reading a look record; cast.md "Rig families"): a
   round fuzzy body (lobed `sphere`s), a collar ruff `torus`, two wing pairs as thin `rbox` plates on `armL` / `armR`
   (flap = arm rotation), feathery antennae (two `tube`s with 5 `cone` barbs each), optional long wing tails, a
   `drowsy` param (eyelids half closed, wings drooping 20°, a 0.5 Hz sway). ≤ 1,600 tris, ≤ 6 materials.
2. **Look records** (`js/content/looks.js`, `realm: 'duskwick'`):

   | Id | Name plate | h (m) | Palette and pieces |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `wick` | Granny Wick | 0.95 (antennae 1.1) | wings `#cfeedd`, eyespots `#b8a6e0` ringed `#f2b45a`, tails `#a8d8c0` (`tails: true`), fluff `#fff4e0`, ruff `#ffffff`, shawl `#5a3d6b`, spectacles two `torus` `#f2c94c`, pole `extra` (`cyl` 1.4 m `#c99a4a` + a `torus` hook + a glowing `sphere` `#ffcf7a`), candle `#fff8e6` |
   | `flick` | Flick | 0.55 | wings `#f2b45a`, eyespots `#ff8f7a`, fluff `#fff4e0`, a too-big cap `cyl` `#2b2238` with a brass badge `#c99a4a` sliding over one eye, toy pole `#8a5a30` with a paper lantern `#ff8f7a`; wings flutter at 3 Hz; never drowsy |
   | `wickling_taper` | Taper | 0.8 | wings `#8f7aa8`, fluff `#f3e6d0`, apron `#2b2238` with oil stains, a small oil can; drowsy |
   | `wickling_mallow` | Mallow | 0.75 | wings `#c9a46b`, cap `#3fa79a`, a punt pole; drowsy |
   | `wickling_dimmity` | Dimmity | 0.7 | wings `#7fb3a8`, one hand cupped to her mouth; drowsy |
   | `wickling_1`, `_2`, `_3` | A drowsy Wickling | 0.6-0.85 | wings `#d9a0a0` / `#8f7aa8` / `#c9a46b`, fluff `#f3e6d0`, aprons `#2b2238` / caps `#3fa79a` alternating; drowsy |

3. **Spots** (Wickhaven is a town: no enemies ever; R14): Granny Wick at the Long House door `(−34, 99.5)` on the
   deck, facing the square, except 18:00-21:00 at the Tower foot (124, −104) facing the Tower; Flick on Wick Square by
   the mast (−4, 84); Taper (−16, 82); Dimmity (0, 94); Mallow (−8, 50) on the north boardwalk; Wickling 1 (−50, 92);
   Wickling 2 (40, 90); Wickling 3 soaking in a hot pool (−116, −48). Each is an NPC spot in `W.npcSpots` (counts as a
   person POI).
4. **Dialogue** (`js/content/dialogue/duskwick.js`, text `js/text/en/duskwick.js`; speakers as the name plates):

   | Entry | NPC | When | Priority | Lines |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `flick.hello` (once) | flick | `!met:wick` | 80 | "Oh! A visitor! Nobody visits. Great-Granny will want to see you. She wants to see everybody. She can't see very well." (story.md#ch8) |
   | `flick.idle` | flick | `met:wick` | 10 | "Can I light it? The lamp? Any lamp? ...Great-Granny says not yet. She says that a lot." / "Can I light it? ...You've got a lamp. A GLOWING one. Can I hold it? No. Can I look at it? Yes. Looking." (rotation by day index) |
   | `wick.idle` | wick | `!flag:ch8` | 20 | "Hm. Hm. ...There. Lit again. My eyes aren't what they were, dear, but I can still see a light when one walks in." |
   | `wick.ch8` | wick | `flag:ch8`, `!flag:beat:beacon1` | 50 | "Sit down, dear, sit. Mind the moth on the chair; that's Flick's cousin." (story.md#act-II-reactions) |
   | `taper.idle` | wickling_taper | always | 10 | "...mmh... Lamp oil... half price... nobody's buying... mmh." |
   | `mallow.idle` | wickling_mallow | always | 10 | "...mmh... the punt can wait... everything can wait... mmh." |
   | `dimmity.before` | wickling_dimmity | `!flag:thiefSeen` | 20 | "...mmh... the Tower window went dark the night your Tree bloomed... everyone saw... nobody says... mmh." |
   | `dimmity.after` | wickling_dimmity | `flag:thiefSeen` | 20 | "...mmh... a light went straight UP off the Tower? I KNEW it. ...mmh. Knew what. Something." |
   | `wickling.idle` | wickling_1-3 | always | 10 | rotation: "...mmh... is it lamp time?" / "...mmh... the lamps are all so dim..." / "...mmh... five more minutes..." |

5. **Stilt-house doors** (`[E] Knock · Door` on the eight houses W1-E3 of S-084b): the 16 lines of
   [../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-interiors](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick-interiors) verbatim, the first
   column before `flag:act2done`, the second after (dormant until S-165). Text ids `cc.door.<house>.1/.2`.
6. **Sound cues and their twins:** Granny Wick: a match strike and a soft *fwup* every 30 s within 12 m (SFX
   `candleFwup`: `N({ t, d: 0.08, v: 0.08, type: 'highpass', f: 3000 }); N({ t: t + 0.1, d: 0.35, v: 0.05, type:
   'lowpass', f: 600 })`) with her pole lamp brightening (+0.4 emissive, 0.5 s); Flick: a fast wing-flutter buzz within
   6 m with her paper lantern flickering; the Wicklings: the `ambMoth` ambience of `duskwick_town` (S-085) with dusty
   glints when a wing moves.
7. **Charter:** add the reserved markers "Hm. Hm." (Granny Wick), "Can I light it?" (Flick), "...mmh..." (the
   Wicklings) and the name-use row: Granny Wick may say "Wenna" and "dear".

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject (Look line + palette) | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `portrait_wick` (`ui`, core, R36) | `portrait` · 512² webp ≤ 60 KB | "A tiny, very old moon moth grandmother with large pale mint wings with lilac eyespots ringed in gold folded around her like a cloak, a fluffy white collar, feathery antennae, round gold spectacles, a plum shawl, holding a tall brass lamplighter's pole with a tiny glowing lamp, a dry fond smile. Palette: #cfeedd #b8a6e0 #5a3d6b #f2c94c." | name only |
| `portrait_flick` (`ui`, `realm: 'duskwick'`) | same | "A small, fuzzy, wide-eyed young moth girl with orange-gold wings with coral eyespots, a lamplighter's cap far too big for her sliding over one eye, holding a toy pole with a paper lantern, bursting with excitement. Palette: #f2b45a #ff8f7a #2b2238 #fff4e0." | name only |

Turnarounds `docs/art/ref/wick.webp`, `docs/art/ref/flick.webp` (docs only). Commented slots `npc_wick` (1.1 m),
`npc_flick` (0.55 m), `npc_wickling` (0.75 m). ART BRIEFs `npc_wick`, `npc_flick`, `npc_wickling` from cast.md
pasted in the PR.

#### Acceptance criteria

- Every listed NPC stands at its spot in `?realm=duskwick`, reachable, facing as stated; Granny Wick moves to the
  Tower foot from 18:00 to 21:00 (debug clock).
- Talk lines follow the table and pass the lint with the new reserved markers; no enemy spawns within 12 m of
  Wickhaven's edge.
- The `moth` rig builds every look within its budget; the realm's 4 views stay in budget with the people present.

`bun test` (looks, lint, rig tris); `node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick` (add: NPC presence, Talk lines, the dusk move);
`node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

The ch8 scenes (S-091); Granny Wick's Library (S-167); the Wicklings' side stories (S-167, S-168).
