<a id="s-089"></a>

### S-089 · feat: Duskwick enemies: Sizzle Beetle and Coco Clonk

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-042, S-086b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden: true` (until
S-091) · Bible: [../bible/creatures.md#enemy-sizzle-beetle](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-sizzle-beetle),
[../bible/creatures.md#enemy-coco-clonk](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-coco-clonk),
[../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables), [../bible/creatures.md#telegraphs](../bible/creatures.md#telegraphs),
[../bible/creatures.md#bestiary](../bible/creatures.md#bestiary)

#### Why

The first new species since the shipped six: gentle, readable, beatable with the starter sword, and each teaching one
idea (make the charger hit a rock; step off the reticle). Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-089 (M12); vision
item **Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens**.

#### What

1. **`ENEMY.sizzle_beetle`** (realm species file `js/realms/duskwick/enemies.js`, the shape of
   [../bible/creatures.md#archetypes](../bible/creatures.md#archetypes)): name "Sizzle Beetle", archetype `charger`, HP
   4, r 0.46, ground, emerge `sand`, gems tier 1, poof `#ffb36b`, `realm: 'duskwick'`, params `{ windup: 0.65,
   speed: 7.5, dur: 0.8, reach: 6.8, guard: false, bonkOpen: 1.2, stripeLock: 0.25, missCool: 0.6, tier: 1 }`.
   Behaviour: scuttles; at ≤ 6.8 m it digs in, its belly glows brighter and it hisses for 0.65 s (the wind-up; the
   stripe is drawn from the start of the wind-up and locks 0.25 s before it goes); charges 7.5 m/s for 0.8 s; hitting a
   rock, wall, palm or any collider flips it on its back, legs waving, for 1.2 s (gold "Now!" opening); a clean miss
   leaves it cooling 0.6 s. If `js/ai/charger.js` lacks the obstacle bonk, add param `bonkOpen` (collider-hash test
   along the charge each step) without changing the crab's behaviour (`wp4`, `wp5` unchanged).
2. **`ENEMY.coco_clonk`**: name "Coco Clonk", archetype `lobber`, HP 3, r 0.40, ground (hops between palm bases),
   emerge `bush` (drops from a palm: the spawn point is the base of a palm instance in its region), gems tier 1, poof
   `#f3d7b0`, params `{ windup: 0.60, range: 12, volley: 1, hopBack: 2, hopBackT: 0.5, keep: [8, 12], tier: 1 }`.
   Behaviour: sits 8-12 m away under a palm, lifts a coconut over its head and wobbles for 0.60 s, then lobs a flat
   arc onto a shrinking reticle (the snowpuff idiom, flight `clamp(d / 8, 0.45, 1.1)` s); the coconut lands and cracks
   into two harmless halves (visual); if Pip comes within 3 m it hops back 2 m in 0.5 s before its next throw.
3. **Models** (`js/models.js` `enemyDef` cases, ≤ 1,200 tris and ≤ 6 materials each, drawn through `instRig`):
   - Sizzle Beetle: a dome shell (half `sphere`) `#2b2238` with a gold rim line `#f2b45a`, a small head with two cream
     highlight eyes `#fff3d8`, six stubby `capsule` legs `#4a3a50`, a glowing belly disc `#ff8f4a` (emissive); 0.55 m
     tall, 0.9 m long; leg patter, wind-up glow pulse, flipped-on-back wiggle.
   - Coco Clonk: a ball-shaped tan monkey body `#b07a4a`, a cream face disc `#f3d7b0`, round ears, half a coconut as a
     helmet `#6b4a2e` with a teal leaf tuft `#3fa79a`, eyes `#2a2440`; 0.7 m; coconut raise, throw, hop-back.
4. **Spawn tables** (R10; `W.regions` of Duskwick): `embersands` `[['sizzle_beetle', 4], ['coco_clonk', 2]]` max 6;
   `coralsteps` `[['coco_clonk', 3], ['sizzle_beetle', 2]]` max 5; `towerhead` `[['sizzle_beetle', 2], ['coco_clonk',
   2]]` max 5; `wickhaven` none, max 0; the dark zones (cells `W.dark`: the Tube and the Cupboard) `[['dimling', 1]]`
   max 3, spawning only inside dark cells. Spawning refuses any point within 12 m of a town region's edge.
5. **Bestiary** (`S.bestiary`, S-046): entries with Hoot's note and Clover's crayon verbatim from creatures.md:
   beetle — Hoot "The Sizzle Beetle warms its tummy on hot sand to keep its eggs cosy. It has no eggs. It just likes
   it." Clover "Make it charge into a rock. Then it's upside down!"; coco — Hoot "Coco Clonks wear coconuts as hats
   because the sun is very bright. Very sensible." Clover "Step off the circle. The coconut lands right in it."
6. **SFX** (wind-ups need a sound with a pitch rise, creatures.md first-person rules): `beetleHiss`: `N({ t, d: 0.65,
   v: 0.07, type: 'highpass', f: 2200, f1: 4200 })`; `cocoWobble`: `T({ t, f: 300, f1: 520, d: 0.6, v: 0.05, type:
   'triangle', vib: [9, 20] })`; `coconutCrack`: `N({ t, d: 0.12, v: 0.12, type: 'bandpass', f: 900 })`.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject (after preamble + `card-beast` suffix) | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `card_sizzle_beetle` (`ui`, `realm: 'duskwick'`) | 1024² → 384² webp q82 ≤ 40 KB | "A round black-and-gold dome beetle with a softly glowing ember-orange belly, little stubby legs, standing on warm black sand with a curl of steam, in its home on Duskwick's black dunes. Palette: #2b2238 #f2b45a #ff8f7a #3fa79a." | swatch disc + a blob in `#2b2238` with two dot eyes |
| `card_coco_clonk` | same | "A round little tan monkey with a cream face, wearing half a coconut shell as a helmet with a teal leaf on top, holding a coconut overhead under a palm, in its home on Duskwick's coral terraces. Palette: #2b2238 #f2b45a #ff8f7a #3fa79a." | same in `#b07a4a` |

ART BRIEFs `sizzle_beetle` and `coco_clonk` from creatures.md pasted in the PR (silhouette, size, palette, materials,
animation, poly 900 code / 3,000 GLB, the 3D prompt pattern); commented model slots `enemy_sizzle_beetle` (0.55 m),
`enemy_coco_clonk` (0.7 m) with `realm: 'duskwick'`.

#### Acceptance criteria

- For each species (in-page, `G.debug.actors.spawn('<id>')` in Duskwick): the telegraph is drawn ≥ 0.55 s before
  the first damage frame; every hit on Pip is exactly 1 HP; it poofs at 0 HP with its colour and counts in the
  bestiary; the beetle flips for 1.2 s after charging into a rock; the coco hops back when Pip is within 3 m.
- With the starter sword, a careful bot poofs a beetle in ≤ 12 s and a coco in ≤ 10 s in the sandbox (logged).
- Region spawns follow the table; nothing spawns in Wickhaven or within 12 m of it; Dimlings appear only in dark
  cells; at most 2 attackers wind up at once.
- `wp4` and `wp5` pass unchanged (the charger change is behaviour-identical for crabs).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Tone* (telegraphs, half-heart hits, poof), *Budgets per view*, *Assets*,
  *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (ENEMY schema lint: wind-up ≥ 0.55 s, damage 1, charter decision 22); `node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick`
(add the two species tests); `node tests/inpage.mjs wp4`, `wp5`; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Any other Act II species (their land steps).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Hidden realm until S-091.

<a id="s-090"></a>

### S-090 · feat: Mote's emissive figure and the Lamp Thief glimpse on the Tower stair

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-034, S-084b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/cast.md#mote](../bible/cast.md#mote), [../bible/story.md#ch8](../bible/story.md#ch8),
[../bible/charter.md#tone-mote](../bible/charter.md#tone-mote), [../bible/art-audio.md#cards](../bible/art-audio.md#cards)

#### Why

The first sight of the story's other lead: a little cloaked light on the Tower stair that flees straight up. It plants
the question every later act answers ("who is taking the lights?") without a villain: he is startled, not menacing.
Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-090 (M12); vision item **Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens**.

#### What

1. **The `starkin` rig family** (`js/models.js`; cast decision 25: unlit glowing materials and an additive ground glow,
   never a real point light): a capsule body (unlit, `glow: 1`, `#fff2c4`); a cloak shell (an open `lathe` cone,
   back faces rendered, `colorFn` patch grid of `#2a3358` with patches `#7fd3d0`, `#c9b6ec`, `#e9d8b0`, `#cfeedd`);
   a hood (half `sphere` shell, toggled); a face disc (`cyl` 0.02 m, `#fff8e6`) with two `INK` dot eyes and a small
   `torus`-arc mouth `#c98b4b`; three `cyl` firefly jars at the hem (`glow: 1`, `#f2c94c`); a brooch (`cyl` `#ffe9a8`
   with a dark `box` crack `#8a6a3a`); no feet (the hem tapers into a wisp cone); an additive `fx_mote_glow` sprite at
   the chest and a ground-glow decal. Hover 0.2 m with a 0.3 m / 2 s bob. States: `cloaked` (hood up, the face disc at
   0.15 brightness except two eye glints), `open`, `bright` (core `#ffffff`, glow `#ffe9a8`). Flicker: emissive
   0.6-1.0 at 9 Hz for 0.8 s; spark tears from a pooled emitter of ≤ 24 sprites. ≤ 1,500 tris code-built.
   Look record `mote: { rig: 'starkin', h: 1.0, hover: 0.2, state: 'cloaked' }`; flags on the actor `hittable: false`,
   `lockable: false` (charter decision 21: Pip never hits Mote).
2. **The Tower door sets a flag:** S-084b's `[E] Knock · Tower Door` also applies `set:towerKnocked` (story kind;
   register it). Chapter 8's step `door` reads it (S-091).
3. **Scene `ch8-thief`** (`js/content/scenes/duskwick.js`; R15, R40): `start: 'enter:'` the 2 m disc at
   `W.sites.towerF4` (the top of the fourth flight, h 26), `when: ['flag:towerKnocked', '!flag:thiefSeen', 'calm']`,
   14 s, replayable. Steps (story.md#ch8's block with the R15 anchors):

   ```js
   steps: [
     { emit: 'sfx.mote' },
     { emit: 'spawn', actor: 'thief', model: 'mote', at: 'w:site:towerLedge', state: 'cloaked' },
     { shot: 'look', at: 'actor:thief', dur: 0.5 },
     { wait: 0.8 },
     { pose: 'thief', anim: 'startle' },
     { emit: 'actor.path', actor: 'thief', to: 'xyz:+0,+60,+0', s: 2.5, go: true },   // straight up from the ledge
     { shot: 'look', at: 'actor:thief', dur: 2.5 },
     { emit: 'sky.streak', from: 'actor:thief', to: 'sky:skyIsle', go: true },
     { emit: 'despawn', actor: 'thief' },
     { say: 'card', t: 'cc.scene.thief.1', text: 'A little cloaked light, and then nothing. It went straight up.', wait: 2.5 },
   ],
   after: ['set:thiefSeen', 'journal:thief']
   ```
   `startle`: the figure jolts 0.15 m back, flickers (the 9 Hz flicker) and the hood dips. In first person the `look`
   shots turn the camera up toward the ledge 6 m above (no roll); in third person the same targets.
4. **Journal "the Lamp Thief"** (`journal:thief`): the Bestiary tab gains a **Friends** heading (art-audio decision
   29; no poof count) with the entry titled "The Lamp Thief" until `moteNamed`: card `card_mote`, Hoot's note
   `cc.journal.thief` "The Lamp Thief. A little cloaked light, seen once, going up. I have started a page. It is mostly
   a drawing of up." (no Clover crayon yet).
5. **Sound:** `sfx.mote` (S-066's rising glass trill) at spawn; while the actor exists, a music-box tinkle emitter
   (SFX `moteTinkle`: `bell(t, 1568 + 400 * k, 0.4, 0.2)` every 0.6 s, `k` = closeness 0-1 within 20 m) with the glow
   pool as its twin.
6. **Flags:** register `thiefSeen` (story, `setBy: 'ch8 S-090'`), `towerKnocked`.

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `portrait_mote_cloaked`, `portrait_mote`, `portrait_mote_cross`, `portrait_mote_bright` (`ui`, core) | one `portrait` 2 × 2 sheet generated at 2048², sliced with `slice_sheet.py`, each 512² webp ≤ 60 KB | the cast.md#mote sheet prompt verbatim ("Four portraits of the same small star-child made of soft pale-gold light … top-left hood up, face hidden in shadow except two glinting eyes; top-right hood down, earnest and a little sad; bottom-left cross and flickering, sparks falling like tears; bottom-right glowing bright white-gold and smiling …") + "Palette: #fff2c4 #2a3358 #7fd3d0 #f2c94c." | a canvas radial gradient `#fff2c4` → transparent |
| `fx_mote_glow` (`billboards`, core) | `card` · 256² webp alpha ≤ 20 KB, additive | "A soft round pale-gold glow with a faint warm halo, centred. Palette: #fff2c4 #ffcf7a." | canvas radial gradient |
| `card_mote` (`ui`, core) | `card-beast` · 384² webp ≤ 40 KB | "A soft cloaked little light in a patched navy-violet cloak, two glinting eyes under the hood, standing in a meadow lit by many small scrap lamps at night. Palette: #fff2c4 #2a3358 #7fd3d0 #f2c94c." | swatch disc + a gold blob |

ART BRIEF `npc_mote` (cast.md, batch 14 owner GLB) pasted in the PR; commented slot `npc_mote` (1.0 m); turnaround
`docs/art/ref/mote.webp`. (Portraits are generated now so every later Mote beat has them; only `_cloaked` is used in
M12, by the Friends entry.)

#### Acceptance criteria

- In Duskwick (debug), after knocking on the Tower door, walking up to F4's top plays `ch8-thief` once; watched or
  skipped it sets `thiefSeen` and adds the Friends entry; replay works from the Lore tab.
- The figure is visible against the day sky from F4 in both cameras (screenshot in the PR) and is never targetable or
  hittable (the in-page test swings at it and asserts no hit, charter decision 21).
- No real point light is added (the light pool count is unchanged during the scene).
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Tone* (Pip never hits Mote; nothing scary), *Precache* (+≤ 300 KB core),
  *Assets*, *Original IP only*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (scene validation, flags); `node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick` (add: knock → climb → scene → flags; swing
test; light-pool count).

#### Out of scope

The Act II glimpses on other isles (cast decision 31, with each isle); II.M (S-128); Mote speaking (S-128).

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Hidden realm until S-091.

<a id="s-090b"></a>

### S-090b · feat: Mote's traces: a scrap lamp and a note signed M by each dark beacon

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-090, S-034, S-038 · Size: M (about 260 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#motes-traces](../bible/story.md#motes-traces), [../bible/cast.md#mote](../bible/cast.md#mote),
[../bible/systems.md#journal](../bible/systems.md#journal)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): between ch8's glimpse and II.M the first isle a player visits
(4-6 hours) had no trace of the Lamp Thief, so the main mystery stalled.

#### Why

Part of **Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens**. Each isle's dark beacon gets a small sign that someone was there
first: a scorched spiral and a jam-jar lamp holding a note signed "M". The notes are keyed to how many beacons are lit,
never to which isle, so they read in order in every isle order and the antagonist's voice arrives before his name.

#### What

1. **Props** (`js/props.js`, code-built):
   - `prop_scrap_lamp`: a jam jar 0.25 m tall (`lathe` profile r 0.07-0.09, glass material `#e8f0f2` at opacity 0.45),
     a wire handle (`tube` torus half, r 0.06, `#8a8f98`), a folded paper inside (`box` 0.05 × 0.08 × 0.01, `#f3ead8`),
     the glow off (an emissive core `#f2b45a` at intensity 0, to show the lamp was emptied). ≤ 220 tris, one draw with
     the props instancer. Collider none (a 0.4 m interact radius).
   - `decal_scorch_spiral`: a canvas decal 1.6 m, a three-turn spiral in soot `#3a2f2a` at alpha 0.55 with an ember edge
     `#c9772e` at alpha 0.25, laid flat with polygon offset; one per trace.
2. **Note runner** (`js/content/mote-notes.js`, pure data + `js/mote-traces.js` runtime):
   ```js
   export const MOTE_NOTES = [
     { id: 'mote-note-1', when: 'beacons<1', text: 'mote.note.1' },  // "Borrowed: one light. I will give it back. I always mean to. — M"
     { id: 'mote-note-2', when: 'beacons<2', text: 'mote.note.2' },  // "You lit one again. Why? She needs it more than you. — M"
     { id: 'mote-note-3', when: 'tower', text: 'mote.note.3' },      // "Only one more. The oldest one. Then she can rest. — M"
   ];
   ```
   - A trace record `{ id: '<realm>:mote-trace', x, z }` in the realm's `W.storyMarks` (placed by each isle's chain
     step with one row: S-106a, S-127a, S-148a). `[E] Read · A note` on the scrap lamp picks the note to show:
     with `beacons == 0` note 1; with `beacons == 1` note 2 if note 1 has been read, else note 1; with `beacons >= 2`
     (after II.M) the first unread of notes 1-2, else the line "An empty jam jar. It's still a little warm." Reading
     sets `seen:mote-note-<n>`. **Nothing is missable**: a player who skipped the first isle's lamp reads note 1 at the
     second, and both remain in the journal.
   - Journal: each read note adds a Lore entry "A note signed M" (`journal:mote-note-<n>`, the note text plus Hoot's
     margin line: note 1 "Hoo. Neat handwriting. Small. Someone who writes a lot of letters and sends none."; note 2
     "He writes 'she'. Who is she?"; note 3 "Hoo. 'Rest.' I do not like that word here."). Before `moteNamed` the
     signature shows as "— M"; after, the Lore entries are grouped under "Mote".
   - The scorched spiral stays for good; Look on it: "A scorched spiral. Someone warm sat here, and not long ago."
   - Note 3 is placed by S-163 on the lamp-room door of the Lamplighters' Tower (one row, `tower:mote-trace`); it is
     read before the ch12 fight and uses the same runner (`when: 'tower'` always shows note 3).
3. **Text** (`js/text/en/mote.js`): the three notes verbatim above (each ≤ 220 characters, signed "— M"), the empty
   jar line, the scorch line and Hoot's three margin lines.
4. **Debug:** `G.debug.mote.traces()` lists placed traces and their next note; `G.debug.flags.set('beacons', n)` is
   enough to test the keying.
5. **Tests**
   - `tests/mote-notes.test.js`: the note picked for beacons 0, 1, 2 and 3 with each read-state combination; note 3
     only at the Tower; nothing returns undefined.
   - In-page (`duskwick` suite, case `mote-trace`): a debug trace placed on Duskwick's Candle Knoll ring (removed
     after the test) reads note 1, then with `beacons = 1` note 2, then the empty-jar line; the Lore tab lists two
     entries.

#### Assets

- `prop_scrap_lamp` code-built. **ART BRIEF** (`docs/roadmap/briefs/` via the M12 batch): silhouette a squat jam jar
  with a wire bail handle and a folded note inside, 0.25 m, palette `#e8f0f2` glass, `#8a8f98` wire, `#f3ead8` paper,
  `#f2b45a` cold wick; materials glass (alpha 0.45), metal, paper; ≤ 600 tris GLB; pose upright, handle raised; image
  prompt "[style preamble] a small empty glass jam jar with a wire handle, a folded handwritten note inside, sitting
  on dark stone with a faint scorched spiral around it, soft painterly storybook style, warm dusk light, transparent
  background, 1024 × 1024"; 3D prompt "[3D preamble] a small glass jam jar with a wire bail handle and a folded paper
  note inside, 0.25 m, hand-painted toon storybook prop, under 600 triangles, origin at the base centre".
- `decal_scorch_spiral`: canvas (no generated art).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Notes read in beacon order whatever the isle order (unit test over all six isle orders, using the trace rows'
  realms); a skipped lamp never loses a note.
- Each note is ≤ 220 characters and signed "— M"; Pip never speaks.
- The prop adds no draw call beyond the props instancer at the Duskwick test view.
- Invariants: *Frozen realms* (traces are post-pass `storyMarks` rows only), *Tone* (nothing missable), *Assets*,
  *Original IP only*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/mote-notes.test.js tests/content.test.js && bun test
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick
```

#### Out of scope

Placing the three isle traces (one row each in S-106a, S-127a, S-148a) and the Tower note (S-163). Mote in the present
during Act III (S-212d).

#### Risk & rollback

Additive data, one prop and one runtime module. Revert the PR.

<a id="s-091"></a>

### S-091 · feat: chapter 8 Duskwick: the Chart, Lamp Log 1 and the locked Tower; Duskwick opens

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-088, S-090, S-081, S-030, S-037b · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: `REALMS.duskwick.hidden` → `false` ·
Bible: [../bible/story.md#ch8](../bible/story.md#ch8), [../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions),
[../bible/lore.md#myth](../bible/lore.md#myth), [../bible/lore.md#log-1](../bible/lore.md#log-1),
[../bible/systems.md#chart](../bible/systems.md#chart), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#horizon](../bible/atlas-isles.md#horizon)

#### Why

Act II begins: Pip sails for the first time, meets the last Lamplighter, learns what a beacon is, receives the
Lamplighters' chart and the first page of their book, and sees something leave the Tower. Duskwick unhides in this
PR, with everything behind it already built. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-091 (M12); vision item
**Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens**.

#### What

1. **Unhide** `duskwick` (`REALMS.duskwick.hidden = false`). Its chart node is `open` (`flag:ch7` holds), so
   `[E] Sail · the Dandelion` now sails there (S-081).
2. **Quest `mq-ch8`** (`js/content/quests/duskwick.js`; R1; n 8, kind `main`, realm `duskwick`, title "Duskwick",
   owner `wick`, start `flag:ch7`, `set: ['set:ch8']`, hints `ht.ch8.*`):

   | Step | Journal text | Done | On | Target | Hint nudge | Hint answer |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `arrive` | Sail to Duskwick. (short "Sail to Duskwick") | `realm:duskwick.visited` | `REALM` | realm `duskwick` (the compass points at home's jetty) | Nell knows the way. | Talk to Nell at the jetty and pick Duskwick on the chart. |
   | `meet` | Find out why Wickhaven is so sleepy. | `flag:scene:ch8-granny` | `SCENE_END` | `npc:wick` | The moths all point the same way when they yawn. | Granny Wick lives in the Long House at the south-west corner of Wick Square. She stands at its door by day. (R14) |
   | `door` | Visit the Lamplighters' Tower. | `flag:towerKnocked` | `TALK_END` | the Tower door | It's the tallest thing on the isle. | Walk north-east to Tower Head and try the door. |
   | `stair` | Climb the Tower's outside stair. | `flag:thiefSeen` | `SCENE_END` | `W.sites.towerF4` | The door won't open, but the stair goes up. | Follow the wooden stair that zig-zags up the Tower's west side. (R40) |
   | `report` | Tell Granny Wick what you saw. | `flag:seen:cc.wick.report` | `TALK_END` | `npc:wick` | Someone old will want to hear this. | Go back to Granny Wick in Wickhaven. |

   The chapter banner "Chapter 8 · Duskwick" (S-067's rule) shows when the quest starts.
3. **Scene `ch8-granny`** (start `talk:wick` while `quest:mq-ch8>=1` and `!flag:scene:ch8-granny`; 60 s; replayable),
   at the Long House door (R14): `fade out 0.3` · `warp pip` 2.2 m in front of her facing her · `fade in 0.5` · `shot
   two at npc:wick` · five `say wick` lines `cc.scene.granny.01`-`.05` verbatim from
   [../bible/story.md#ch8](../bible/story.md#ch8) ("A little one in a green hood. With a light in his hand. ...Oh, my.
   That's her light, isn't it?" … "Take this. The old Lamplighters' chart. And this, the first page of our logbook.
   Read it when you're somewhere cozy.") · `emit item {id: 'chart', text: "You got the Lamplighter's Chart! The isles
   of the Wide Blue, drawn by the Lamplighters. Open it with {map}."}` · `emit item {id: 'log:1'}` · `shot pov`.
   `after: ['give:chart', 'give:log:1', 'collect:log:duskwick:1']`. (Story italics dropped; text tables are plain.)
4. **Items** (`js/items.js`): `chart` (kind `key`, icon `icons_items.chart`, desc "The Lamplighters' chart of the Wide
   Blue. Open the map's World tab with {map}."), `log:1` (kind `key`, the page; reading it from Items or Lore shows Lamp
   Log 1). **Lamp Log 1** "The Song of the Sea-Caught Star" (Keeper Taper): its body is the eight myth lines
   `lore.myth.1`-`.8` verbatim from [../bible/lore.md#myth](../bible/lore.md#myth) (add them to `js/text/en/lore.js`
   if S-038 did not), sign "— copied from the old song by Keeper Taper"; the Lore tab's "The Myth" page shows the same
   eight lines once `collect.log['duskwick:1']` is set.
5. **The report** (`wick.report`, priority 100, `quest:mq-ch8>=4`, once, sets `seen:cc.wick.report`): "Straight up, you
   say? Then somebody knows the way up. Nobody's known the way up in a hundred years." / "The Tower can wait; whoever
   locked it isn't coming out today. Wake the other isles first, dear. Three lights should be enough to wake the old
   lamp." Quest done → `ch8`.
6. **After `ch8`:** the chart is in `chart` mode and lists Pinewhistle, Archwater and Threemills (fog: hidden). The
   objective reads "Relight a beacon" (target: the nearest jetty) only while one of those three isles is unhidden;
   otherwise it reads "To be continued ✿" and Wenna's S3 lines carry the story. **Remove S-068b's `tbc` state** in this
   PR: the `mq-ch8-tbc` placeholder row, the pre-ch8 "To be continued ✿" compass text and Nell's fog line (her
   "Lamp's on the mast" line stays), since Duskwick is now unhidden.
7. **Reactions at home** (the ch8 column of [../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-II-reactions),
   priority 50, `when: ['flag:ch8', '!flag:beat:beacon1']`, verbatim): Shellby "Granny Wick! Is she still... well, of
   course she is. Moths are stubborn. Give her my best." · Ribbit "A whole other isle! With moths! I hereby declare
   them our friends. Pending paperwork." · Miso "Duskwick? My brother sails those waters. If you see a boat covered in
   junk, don't buy anything~" · Clover "A whole new isle! Can you draw me a map? A little one? On anything?" · Hoot
   "Moth folk! Lamplighters! My paper on them was thought to be fiction. By me, mostly." · Digby "Moths, is it? Soft
   things. I bumped into one once. Very polite about it." · Nell "Granny Wick waved at us from the jetty. Or swatted a
   fly. Hard to say with moths."
8. **Duskwick's sky** (`js/sky-home.js` grows a per-realm table `SKY_TABLES`; home's rows unchanged): from Duskwick,
   `horizon_home` (new, below) at bearing 337.5° width 200, `horizon_isle_b` (Archwater) at 45° width 165,
   `horizon_isle_d` (Threemills) at 292.5° width 190 (atlas-isles#horizon; Pinewhistle is hidden behind home); their
   window rules are home's (dark until each beacon); `horizon_home`'s window `[0.50, 0.22, 0.05]` (the crown) lights at
   night when `lanterns>=1` (dormant until S-106). The sky isle stays NE. On home, card c's tower window stays dark
   (S-064's rule) until `beacon:duskwick`.
9. **Chapter test `ch8`** (`tests/fixtures/saves/ch8-start.json` = ch7's end with `mq-ch8: 0`; spec
   `tests/fixtures/chapters/ch8.json`): `sail duskwick` → `talk wick` → `scene.skip` → `warpTo` the Tower door → `use`
   → `warpTo` `towerF4` → `wait 2` → `scene.skip` → `talk wick`; expect flags `ch8`, `thiefSeen`, `towerKnocked`,
   items `{ chart: 1, 'log:1': 1 }`, quests `{ 'mq-ch8': 'done' }`, collect `{ log: ['duskwick:1'] }`, lanterns 0.
   Update ch7's end fixture (`mq-ch8: 0` now starts at `ch7`) and the chain test; add `ch8` to the CI chapter matrix.
10. **Flags:** register `ch8` (story, `setBy: 'ch8 S-091'`).

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `icons_items` row 2 (`ui`, core) | `sheet-items` N = 8, appended with `tools/pack_atlas.py --append`: `gloves`, `cloudstep`, `sword_wood`, `sword_shiny`, `sword_starsteel`, `rod`, `chart`, `chart_clover` | the cell subjects of [../bible/art-audio.md#ui](../bible/art-audio.md#ui) row 2, "Palette: #8aa83c #c98b4b #fdf6e3 #ffd98a." | glyphs |
| `horizon_home` (`billboards`, core) | `horizon` · 1024 × 307 webp alpha ≤ 60 KB | "A far-away round green island with one enormous gentle tree rising from its middle, a tiny striped lighthouse on its southern shore and a little windmill on its west side. Palette: #6f8f3a #8a5a3c #f7b6c8 #f3e3c3." | `F-none` |

#### Acceptance criteria

- New Game → … → ch7's end → sail to Duskwick → the whole chapter plays with no debug help; `node tests/chapter.mjs
  ch8` passes and the chain test `ch7` → `ch8` passes.
- `REALMS.duskwick.hidden === false`; the realm suite, the coverage test and the `duskwick` suite pass; the chart
  shows Duskwick open and the three isles as fog.
- Skipping `ch8-granny` still gives exactly one Chart and Lamp Log 1; Lamp Log 1 reads the eight myth lines.
- Every home villager and Nell has their ch8 line; Wenna's S3 lines play.
- S-068b's "To be continued ✿" compass text, journal placeholder and Nell's fog line are gone (grep finds no
  `mq-ch8-tbc`); after `ch8` with the three isles hidden the compass reads "To be continued ✿".
- From Duskwick at night the Great Tree card stands N-NW and the Archwater and Threemills cards are dark.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Hidden until whole* (Duskwick is whole: people, enemies, finds, art; the three
  isles stay fog), *Frozen realms* (Duskwick's prints unchanged from here on: later content only through post-passes),
  *Budgets per view*, *Assets*, *Tone*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (quest lint with hints, chain test, sky tables); `node tests/chapter.mjs ch7`, `ch8`;
`node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick`, `realm`, `wenna`; `bun run test:playthrough`; `bun run test:smoke`.

Kinako (S-092); the Hollows (S-093); the isle quests and Lamp Log 2 (S-106); Granny Wick's Library (S-167).

One revert re-hides Duskwick (saves that reached it keep their flags; a hidden realm id in `S.realm` lands on home at
the Tree, S-024's rule).

<a id="s-091b"></a>

### S-091b · feat: The Lamp Round: Pip relights Wickhaven's eight stilt lamps so the moths wake and Granny Wick's door opens

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-091 · Size: M (about 380 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#set-pieces](../bible/story.md#set-pieces), [../bible/story.md#ch8](../bible/story.md#ch8),
[../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick](../bible/atlas-isles.md#duskwick), [../bible/systems.md#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): ch8 was four talk-and-walk steps priced at 2 hours with no set
piece. Filed **after** S-091 (Duskwick is open), so it adds a `before` sub-objective and never shifts a saved step
index (INDEX §1.8).

#### Why

Part of **Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens**. The first new land should open with something to do, not only
someone to talk to: Pip carries Wenna's light along the stilt-town's deck walk at dusk and relights the town lamps one
by one, and the drowsy moths wake as each lamp catches, until the oldest moth's door opens. 15-25 minutes, no fail
state, and it shows the Lantern's job in the new act.

#### What

1. **Quest `before` field** (`js/quests.js`, the first user; later set pieces reuse it): a step may carry
   `before: [{ id, until, text, short, target, hint: [nudge, answer], minutes }]`. While the step is current, the
   journal and compass show the first entry whose `until` condition does not hold yet (text, short, target, hint),
   and fall back to the step's own text once all hold. The step's `done` condition is unchanged, so saves keep their
   indices. `tests/quests.test.js`: a step with two `before` entries shows them in order and then its own text; a save
   already past the step never sees them.
2. **Data** (`js/content/quests/duskwick.js`): step `meet` gains
   ```js
   before: [{ id: 'lampround', until: 'flag:cc.lampRound', minutes: 20,
     text: 'qt.ch8.lamps', short: 'qt.ch8.lamps.short', target: 'w:duskwick.lamp-next',
     hint: ['ht.ch8.lamps.n', 'ht.ch8.lamps.a'] }]
   ```
   Texts: journal "Relight Wickhaven's stilt lamps ({n}/8)." · compass "Relight the stilt lamps" · nudge "The lamps
   along the deck walk are dark, and so are the moths." · answer "Walk the deck walk from the jetty with the Lantern
   lit and press {interact} at each dark lamp post."
3. **Eight stilt lamps** (Duskwick post-pass `placeIsleStories`, `new RNG(40211 * 73 + 7)`, appended rows
   `duskwick:lamp-1..8`; shared kit `kit_lamp_post` 2.6 m, cold): one at the jetty head (−8, 98), then along the deck
   walk at the nearest deck cell to each of (−20, 92), (−30, 84), (−22, 74), (−6, 72), (8, 78), (14, 90), and the last
   on the Long House landing (−34, 104). Each `[E] Kindle · Lamp` (Lantern lit; unlit Lantern: "The lamp wants a
   flame."): the wick takes over 0.6 s (`fx.kindle` small, `sfx.kindle` at 0.6 volume), warm light `#ffd27a` r 6 m
   from then on (one shared point-light budget: the four nearest lit lamps get real lights, the rest an emissive card),
   and the two to three moth NPCs whose spots are nearest lift their heads, stretch and flutter once (S-088's
   `wake` idle; add it to the Wickling rig here if S-088 did not) and from then on use their awake idle. Order is free; `target: 'w:duskwick.lamp-next'` points at the
   nearest unlit lamp. Sets `cc.lamp<k>` and `cc.lamps` (counter).
4. **Flick guides** (priority 90, while `cc.lamps < 8` and the step is current): on arrival Flick says "Oh! A visitor
   with a light! Could you... the lamps, I mean. We can't wake up without them. Nobody's had a flame since the night
   the Tower went out." After lamp 4: "Halfway! I can feel my wings again. Mostly the left one." Flick flies 3 m
   ahead of Pip to the next unlit lamp (S-152a's guide rule, using a simple straight hop if `js/npc-guide.js` is not
   on the branch yet).
5. **The eighth lamp** (scene `ch8-lampround`, 18 s, replayable): the camera rises over Wick Square; every lamp on
   the walk pulses once in sequence; the moths on the decks open their wings together (a 1.2 s `wingsOpen` on every
   Wickling, staggered by distance); the Long House door's latch clicks and the door swings open; Granny Wick's voice
   from inside: "Is that a flame I smell? Come in, little one, come in. Mind the step; it's older than I am."
   `after: ['set:cc.lampRound']`. Until then her door shows `[E] Knock · Long House` "No answer. Somebody inside is
   snoring, very politely."
6. **Dusk**: the round can be done any time; if `cc.lampRound` is set by day the lamps still burn, and from then on
   Wickhaven's lamps light at 17:24 each evening (the restored-town state stays for good).
7. **Saves already past `meet`**: on load, if `quest:mq-ch8>=2`, set `cc.lampRound` and `cc.lamps = 8` silently so the
   town is lit (nothing earned is taken; the round is not replayable as a quest, but its scene is in the Lore tab).
8. **Chapter test** `ch8` (S-091's spec): insert `G.debug.duskwick.lightAll()` (kindles the eight in order with the
   real interaction) before `talk wick`; expect `cc.lampRound`, `cc.lamps = 8`. **Debug:** `G.debug.duskwick.lightAll()`,
   `G.debug.duskwick.unlightAll()`.

#### Assets

- Reuses `kit_lamp_post` (S-082) and the shipped kindle effect. No new art. The lamp's lit glow uses `horizon_window`
  as an additive card (fallback: canvas gradient).

#### Acceptance criteria

- On a ch8 save at step `meet` without the flag, the journal shows "Relight Wickhaven's stilt lamps (0/8)" and the
  compass points at the nearest unlit lamp; each kindle advances the count, wakes nearby moths and lights the lamp for
  good; the eighth plays `ch8-lampround` and opens Granny Wick's door; then `ch8-granny` plays as before.
- A save already past `meet` loads with the town lit and no `before` text.
- Draw calls at Duskwick's town view stay ≤ 200 main pass with all eight lamps lit (lamp cards instanced).
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch8` passes with the new script.
- Invariants: *Frozen realms* (lamps are post-pass `storyMarks` rows), *Budgets per view*, *Tone* (no fail state;
  Pip never speaks), *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/quests.test.js tests/content.test.js && bun test
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick
node tests/chapter.mjs ch8
```

#### Out of scope

Later set pieces (S-106c, S-127c, S-148c, S-203b, S-232c, S-254c) reuse the `before` field. Granny Wick's Library
interior (S-167a).

#### Risk & rollback

One quest field and an additive post-pass. Rollback: revert; ch8 returns to S-091's shape and the town stays dim
until the beacon.

<a id="s-092"></a>

### S-092 · feat: Kinako's Floating Bazaar docks at Duskwick

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-091, S-078 · Size: M · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/cast.md#kinako](../bible/cast.md#kinako), [../bible/systems.md#economy](../bible/systems.md#economy),
[../bible/systems.md#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools), [../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-small](../bible/atlas-isles.md#brief-duskwick-small)

#### Why

Miso's twin brother brings the first gem sinks beyond home (a heart container and a brighter Lantern), a second shop
voice ("limited edition!!") and the start of the twins' story. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-092 (M12);
vision item **Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens**.

#### What

1. **Kinako** (look record; the shipped `cat` rig case generalised to read a look record, with `npcDef('miso')`'s
   geometry unchanged, checked by a vertex-count test): `kinako: { rig: 'cat', h: 1.1, eyes: 'open', pal: { fur:
   '#e2c79a', stripes: '#b8894a', muzzle: '#fff0d8', waistcoat: '#6b4a8c', trim: '#f5c040', neckerchief: '#3fa79a',
   cap: '#2b2238', badge: '#f2c94c', pins: ['#ff8f7a', '#7fd3d0', '#f2c94c'] }, acc: ['waistcoat', 'pins',
   'neckerchief', 'captainCap'] }` (the cap carries a small gold star badge, R26). He stands on the bazaar pier at
   (20, 108) from `realm:duskwick.visited` (R38). Speaker "Kinako"; marker `!!` (already reserved for him).
2. **The bazaar boat** (`duskwick_bazaar_boat`, code-built, moored at (24, 114)): a wide round-bellied sailing barge
   12 × 5 m, a striped awning `#e8c46a` / `#b55d4c`, crates, a mast with striped bunting, and a hand-painted sign as a
   canvas texture reading "Kinako's Floating Bazaar — limited edition!!"; ≤ 3,500 tris; no collider on the hull beyond
   the pier's. Sound cue: a brass bazaar bell clanging every 20 s, audible within 150 m (SFX `bazaarBell`:
   `bell(t, 1175, 0.8, 0.5); bell(t + 0.25, 1175, 0.8, 0.4)`), its twin the bunting snapping on the mast.
3. **The shop** (the shipped shop modal with a `seller` field; `js/content/shops/kinako.js`; R37):

   | Item id | Name | Price | In stock when | Once | Description (≤ 120) | Effect |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `potion` | Potion | 30 | always | no (carry cap as Miso's) | "Heals every heart!! Limited edition!! (It's a potion.)" | +1 `S.potions` |
   | `kinako_heart` | Heart Container | 300 | always | yes | "+1 max heart!! The only one on the whole boat!!" | `heart` (maxHp +2 up to 32, full hp) |
   | `bright_wick` | Bright Wick | 400 | `item:lantern` | yes | "Your Lantern glows 9 m instead of 6!! Warm, bright, extremely limited!!" | the Lantern's light and ghost radius 6 → 9 m |
   | `swift_fluff` | Swift Fluff | 400 | `ability:glide` | yes | "Glide faster!! 7.5 m/s!! Fluffier than it looks!!" | read by the Glider (S-102) |
   | `quick_draw` | Sling quick-draw | 300 | `ability:sling` | yes | "Draw your Sling twice as fast!! Twice!!" | read by the Sling (S-227) |

   Lines: open "What'll it be, valued customer!!" · bought heart "Another heart!! You're practically a heart CONTAINER
   now!!" · bought wick "Ooh, look at it GLOW!!" · bought potion "A potion!! Bottoms up!! Later!!" · not enough gems
   "Not enough gems!! Come back richer!!" · sold out "Sold out!! It was limited!! I did say!!" · potions full "Your
   pockets are full of potions!! Limited pockets!!".
4. **Talk** (priority 80 once, sets `met:kinako`): "A CUSTOMER!! Welcome aboard Kinako's Floating Bazaar!! Everything
   here is limited edition!!" / "You've met Miso? Don't tell her where I am!! ...Actually, do. No. Don't!!"; idle
   (priority 10): "Everything on this boat is limited edition!! ...There's only one of each, you see." Then `[E] Shop ·
   Kinako`.
5. **Upgrades wired now:** S-057's Lantern light radius and the ghost-archetype light radius read
   `has(S, 'bright_wick') ? 9 : 6`; `swift_fluff` and `quick_draw` exist as items only.
6. **Economy:** `js/content/economy.js` reads Kinako's table; `tests/economy.test.js` prints the shipped ratio (R41:
   ≈ 1,350 / 1,310 with drops, warn only) and the planned 113.9 %.
7. **Flags:** `met:kinako` (family).

#### Assets

| Key | Kind · size | Subject | Fallback |
|---|---|---|---|
| `portrait_kinako` (`ui`, core) | `portrait` · 512² webp ≤ 60 KB | "A sandy-beige tabby cat merchant with toffee stripes and wide sparkling eyes, a flashy purple waistcoat with gold trim covered in colourful pin badges, a teal neckerchief, a small dark captain's cap with a small gold star badge, holding up a shiny spoon like a treasure, huge salesman grin. Palette: #e2c79a #6b4a8c #3fa79a #f5c040." | name only |
| `icons_items` row 5 cells `bright_wick`, `swift_fluff`, `quick_draw` (`ui`, core) | appended cells (the rest of row 5 later) | the art-audio row-5 subjects | glyphs |

ART BRIEFs `npc_kinako` (cast.md) and `duskwick_bazaar_boat` (atlas-isles) pasted in the PR; turnaround
`docs/art/ref/kinako.webp`; commented slots `npc_kinako` (1.1 m) and `prop_duskwick_bazaar_boat` (`realm:
'duskwick'`, 7 m).

#### Acceptance criteria

- After arriving in Duskwick, Kinako and the boat are at the pier; the bell is heard across the lagoon.
- The shop lists Potion, Heart Container and (with the Lantern) Bright Wick; Swift Fluff and quick-draw do not show.
- Buying the heart adds a whole heart (two rows of hearts render correctly at 9+ hearts); buying the wick makes the
  Lantern's light reach 9 m (measured) and Dimlings hittable at 8.5 m.
- `npcDef('miso')` builds exactly as before (vertex count unchanged); `tests/economy.test.js` passes.
- Every Kinako line contains `!!` and no `~`; Miso's never contain `!!`.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Tone*, *Precache* (+≤ 80 KB core), *Assets*, *Original IP only*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test` (shop lint: price, stock condition, desc ≤ 120; economy; Miso geometry); `node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick`
(add: Talk, shop stock with and without the Lantern, buy heart and wick, insufficient-gems line);
`node tests/inpage.mjs wp6` (hearts).

#### Out of scope

The day-by-day dock rotation (M13, R38); Kinako's furnishings and trims (S-302, S-303); ss-03 and ss-18.

#### Risk & rollback

One revert. Purchases already made keep their items (nothing earned is taken back); a revert only hides the shop.

<a id="s-093"></a>

### S-093 · feat: Duskwick's two Hollows

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-073, S-086a, S-074 · Size: S · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h03](../bible/dungeons.md#h03),
[../bible/dungeons.md#h04](../bible/dungeons.md#h04)

#### Why

One heart petal and one gem bag behind Duskwick's two spiral doors: a Lantern puzzle in the dunes and a two-block
puzzle in the coral. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-093 (M12); vision item **Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II
opens**.

#### What

1. **`hollow_duskwick_1` "The Ember-Sand Hollow"** (`js/realms/hollows/hollow_duskwick_1.js`): the H03 map
   verbatim from [../bible/dungeons.md#h03](../bible/dungeons.md#h03). Dark. Low walls (3-4, 7) and (6-7, 7). Kindle
   lanterns (2, 5), (8, 5), (2, 8): the bridge (5, 3)-(5, 4) rises to the petal (5, 1) (`hollow_duskwick_1:p1`).
   Wave: one Dimling at (8, 8), one near (2, 5). Hint ["Three little lights.", "Kindle the three lanterns; the bridge
   rises to the petal."].
2. **`hollow_duskwick_2` "The Coral Shelf Hollow"**: the H04 map verbatim. Blocks (2, 6) and (8, 6); hold plates
   (3, 8) and (7, 8): block 1 east × 1 then south × 2; block 2 west × 1 then south × 2; the bridge rises **while both
   are held**; reset lever (1, 9). Prize: a 60-gem bag. Hint ["Two blocks, two square plates.", "Push each block one
   step towards the middle, then twice south onto its square plate."].
3. **Registry** (`kind: 'hollow'`, `size: 48`, `parent: 'duskwick'`, frozen time 0.5, 4 views each; music `hollow`);
   the doors placed by S-086a (`duskwick:hollow1` in the Ember Sands dune bank, `duskwick:hollow2` in the h-9
   riser) are now offered (`[E] Enter · Spiral door`); a cleared Hollow lights the small lamp above its
   `kit_hollow_door`. Prints fixtures; realm suite entries; `STATS.duskwick` gains `hollows` and the petal;
   `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`.

#### Assets

None new (dungeon kit, `kit_hollow_door`, the root-wall and Duskwick basalt textures).

#### Acceptance criteria

- Both maps equal dungeons.md (S-049's map test); the solver proves prize and exit reachable (with the Lantern for
  H03) and H04's lever recovers every block state.
- Entering, solving and leaving each Hollow works; `collect.hollow` and the petal or +60 gems are recorded once; the
  door lamp lights; the Completion page's Duskwick Hollows row reads 2/2.
- Invariants: paste the block; keep *Frozen realms* (new prints), *Budgets per view*, *Tone*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

`bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/stats.test.js` and the solver tests; `node tests/inpage.mjs duskwick`
(add: both Hollows end to end); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.

#### Out of scope

Other isles' Hollows.

#### Risk & rollback

One revert hides the doors again.

<a id="s-093b"></a>

### S-093b · feat: Duskwick's Hollows get their second stage, and The Ember-Sand Hollow becomes a Deep Hollow

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-093, S-090b · Size: M (200-500; mostly map data) · Save: none · Flag: the land's Hollow realms keep the land's hidden flag · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h03](../bible/dungeons.md#h03), [../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [../bible/story.md#motes-traces](../bible/story.md#motes-traces)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md §7 and R15, CANON §11.1 A3; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after S-093.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M12 · Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens**. Every Hollow was a one-step room that played in 2-3 minutes and all 24 shared one
shape. Now every Hollow has two stages with the prize behind the second, and one Hollow per land is a three-room
**Deep Hollow** (15-20 min) that ends in a reading nook with a Lamp Log and a note from the Lamp Thief, so the
optional bucket of CANON §11 is real play and each land has one hidden place worth remembering.

#### What
1. **The reliquary** (if S-093b has not added it yet, this step does; later steps reuse it): entity `reliquary: { when:
   [...] }` on a Hollow's prize tile; a code-built cage of four curved roots `#6f5a44` with gold buds `#ffe9a8`, 1.0 m,
   ≤ 120 tris; it opens in 1.0 s with `chime_up` when its condition holds; its state lives in
   `S.dungeons['<hollow>'].stage` until the prize is taken, then is dropped (the shipped Hollow rule). The solver proves
   stage 1, then stage 2, then the prize.
2. **Stage 2 for H01 The Glow-Ring Hollow, H02 The Moonring Hollow, H04 The Coral Shelf Hollow** (copy each Hollow's **Stage 2** line from [dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows)):
   - **H01 The Glow-Ring Hollow** (`hollow_home_1`): push-block `b` (3,2) east ×4 onto hold plate `P` (7,2) (Pip pushes from (2,2)); condition `plate`.
   - **H02 The Moonring Hollow** (`hollow_home_2`): new wall-lanterns `l` (1,1) and (9,1) on the petal side; kindle both (the bridge is live, so the block stays on the plate while Pip is across); condition `lit:[l3,l4]`.
   - **H04 The Coral Shelf Hollow** (`hollow_duskwick_2`): Dimling ×2 rise on rows 1-2 once the bridge is up (hittable only in Lantern light) and a crystal switch `s` (9,1) sits on a raised root (digit 2, a hop); condition `clear && sw`.
3. **The Deep Hollow H03 The Ember-Sand Hollow** (`hollow_duskwick_1`): replace its one-room data with the 31 × 11 composite map
   from [dungeons.md#h03](../bible/dungeons.md#h03) (copy, never paraphrase; realm grid 78 m). Room 1 is the old
   puzzle (stage 1) with a door (10,1) where its prize stood. **Room 2 The Wax Cellar**: (dark) push-block (13,2) east ×4 onto hold plate (17,2); kindle wall-lanterns (11,3) and (19,3); the bridge (15,4)-(15,5) rises while the plate is held and both are lit, and stays once Pip is across; Dimling ×1 (13,7); exit door (20,9). **Room 3 The Reading
   Nook**: the prize on its pedestal (25,1); **Lamp Log 5** on a page pinned to stone (23,3) (`Read · Lamp Log`;
   it moves here from its atlas slot in Duskwick: edit that land's finds data so the slot is skipped, update the
   atlas row and run `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`; land and game totals unchanged); a scrap jam-jar lamp (`prop_scrap_lamp`
   from S-090b, or its fallback) holding a folded note (27,3), `[E] Read · Note`: "Practised here first. Small lights. Jam jars are very forgiving. — M" (text id
   `lore.hollow.duskwick`, ≤ 220 characters; it never names him, the charter spoiler rule; reading it adds the Lore
   entry "A note in a Hollow" and sets `trace:hollow:duskwick`; if story.md's traces define this land's note, its
   text wins); and its own exit `X` (25,10) back to the spiral door.
4. **Hints** (Ask the Tree): one pair per stage-2 line (nudge = the condition's object, plain = the line), and for the
   Deep Hollow's room 2: "Two lights and a weight." / "In the Wax Cellar, push the block onto the square plate and kindle both lanterns; the bridge rises.".
5. `STATS` is regenerated (`node tools/realm-stats.mjs`): same Hollow ids, same prizes, the log's new position.

#### Assets
- `int_reliquary` (code-built as above; ART BRIEF: a small cage of four curved roots with gold buds over a stone
  pedestal; 1.0 m; palette `#6f5a44` `#8a5a3c` `#ffe9a8`; toon bark; 120 / 500 tris; it opens like a flower; image prompt
  "<style line> a small cage of four curved tree roots with tiny gold buds over a mossy stone pedestal, cosy"; 3D prompt
  "stylised low-poly root cage over a stone pedestal, hand-painted, under 500 triangles, 1.0 m"). Fallback: four bent
  cylinders. The Deep Hollow reuses the land's Hollow kit and the scrap lamp; no new images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the edited Hollow blocks (16 `+` and 8 `*` still counted across the Hollows);
  data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves each Hollow's two stages and the Deep Hollow's three rooms in order, with no soft-lock, using only
  the tools listed in the Hollow table.
- In page: a bot solves each stage-2 Hollow and the Deep Hollow; reading the note sets `trace:hollow:duskwick`; the
  Lamp Log counts once; prizes and `collect.hollow` keys are unchanged.
- Content lint (≤ 220 characters, no spoiler name). Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (Hollow realms only; the land's
  overworld prints are untouched except the log slot, refreshed in this PR before the land is unhidden or, if it is
  already unhidden, by a finds-data change that the prints test lists), *One save slot*, *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/stats.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `hollows_duskwick`).

#### Out of scope
- New prizes or Hollows; the land's other finds.

#### Risk & rollback
Data changes inside Hollow realms plus one log slot. Revert the PR: the one-stage Hollows and the log's atlas slot come
back.

### S-094 · chore: close M12: Act II opening documented, brief batch 04

Milestone M12 · Depends on: S-091, S-091b, S-090b, S-092, S-093, S-089, S-001, S-093b · Size: S · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/charter.md#close-out](../bible/charter.md#close-out), [../bible/art-audio.md#brief-template](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-template),
[../bible/art-audio.md#brief-index](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-index)

#### Why

Close M12: the first land beyond home is open, documented and measured, and the owner receives the fourth batch of
model briefs. Roadmap: `docs/roadmap/INDEX.md` S-094 (M12); vision item **Duskwick and chapter 8: Act II opens**.

#### What

The charter's milestone close-out, items 1-10, with:

1. **Suites:** everything in S-087's list plus chapter `ch8` and the chain test.
2. **`docs/DESIGN.md`:** an "Act II" overview (Duskwick first, the three isles in any order, the Tower last; the dim
   state "missing a sound, a rhythm or a glow"; the Lamp Thief beats), Duskwick's land page (regions, landmarks,
   finds by tool), the new species, Kinako's shop.
3. **`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`:** the `moth` and `starkin` rig families, per-realm sky tables, the chapter-8 quest and
   scene files, Kinako's shop data.
4. **`docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-04.md`** (template [../bible/art-audio.md#brief-template](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-template)):
   `npc_wick`, `npc_flick`, `npc_wickling`, `npc_kinako`, `npc_mote`, `prop_duskwick_bazaar_boat`,
   `prop_duskwick_tower`, `prop_duskwick_wick_mast`, `prop_duskwick_stilt_house`, `prop_duskwick_long_house`,
   `prop_duskwick_old_fan`, `duskwick_chimney`, `enemy_sizzle_beetle`, `enemy_coco_clonk`, and the kit
   (`kit_bench`, `kit_beacon`, `kit_ruin_*`, `kit_log_niche`, `kit_hollow_door`, `kit_rootgate`).
5. **Progress entry:** +2.0 h (cumulative 10.0); Duskwick's views with people and enemies; core and bundle sizes;
   save v9 fixture sizes; the coverage numbers.
6. **Release check:** Duskwick unhidden and whole; Pinewhistle, Archwater and Threemills fog on the chart; no act
   card ahead of its content.
7. **Reconciliations:** copy R14, R15, R16, R26, R36, R37, R38 and R41 into the losing bible files.

#### Assets

`docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-04.md` only.

#### Acceptance criteria

Every close-out item ticked with numbers; `bun test tests/docs.test.js` passes; one complete brief per listed key.

#### Verification plan

The full local suite run of the charter; `bun test tests/docs.test.js`.

#### Out of scope

Feature code.

#### Risk & rollback

Docs only; one revert.
